### [1] [Multivariable Extremum Seeking for Locally Lipschitz Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04401)
*Alan Williams*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出对经典多变量扰动-解调极值搜索（ES）架构的极小修改：采用有理无关的扰动频率和匹配的解调信号，结合Kronecker-Weyl定理证明对于任意局部Lipschitz静态目标，固定扰动幅度下长时间平均动力学恰好是核平滑目标的负梯度，并基于一般平均理论（而非周期平均理论）建立了实际全局一致渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极值搜索通常被解释为梯度下降的近似，但在非光滑目标函数及连续时间多变量情形下这一解释并不清晰。作者希望填补这一理论空白，为非光滑多变量ES提供严格的梯度流解释。

Method: 对经典多变量扰动-解调架构做最小改动：使用有理无关的扰动频率与匹配的解调信号。借助Kronecker-Weyl定理，证明在任意固定扰动幅度下，长时间平均动力学恰为核平滑目标的负梯度。由于有理无关频率使信号非周期，采用一般平均理论而非周期平均理论分析；同时推导了扰动占据密度、解调信号与平滑核之间的一般匹配条件，并提供备选设计方案族。

Result: 若平滑目标的梯度流全局一致渐近稳定，则ES动力学实际全局一致渐近稳定。数值实验涵盖非光滑目标（可视为非线性规划的罚函数）以及Rastrigin函数（平滑消除了所有不期望的局部极小值），验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 有理无关频率加匹配解调的极小架构修改，使多变量非光滑ES获得精确的核平滑梯度流解释与稳定保证，扩展了ES理论到非光滑与多变量情形，并提供了更多设计灵活性。

Abstract: Classical extremum seeking (ES) is commonly interpreted as approximating gradient descent, but this interpretation is less clear for nonsmooth objectives in the continuous-time multivariable setting. We propose a minimal modification of the classical multivariable perturbation--demodulation architecture: rationally independent perturbation frequencies and matched demodulation signals. For any locally Lipschitz static objective, the Kronecker--Weyl theorem shows that, at every fixed perturbation amplitude, the long-time averaged dynamics are exactly the negative gradient of a kernel-smoothed objective. Because rationally independent frequencies render the perturbation and demodulation signals nonperiodic, we employ general averaging theory rather than periodic averaging theory. If the gradient flow of the smoothed objective is globally uniformly asymptotically stable, then the ES dynamics are practically globally uniformly asymptotically stable. We also derive a general matching condition relating the perturbation occupation density, demodulation signal, and smoothing kernel, yielding a family of alternative designs. Numerical examples include a nonsmooth objective function, which may be interpreted as the penalty function of a nonlinear program, and the Rastrigin function, for which smoothing eliminates all undesired local minima.

</details>


### [2] [Multi-Principal Competition for an Exclusive Agent: characterization and approximation of Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04426)
*Luis Briceño-Arias,Nicolás Hernández-Santibáñez,Vicente Moreno-Garrido*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个风险中性委托人竞争雇佣单一代理人的主从竞争模型，通过将纳什均衡与个体委托代理契约问题及代理人选择偏差联系起来加以刻画，并提出基于二分法的数值算法以近似求解纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有委托代理竞争模型多关注多代理人场景，而单代理人面对多个委托人竞争的情形下，代理人的保留效用是内生的（取决于竞争报价），这给均衡刻画带来独特挑战，需要系统的理论分析与数值方法。

Method: 将博弈的纳什均衡与个体委托代理问题相联系，分析代理人选择偏差对均衡的影响；提出基于二分法的数值算法，通过定位委托人价值函数的符号变化来近似纳什均衡。

Result: 成功刻画了双委托人竞争单代理人博弈的纳什均衡结构，并通过指数、对数和CRRA效用函数在Renner-Schmedders多项式框架下的多组数值示例验证了所提算法。

Conclusion: 该模型将代理人保留效用内生化的方案为委托人竞争问题提供了新视角，二分法数值算法能有效逼近纳什均衡，为后续更复杂的竞争契约设计研究奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces and studies a Principal-Agent competition model where two risk-neutral Principals compete to hire a single Agent. In this framework, the Agent chooses to work exclusively for one Principal, making the Agent's reservation utility endogenous as it is directly tied to the competing offers. We characterize the Nash equilibria of the resulting game by linking them to the individual Principal-Agent contracting problems and analyzing the Agent's selection bias. Furthermore, we introduce a numerical algorithm based on the bisection method to approximate the Nash equilibria by locating the sign changes of the Principals' value functions. The procedure is illustrated through several examples involving exponential, logarithmic, and constant relative risk aversion (CRRA) utility functions within the polynomial framework of Renner and Schmedders (2015).

</details>


### [3] [Distributed risk-averse optimization via CVaR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04460)
*Siyi Wang,Kun Huang,Lei Xu,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究时变网络上分布式风险厌恶凸优化问题，各智能体通过噪声损失采样的零阶方法最小化局部条件风险价值（CVaR）目标的平均值，证明了渐近共识、有限时间次优界及几乎必然收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统常面临不确定性，最小化期望损失可能忽视罕见但严重的事件。现有研究缺乏在智能体只能获取有噪损失函数评估（而非CVaR目标或其梯度）的分布式场景下求解风险厌恶优化问题的方法，因此需要开发零阶分布式算法。

Method: 提出一种零阶算法：各智能体通过采样损失构建经验CVaR估计及其梯度估计；每次迭代中，智能体结合邻居决策并执行局部更新。在凸性和Lipschitz连续假设下，使用衰减步长和固定样本量进行分析，包括加权遍历迭代的有限时间期望次优界及局部最后迭代的几乎必然收敛性。

Result: 证明了智能体达到精确渐近共识；建立有限时间期望次优界；局部最后迭代以概率1收敛到公共最优点；限制性期望CVaR间隙由平滑误差和有限样本误差界定；分布式界与文中提供的集中式基准在参数依赖上相匹配。传感器网络估计问题的仿真验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该零阶分布式算法可在只依赖噪声损失采样的条件下有效解决平均CVaR分布式优化问题，其性能界与集中式基准匹配，为实际分布式风险敏感系统提供了理论保障的求解方案。

Abstract: Distributed systems often operate under uncertainty, where minimizing expected loss may overlook rare but severe events. This paper studies a distributed risk-averse convex optimization problem in which agents cooperatively minimize the average of local conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) objectives over a time-varying network. Each agent has access only to noisy evaluations of its local loss function, rather than to its CVaR objective or gradient. We therefore develop a zeroth-order algorithm that uses sampled losses to construct empirical CVaR estimates and their gradient estimates. At each iteration, agents combine neighboring decisions and perform a local update. Under convexity and Lipschitz continuity assumptions, we prove that the agents reach exact asymptotic consensus. We also establish a finite-time expected suboptimality bound for the weighted ergodic iterate. With diminishing step sizes and fixed sample sizes, the local last iterates converge almost surely to a common optimum, and their limiting expected CVaR gap is bounded in terms of the smoothing and finite-sample errors. This distributed bound matches the parameter dependence of the centralized benchmark provided in this paper. Finally, simulations on a distributed sensor network estimation problem illustrate the efficacy of the method.

</details>


### [4] [A bipolar fuzzy relation equation framework for clinical decision support systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04470)
*Amin Ghodousian,Mohammad Sedigh Chopannavaz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双极模糊关系优化的临床决策支持框架，通过正负模糊关系矩阵联合表示有利与不利的临床关系，采用最小t-范数提供非补偿性的瓶颈式交互解释，将可行推荐集表示为有限个临床推荐区域的并集，并通过区域级优化与全局选择算法获得全局最优推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持中常涉及异质测量、专家评估和基于指南的知识，这些信息与临床决策之间的关系通常是渐进的而非纯二元的，需要一种能够同时刻画有利与不利临床关系的统一数学框架。

Method: 构建双极模糊关系优化框架，通过正负模糊关系矩阵与模糊需求向量联合建模；采用最小t-范数实现非补偿性的瓶颈型交互解释；推导临床可接受区间与有效证据激活集进行可行性分析与系统约简；将完整可行集表示为有限个与可接受临床证据赋值函数对应的临床推荐区域的并集，并对每个区域构造最优候选后全局比较。

Result: 开发了集成可行性分析、系统约简、区域级优化与全局选择的算法，并通过数值CDSS算例验证，结果表明该框架能将模糊临床关系信息转化为可行且优化的决策支持推荐。

Conclusion: 双极模糊关系优化为将模糊临床关系信息转化为可行且优化的决策支持推荐提供了一种结构化、数学严格且有效的机制。

Abstract: Clinical decision support frequently involves heterogeneous measurements, expert assessments, and guideline-based knowledge whose relationships to clinical decisions are gradual rather than purely binary. This paper develops a clinical decision-support framework based on bipolar fuzzy relational optimization, in which favorable and unfavorable clinical relationships are represented jointly through positive and negative fuzzy relational matrices together with a fuzzy requirement vector. The simultaneous participation of each decision variable and its complement in the relational constraints enables bipolar clinical information to be incorporated within a unified optimization model. The minimum \(t\)-norm is adopted to provide a noncompensatory, bottleneck-type interpretation of the interaction between fuzzy relational grades and decision levels. We characterize the feasible recommendation set by deriving clinical admissibility intervals and effective evidence activation sets, which provide the basis for feasibility analysis and systematic reduction of the relational system. The complete feasible set is then represented as a finite union of clinical recommendation regions associated with admissible Clinical Evidence-Assignment Functions. A region-wise optimal candidate is constructed for each admissible evidence assignment, and comparison of the resulting finite collection of candidates yields a globally optimal recommendation. An algorithm integrating feasibility analysis, system reduction, region-wise optimization, and global selection is developed and illustrated through a numerical CDSS example. The results show that bipolar fuzzy relational optimization provides a structured and mathematically rigorous mechanism for transforming fuzzy clinical relational information into feasible and optimized decision-support recommendations.

</details>


### [5] [Centered Permutation Prefixes for SGD with Random Reshuffling: Sharp Rates, Hölder Geometry, and Composite Proximal Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04578)
*Jiaxiang Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机重整洗牌（random reshuffling）下的随机梯度下降法（SGD）用于有限和问题。对于常数分量步长的fresh reshuffling，在平均函数为强凸且Hessian Lipschitz连续的条件下，证明了最后周期的收敛率为Õ(T^{-2}+n^2T^{-3})，与已知的二次下界在(n,K)依赖上匹配。还分析了ProxRR（带近端算子）情形，对复合最小化问题给出包含分裂项β_*²/K²的完整收敛率，并在ν≥1/2时证明该分裂项不可避免并给出匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重整洗牌（SGD-RR）在实际中广泛使用且通常优于带放回的SGD，但其理论收敛率研究一直不够完备。此前结果在收敛率的高阶项（如n²T^{-3}）与已知二次下界之间存在差距，且对Hessian连续性的假设或额外有界迭代假设要求较强。作者希望在不依赖组件级Hessian连续性或单独的有界迭代假设下，获得匹配下界的收敛率，并扩展到ProxRR（近端随机重整洗牌）情形。

Method: 作者在以下假设下进行理论分析：(1) 每个f_i具有L-Lipschitz梯度；(2) 平均函数F是μ-强凸且Hessian Lipschitz连续（更一般地，ν-Hölder连续）。对于SGD-RR，使用常数分量步长并分析最后周期（last-epoch）的期望次优性；对ProxRR则逐周期分析到复合最优解x^†的距离。通过精细的误差分解和矩分析，将收敛率分解为与周期数K和总样本数T=nK相关的项，同时利用Hölder连续性参数ν刻画对Hessian光滑度的依赖。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 对SGD-RR，得到last-epoch率Õ(T^{-2}+n²T^{-3})，与已知二次下界匹配；当ν≥1/2时（Hessian至少1/2-Hölder连续）该二次率得以保持，一般ν贡献额外项Õ(n^{1+ν}T^{-2-2ν})。(2) 对凸分量情形，递减步长结果去除了大周期数要求，在nK超过条件数尺度时恢复相同的两项尺度。(3) 对ProxRR，证明期望误差上界为Õ(β_*²/K² + T^{-2} + n²T^{-3} + n^{1+ν}T^{-2-2ν})，其中β_*²/K²项源于分裂（splitting）效应；并在ν≥1/2的常数步长机制下证明该分裂项不可消除，给出匹配对数因子的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了SGD-RR理论中的重要空白：在更宽松的假设（无组件级Hessian连续、无需单独有界迭代假设）下获得了与下界匹配的收敛率，并统一了Hessian光滑度参数ν对收敛率的贡献。对ProxRR的误差分解及其分裂项的下界刻画，揭示了常数步长机制下近端随机重整洗牌算法的内在极限，为该类算法的理解和进一步改进提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study stochastic gradient descent with random reshuffling for finite sums \[ F(x)=\frac1n\sum_{i=1}^n f_i(x). \] For fresh reshuffling with a constant component stepsize, if each $f_i$ has an $L$-Lipschitz gradient and the average $F$ is $μ$-strongly convex with a Lipschitz-continuous Hessian, we prove the last-epoch rate \[ \mathbb E[F(y_K)-F(x_\star)] =\widetilde O\!\left(T^{-2}+n^2T^{-3}\right), \qquad T=nK, \] matching the known quadratic lower bound in its $(n,K)$-dependence. The components may be nonconvex, and no componentwise Hessian continuity or separate bounded-iterate assumption is required. More generally, a $ν$-Hölder-continuous average Hessian adds only $\widetilde O(n^{1+ν}T^{-2-2ν})$, so every $ν\ge 1/2$ preserves the quadratic rate. Under convex components, a decreasing-stepsize result removes the large-epoch requirement and recovers the same two-term scale once $nK$ exceeds the condition-number scale.
  We also analyze epoch-wise ProxRR for $\mathcal P=F+ψ$. Writing $x^\dagger$ for the composite minimizer and $β_\star=\|\nabla F(x^\dagger)\|$, we prove \[ \mathbb E\|y_K-x^\dagger\|^2 =\widetilde O\!\left( \frac{β_\star^2}{K^2} +T^{-2}+n^2T^{-3} +n^{1+ν}T^{-2-2ν} \right). \] For $ν\ge 1/2$, we show that the $β_\star^2/K^2$ splitting term is unavoidable and obtain a matching lower bound up to logarithms in the stated constant-stepsize regime.

</details>


### [6] [Stochastic Maximum Principle for McKean-Vlasov Control with Discrete Path Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04588)
*Yadh Hafsi,Samy Mekkaoui,Huyên Pham*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类具有离散路径依赖性的McKean-Vlasov控制问题，系数和代价泛函依赖有限个固定观测时刻的状态历史值及其联合分布。建立了受控状态方程的适定性，并通过随机Pontryagin极大值原理推导了必要且充分的最优性条件。伴随过程由带固定观测时刻跳的倒向随机微分方程刻画，每个跳代表未来成本对相应观测状态及其分布的敏感性。在LQ情形下，通过延拓论证与均值场Riccati约化证明前向-后向系统的全局可解性，并讨论了在时间序列生成中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的许多控制问题（如金融、经济、数据生成等）不仅依赖当前状态，还依赖过去有限时刻的状态历史及其分布。传统的McKean-Vlasov控制框架通常只考虑连续路径依赖或当前状态，难以捕捉离散观测时刻的历史信息与分布信息的联合影响，因此需要建立适用于离散路径依赖情形的理论框架。

Method: 建立了离散路径依赖的McKean-Vlasov控制问题的一般框架；通过随机Pontryagin极大值原理推导最优性的必要与充分条件；引入带固定观测时刻跳跃的倒向随机微分方程刻画伴随过程；在LQ情形下，采用延拓论证与均值场Riccati约化证明前向-后向随机微分方程系统的全局可解性。

Result: 证明了受控状态方程的适定性；给出了最优控制满足的Pontryagin型条件，并明确每个跳跃项的分布敏感性解释；在LQ框架下取得了FBSDE系统的全局可解性结果；提供了基于核函数的路径分布偏差终端代价下时间序列生成的数值应用。

Conclusion: 该工作将McKean-Vlasov控制理论推广至离散路径依赖情形，为处理含历史观测信息与分布信息的最优控制问题提供了一套完备的理论工具，并在时间序列生成的分布度量优化中展示了其实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study a class of McKean-Vlasov control problems with discrete path dependence. The coefficients and cost functional may depend on finitely many past values of the controlled state, observed at fixed deterministic times, and on their joint law. We establish well-posedness of the controlled state equation and derive necessary and sufficient optimality conditions through a stochastic Pontryagin maximum principle. The adjoint process is characterized by a backward stochastic differential equation with jumps at fixed observation times. Each jump represents the conditional sensitivity of future costs with respect to the corresponding observed state and its distribution. In the linear-quadratic case, we prove global solvability of the resulting forward-backward system by combining a continuation argument with a mean-field Riccati reduction. We finally discuss an application to time-series generation, where the terminal cost is given by a kernel-based discrepancy between the law of the sampled path and a target path distribution.

</details>


### [7] [Blind Random Search with Noisy Loss Measurements: Averaging, Thresholding, and Almost Sure Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04636)
*Zixian Zhou,Xintong Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对盲随机搜索算法在含噪测量环境下失去单调性问题，通过引入平均化与阈值化两种经典工具并将其耦合（阈值随样本量增长以匹配速率收缩），恢复了算法在真实损失上的最终单调性并保证几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲随机搜索在无噪声环境下能保证真实损失严格递减并单调非增收敛；但测量噪声会使较差的候选点显得更优，从而破坏单调性并可能导致不收敛。需要一种机制在噪声环境下恢复算法的几乎必然收敛保证。

Method: 将平均化和阈值化两种经典技术引入原决策准则：对候选点进行多次采样的平均化处理以降低噪声影响，同时设置正阈值并要求改进量超过该阈值才接受更新；两种机制相互耦合，随着样本量的增大，正阈值以匹配的速率同步缩小。

Result: 经过上述修改，盲随机搜索能够在含噪测量下恢复真实损失的最终单调性（即最终阶段真实损失单调非增），并且算法以概率1收敛（几乎必然收敛）。

Conclusion: 通过耦合平均化与阈值化策略，盲随机搜索可以扩展到含噪场景，即使测量存在噪声也能保持最终单调性并实现几乎必然收敛，使该经典方法的理论保证在更实际的噪声环境下依然成立。

Abstract: Blind random search repeatedly draws a candidate point and replaces the current estimate whenever the candidate has a lower loss. In the absence of noise, the true loss is observed directly. It decreases strictly at every accepted update and is monotone nonincreasing over all iterations. Measurement noise can make a worse candidate appear better and thereby break this monotonicity. To recover almost sure convergence under noise, we incorporate averaging and thresholding into the original decision criterion. These two classical tools are coupled. As the sample sizes grow, the positive threshold shrinks at a matched rate. These modifications allow blind random search to recover eventual monotonicity of the true loss under noisy measurements and to converge almost surely.

</details>

Abstract: Transaction costs can make or break a trading strategy, particularly in relative-value trading of commodity and macro markets, where edges are a few basis points. This paper models both effects with a convex quadratic cost. In each period, a positive semidefinite matrix built from volatility, volume, and correlation forecasts couples trades across contracts. A power-law decay kernel then couples trades across periods. The resulting cost admits no price manipulation even when liquidity varies over the planning horizon. The model is demonstrated empirically on calendar spread trading of crude oil futures around the commodity index roll.

Abstract: In this work, we study policy optimization under domain randomization for linear quadratic control, focusing on learning a single state-feedback controller that minimizes the average cost across systems with uncertain dynamics. Abstract: Minimum-weight satisfiability asks for a satisfying Boolean assignment of minimum cost, while many fuzzy decision and knowledge systems operate with graded positive and negative relations rather than crisp literals. This paper introduces a bipolar fuzzy minimum-weight satisfiability problem in which positive and negative clause--variable relations, target clause levels, and variable states are represented in the unit interval. The clause requirements are expressed by bipolar fuzzy relational equalities defined with an arbitrary continuous t-norm. Classical minimum-weight CNF satisfiability is recovered as a crisp special case, independently of the selected t-norm, and an optimal Boolean solution is shown to exist in that specialization even though the proposed formulation uses continuous variables. For the graded problem, clause satisfaction is characterized through admissible variable domains and effective clause-witness sets. This structure leads to an exact clause-witness branch-and-bound method with preprocessing, propagation, lower-point closure, and a disjoint-witness packing bound. Computational experiments verify the solver against explicit witness enumeration, mixed-integer baselines, and external SATLIB formulas; on a focused graded ablation set, the packing bound reduces the median number of explored nodes by about 45 percent relative to the single-row witness bound.

Abstract: In this letter, we study finite-horizon linear-quadratic Stackelberg difference games with coupled-affine state-control inequality constraints. Eliminating the dynamic variables yields a large-scale linear complementarity quadratic program, together with an explicit recovery map for the follower strategy. This reformulation enables the numerical computation of these equilibria. We illustrate the proposed approach using a constrained network-flow game.

</details>

Abstract: This paper studies the relationship between the controllability of a quasi-linear parabolic equation and that of its linear counterpart. Unlike standard controllability problems, the controls constructed here not only steer the system to a prescribed target at a given terminal time, but are also designed to ensure that the deviation between the nonlinear and linear dynamics obeys the aforementioned estimate. This method is applicable to general nonlinear partial differential equations, and the approximation order for controllability is optimal.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种非局部正则化的最优输运框架，弥合了 Kantorovich 与 Monge 两种经典表述之间的鸿沟。通过对输运计划的振荡施加带奇异核的罚项，在足够强的奇异性条件下，作者证明了有限能量输运计划必然由映射诱导，从而实现正则化 Kantorovich 与 Monge 问题的等价。论文建立了分数阶核下的 Sobolev 正则性、一阶最优性条件、消失正则化极限下的 Γ-收敛结果，并提出了对偶形式与不动点数值求解方案。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: (1) 引入非局部正则化项，通过对输运计划作用在近对角线上具有奇异性的交互核来惩罚振荡；(2) 证明当核奇异性足够强时，有限能量输运计划均可由映射表示，建立两种问题的等价性；(3) 对分数阶核，将正则子具体化为分数阶 Sobolev 半范数，允许跳跃间断以覆盖广泛的源/目标测度；(4) 推导一阶最优性条件；(5) 分析消失正则化极限，证明正则化泛函 Γ-收敛到经典 Kantorovich 问题；(6) 将问题提升到输运计划的乘积空间，利用协正（copositive）函数锥给出对偶公式，并提出不动点迭代数值方案。

Result: 证明了正则化 Kantorovich 与 Monge 问题的等价性；建立了 Γ-收敛与一阶 Γ-极限的结果，指出极限选择具有有限能量且非局部能量最小的 Monge 极小元；当不存在此类极小元时识别了新的渐近机制，并在典型的质量分裂示例中给出了精确的标度律；对偶公式基于协正函数锥，数值实验通过不动点方案验证了正则化效果。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了一套统一的正则化最优输运理论：正则化既能恢复 Monge 映射的存在性，又通过 Γ-收敛保持与 Kantorovich 问题的联系。这一框架为理论分析与数值计算之间架起桥梁，为在缺乏传统解存在性条件的测度类上求解 Monge 问题提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We introduce a nonlocal regularization of optimal transport that bridges Kantorovich and Monge formulations. The regularization penalizes oscillations through an interaction kernel singular near the diagonal. We show that, for sufficiently strong singularities, every finite-energy transport plan is induced by a map, yielding equivalence between the regularized Kantorovich and Monge problems. For fractional kernels, the regularizer reduces to a fractional Sobolev seminorm that can allow jump discontinuities, leading to existence results for broad classes of source and target measures. We derive first-order optimality conditions and analyze the vanishing-regularization limit. The regularized functionals $Γ$-converge to the classical Kantorovich problem, while the first-order $Γ$-limit selects, whenever they exist, finite-energy Monge minimizers with minimal nonlocal energy. When no such minimizer exists, we identify a different asymptotic regime and establish a sharp scaling law in a prototypical mass-splitting example. Finally, after lifting the problem to the product of transport plans, we derive a dual formulation using the cone of copositive functions and propose a fixed-point numerical scheme illustrating the regularization.

</details>

The proof combines a perturbed fixed-point argument in the compact-bounded case with truncation, uniform moment estimates, compactness, and stability of controlled martingale problems.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的运筹学（OR）问题描述往往不完整——缺失目标、约束或业务规则会改变最终的数学模型。现有评估大多假设问题描述完整，忽略了代理是否知道在建模前需要澄清这一关键能力，因此缺乏对"建模前澄清"环节的系统评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to formulate optimization models from natural-language problem descriptions, yet realistic operations research (OR) requests are often incomplete: missing objectives, constraints, or business rules can change the resulting mathematical program. Existing evaluations largely assume a complete specification and therefore overlook whether an agent knows when clarification is needed before modeling. We introduce OR-Clarify, a benchmark for pre-formulation clarification. Each task presents a partial public problem description, withholds structured hidden slots, and evaluates agents through bounded interaction with a simulated user. The benchmark supports both openended and choice-based clarification, and measures slot recovery, stopping behavior, silent assumptions, and interaction cost. We further propose Interactive Optimization (InterOPT), a two-stage framework that identifies unresolved formulation-critical gaps and uses them to guide whether to ask the next question or to stop. In our choice-based experiments, InterOPT substantially outperforms all baselines in exact slot recovery; in the open-ended setting, it remains competitive with strong prior methods. Together, OR-Clarify and InterOPT reframe OR assistance as a selective completeness decision: clarify when needed, stop when ready, and quantify what remains missing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős相似性猜想关注哪些集合可以嵌入到正测度或大测度集合中。此前关于Rajchman测度支撑集的研究缺乏统一的构造方法。作者希望能证明Rajchman测度支撑集总能以均匀大测度形式"避免"包含给定集合的仿射拷贝，且不依赖Fourier衰减的定量速率。

Result: 得到两个主要结果：(1) 对任意ε∈(0,1)及Rajchman测度支撑集A，存在闭的1-周期无处稠密极完备集合E，其单位区间测度下界为1-ε，且不含A的仿射拷贝；(2) 对任意Hausdorff gauge h，存在h-零紧Rajchman支撑K，其上对数型上盒维数与对数型填充维数均为1，且该值对两个维数均是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文为Erdős相似性猜想提供了一个强的大集形式解答：每个支撑Rajchman测度的集合都可被"均匀大测度"的闭无处稠密集避免。构造完全不依赖Fourier衰减的定量速率，仅依靠等分布与多尺度阻断器思想，展示了该方法在调和分析与分形几何交叉领域的普适性。

Abstract: Let $A\subseteq\mathbb{R}$ support a probability measure whose Fourier--Stieltjes transform tends to zero at infinity. We prove that, for every $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, there is a closed, $1$-periodic, nowhere dense set $E\subseteq\mathbb{R}$ such that \[ m(E\cap I)\ge1-\varepsilon \] for every interval $I$ of length $1$, while $E$ contains no affine copy of $A$. Thus every set supporting a Rajchman measure satisfies the Erdős similarity conjecture in a uniform large-set form. The proof combines equidistribution modulo one for large dilates of the measure with a multiscale family of low-density periodic blockers; no quantitative rate of Fourier decay is used. We also refine a classical theorem of Ivašev-Musatov, showing that for every Hausdorff gauge $h$ there is an $h$-null compact Rajchman support $K$ satisfying \[ \overline{\dim}_{\mathrm B}^{\log} K=\dim_{\mathrm P}^{\log} K=1. \] The value $1$ is sharp for both dimensions.

</details>

The scalings of these expansions, capturing the intensity of the perturbation, are measured by new quantities called ``Robin-Dirichlet'' capacity or a ``Robin-Neumann'' capacity, which depend on the geometry of $ω_\varepsilon$ and on the value of the parameter $k_\varepsilon$. The confinement on the phase field is guaranteed already for the approximation, a priori estimates follow without modification of Lyapunov functionals, and the approximations are weakly differentiable in time --- even twice in the case of linear mobility. Abstract: Data-Driven Computational Mechanics (DDCM) replaces traditional phenomenological constitutive models by directly reformulating boundary-value problems in terms of local material state data obtained from experiments or fine-scale simulations. This breakdown occurs particularly when input material data sets are subject to noise or local physical non-admissibility. We prove the $Γ$-convergence of the penalized functionals to the exact constrained problem and establish a fully discrete convergence framework incorporating spatial finite-element discretization and empirical data-set approximations. Abstract: Forsythe's conjecture, published in 1968, asserts that for each restart length $s$, every exact-arithmetic restarted conjugate-gradient iteration on a real symmetric positive definite problem either terminates or has normalised residuals that converge separately along the even and odd restart subsequences. Apart from the classical steepest-descent case, this asymptotic question remained unresolved in full generality for nearly six decades. We give a complete classification by restart length in the original finite-dimensional setting and identify a sharp threshold. For $s=2$ and $s=3$, every problem either terminates or has convergent even and odd residual directions. For every $s\ge4$, there is a diagonal positive definite counterexample of dimension $s+4$ which never terminates and whose even residual directions do not converge. Together with Akaike's theorem for $s=1$, this shows that the conjectured universal conclusion is true precisely for $s\in\{1,2,3\}$ and false for every $s\ge4$. The positive results follow from a degree-independent double-orthogonality identity and an analysis of the low-degree fixed-point sets. At restart length four, rational interval arithmetic and Sturm sequences certify a transverse Hopf point of the leading vector field of the rescaled squared-weight map. Analytic periodic-orbit and shadowing arguments yield the counterexample at restart length four, and degree elevation extends the construction to every larger restart length.

</details>

Abstract: Operator splitting provides an attractive approach for the numerical integration of (coupled) port-Hamiltonian systems, as it allows the underlying system structure to be exploited at the level of the individual subproblems. However, the choice of the decomposition is not unique and may strongly affect both the computational efficiency and the preservation of the energy behavior of the original system. In this work, we investigate this interplay systematically and introduce energy consistency as a criterion for assessing splitting methods for port-Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations. We derive sufficient conditions under which a splitting based on a given decomposition inherits the energy behavior of the continuous system and use these conditions to analyze several decomposition strategies for coupled port-Hamiltonian systems. In particular, we compare decompositions that preserve the structure with approaches that exploit lower-dimensional subsystem dynamics or separated time scales. The analysis is complemented by numerical experiments using Strang splitting and its multiple-time-stepping extension. A scalable electro-thermal benchmark with fast electrical and slow thermal dynamics is employed to assess accuracy, energy behavior, and computational efficiency. The results demonstrate that preserving the port-Hamiltonian structure of the subflows is essential for energy-consistent splitting, whereas decompositions that exploit subsystem structure or time-scale separation can provide substantial computational advantages. In particular, the time-scale decomposition yields significant efficiency gains for systems with pronounced multirate characteristics, while structure-destroying decompositions may lead to undesirable energy behavior.

Abstract: We present a unifying framework to construct local Fortin operators for conforming mixed finite element pairs for the Stokes equations. These include applications to non-Newtonian fluid flow, problems with inhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, as well as uniform inf-sup stability relevant for domain approximation and moving domains.

Abstract: We analyze the numerical approximation of time-harmonic scattering by highly heterogeneous penetrable obstacles. Abstract: We develop an energy-optimal generalized scalar auxiliary variable (EOP-GSAV) framework for nonlinear index-one port-Hamiltonian differential-algebraic equations (pH-DAEs). Exploiting the port-Hamiltonian structure, we separate the nonlinear effort, interconnection, and dissipation terms from a constant implicit core. The resulting BDF-1 and BDF-2 schemes require one linear solve per time step with a reusable factorization, while retaining discrete passivity and accurate tracking of the Hamiltonian. The schemes are compared with the implicit midpoint method equipped with full, modified, and frozen-Jacobian Newton iterations. Numerical experiments ranging from a strongly state-dependent nonlinear stress test to large-scale benchmarks demonstrate robust and competitive performance, with substantial efficiency gains in matched-accuracy regimes. A comparison with SUNDIALS IDA shows comparable work-precision behavior at equal order despite a non-specialized Python/SciPy implementation, while unrestricted variable-order adaptive IDA is faster in the high-accuracy regime.

Abstract: We investigate the best $L_2$ approximation of mixed Sobolev spaces by shallow neural networks with $n$ neurons and general activation functions. We first establish an activation-independent Fourier-block principle: if an activation has univariate approximation order $ρ$ in the sense of the Fourier-block property, then the global approximation rate has algebraic order $\min\{α,ρ\}$ for target functions of mixed smoothness $α$, up to explicit logarithmic factors. To verify this property for concrete activations, we introduce a structured univariate approximation condition that implies the Fourier-block property with explicit parameters. For $\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, a matching algebraic lower bound identifies $\min\{α,k+1\}$ as the optimal algebraic approximation exponent in any dimension, up to logarithmic factors in the upper bound. The framework also yields the exponent $\min\{α,k+1\}$ for cardinal B-splines and soft-$\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, and the full mixed-smoothness exponent $α$ for ELU and cosine activations, again up to logarithmic~factors.

</details>

Method: 提出一种神经预条件子，训练其近似离散拉普拉斯算子的逆；核心是采用空间变化卷积核的轻量级神经网络架构，支持快速推理并能泛化到训练分布之外的形状、边界条件和网格尺寸。

Abstract: We introduce a neural-preconditioned iterative solver for Poisson equations with mixed boundary conditions. Typical Poisson discretizations yield large, ill-conditioned linear systems. Iterative solvers can be effective for these problems, but only when equipped with powerful preconditioners. Unfortunately, effective preconditioners like multigrid require costly setup phases that must be re-executed every time domain shapes or boundary conditions change, forming a severe bottleneck for problems with evolving boundaries. In contrast, we present a neural preconditioner trained to efficiently approximate the inverse of the discrete Laplacian in the presence of such changes. Our approach generalizes to domain shapes, boundary conditions, and grid sizes outside the training set. The key to our preconditioner's success is a novel, lightweight neural network architecture featuring spatially varying convolution kernels and supporting fast inference. We demonstrate that our solver outperforms state-of-the-art methods like algebraic multigrid as well as recently proposed neural preconditioners on challenging test cases arising from incompressible fluid simulations.

</details>

### [57] [Minimax Lower Bound for Estimating Diffusion-based Local Intrinsic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04822)

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于扩散（diffusion）方法的局部本征维度（LID）估计量的统计学性质。在正则流形模型下，证明FLIPD所定义的有限尺度场与流形真实维度d的偏差至多为O(σ²)，并建立了该估计量的极小极大下界(nσ^d)^{-1}，揭示了扩散方法估计LID的统计难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于扩散的方法Recently已成为探测高维数据内在几何的有效工具，但其统计难度在很大程度上仍未得到探索。本文旨在从理论层面填补这一空白，严格刻画扩散型局部本征维度量的估计极限。

Method: 作者将高斯平滑后的密度对数尺度导数作为FLIPD的有限尺度总体泛函进行研究。直觉上，高斯平滑将局部维度转化为尺度律：在d维流形附近，核质量按σ^d增长，因此对噪声尺度求导可揭示本征指数。在正则流形模型假设下，作者证明了该有限尺度场与流形维度的一致性（偏差O(σ²)），并通过极小极大下界构造论证了估计的统计下界(nσ^d)^{-1}。

Result: 在模型类上一致地证明了有限尺度场与流形维度d的偏差至多为O(σ²)；建立了估计该场的极小极大下界(nσ^d)^{-1}，其中σ满足n^{-1/(2α+d)}≲σ≤σ₀；在最小可覆盖尺度处，下界退化得非参数率n^{-2α/(2α+d)}。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统刻画了扩散型LID估计量的统计极限，揭示了有限样本下估计固有几何量的本质困难，为扩散方法在高维几何探测中的理论基础提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: While diffusion-based methods have recently emerged as effective tools for probing the intrinsic geometry of high-dimensional data, their statistical difficulty remains largely unexplored. We study estimation of the finite-scale population functional underlying FLIPD (Kamkari et al., 2024; arXiv:2406.03537), a diffusion-based local intrinsic dimension (LID) quantity defined through the logarithmic scale derivative of a Gaussian-smoothed density. Intuitively, Gaussian smoothing turns local dimension into a scale law: near a $d$-dimensional manifold, the kernel mass grows like $σ^d$, so differentiating with respect to the noise scale reveals the intrinsic exponent. Under a regular manifold model, we show uniformly over the model class that the finite-scale field differs from the manifold dimension $d$ by at most $O(σ^2)$. We then establish a minimax lower bound of order $(nσ^d)^{-1}$ for estimating this finite-scale field from $n$ observations, for $n^{-1/(2α+d)}\lesssimσ\leσ_0$. At the smallest scale covered by our lower-bound construction, the bound becomes the nonparametric rate $n^{-2α/(2α+d)}$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中自监督学习会将同一数据点产生的多个相关增强样本池化在一起学习不变子空间V，但现有理论工作通常假设增强样本相互独立，仅能处理较小的独立数据子集。理论与实际之间存在明显差距，缺乏对池化方法统计性质的理论刻画。

Method: 作者通过理论分析比较两种估计不变子空间V的策略：（1）池化策略——将同一数据点的所有增强样本（包括依赖关系）合并训练；（2）划分基线——将数据划分为独立子集分别训练。作者推导了两种策略下V的统计估计误差界，考察了增强间相关性对估计的影响，并针对浅层神经网络上的掩码/噪声注入增强场景进行了具体分析。

Result: 证明了池化方法的估计误差界在所有情况下都不劣于划分基线；在掩码或噪声注入等增强场景下，朴素池化在增强数量维度上能达到更快的收敛速率；当增强之间的相关性对估计影响温和或有助于降低方差时，池化的优势尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该分析为自监督预训练中池化增强样本的有效性提供了理论依据，解释了实践中偏好使用大量增强的直觉，弥合了理论与实际应用之间的差距。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning relies on so-called data augmentations $φ(x)$ of unlabeled datapoints $x$ --- for example, masking random pixels in an image $x$ --- that should leave the label of $x$ invariant and are often used to learn a lower-complexity invariant subspace $\cal V$ for downstream tasks. In practice, such augmentations $\{ φ_l(x_i) \}$ are pooled together to learn $\cal V$, despite obvious inter-dependencies between different augmentations $φ_l(x), φ_k(x)$ of the same datapoint $x$. However, theoretical works on the subject typically consider procedures that avoid such dependencies, and are therefore limited to operate on smaller subsets of independent data.
  We show in this work that pooling augmentations together, despite inter-dependencies, is a better alternative than the baseline of partitioning the data into subsets of independent data. More precisely, in the context of estimating $\cal V$, the statistical estimation error bounds for pooling are never worse than the partitioning baseline, and in some cases --- such as masking or noise injection-based augmentations over a shallow neural network --- naive pooling leads to faster rates in terms of the number of augmentations. The benefits of pooling are particularly prominent when the correlations between different augmentations $φ_l(x), φ_k(x)$ have mild effects on estimation or help decrease the estimation variance. The analysis, therefore, yields new insights into the success of pooling augmented samples in self-supervised pre-training, and provides an intuition behind the practical preference towards using many augmentations.

</details>

Abstract: Dynamical symbolic regression methods identify governing differential equations from noisy data, balancing interpretability and predictive accuracy. However, standard methods often produce expressions that violate known physical laws. To address this, we propose FluxDisco, a physics-informed framework tailored for flux-based, stoichiometric ODE systems. By leveraging a known stoichiometry, we reduce the expression search space and ensure physical adherence. Our framework adapts the Monte Carlo Graph Search algorithm for the unique challenges associated with joint flux discovery of stoichiometric systems. We evaluate our method across a range of physical and biological systems, demonstrating its ability to accurately recover governing dynamics through interpretable equations.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文填补了时间序列潜在变量模型泛化理论分析的空缺，为马尔可夫潜在结构提供了可验证的PAC-Bayesian保证，且其假设条件在文献中具有普遍性而非过度限制性。

Abstract: Forecasting time series accurately is critical for applications with complex data ranging from energy systems to healthcare and finance. Among current state of the art models, generative latent variable models are increasingly implemented; yet principled generalisation guarantees for modern latent variable models remain limited. In particular, while Variational AutoEncoders are widely used for sequential data, their theoretical analysis is largely restricted to i.i.d. settings. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for latent variables models applied to time series. Building on reconstruction-based bounds, we extend PAC-Bayesian guarantees to Markovian latent structures, capturing temporal dependencies through a sequential generative process. These guarantees do not grow with the length of the trajectory. Our bounds depend on assumptions which are common in the literature; we provide an example framework where they would be verified to show that they are not as restrictive as they may seem.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不稳定的周期轨道和稳态被认为是时空混沌与湍流结构的"骨架"，但在热驱动流动中，尤其是过渡性（transitional）区域，其计算仍十分稀缺。作者希望通过在近混沌转变的热对流系统中计算并分析这些轨道，理解混沌的起源以及系统如何通过轨道间的跳跃实现复杂动力学。

Method: 在Pr=1的二维瑞利-贝纳德系统中，数值计算一个稳态和三族周期轨道；利用Floquet分析研究轨道稳定性并刻画其分岔；分析流场对轨道的shadowing行为，确定各轨道在不同参数区域中的动力学相关性；同时监测对称性破缺与热输运行为。

Result: 发现系统在混沌之路中，经历准周期化后会在多组轨道集合之间跳跃；通过Floquet分析揭示了主频和次频的出现及相位锁定机制与轨道动力学的关联；两种重要发现为：(1)所有对称性在混沌onset之前已被打破，(2)混沌的onset并未改变热输运行为。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在近转变的热驱动流动中系统地刻画了周期轨道及其分岔结构，证明了不稳定周期轨道确实是该系统混沌动力学的骨架。对称性在混沌onset前完全破缺以及热输运不受混沌onset影响的发现，对理解湍流中轨道骨架假设的普适性与极限具有重要启示。

Abstract: Unstable periodic orbits and steady states are believed to form the backbone of spatiotemporal chaos and turbulence, yet their computation in thermally driven flows remains scarce for transitional regimes. In this work we compute and characterize a steady state and three families of periodic orbits in two-dimensional Rayleigh-Bénard at $\mathrm{Pr}=1$, near the transition to chaos. We find that in its route to chaos, the flow hops between several sets of orbits after becoming quasiperiodic. We use Floquet analysis to study the stability of the orbits obtained and characterize their bifurcations, showing how the appearance of primary and secondary frequencies, as well as phase-locking mechanisms, are all related to the dynamics of the orbits. We study in detail how the flow shadows the orbits found and determine in which regimes each orbit is dynamically relevant or not. Our analysis also reveals two important insights: (1) all symmetries are broken before the onset of chaos, and (2) this onset does not alter the behavior of the heat transport.

</details>

Abstract: What controls heat transport when liquid water interacts with ice? Answering this question is essential for understanding water bodies undergoing phase change. Yet experimental progress remains limited by the lack of a minimally invasive methodology for simultaneously resolving temperature fields in coupled liquid water and non-isothermal ice systems. Here, we introduce a physics-based data-assimilation method for reconstructing temperature fields in buoyancy-driven flows interacting with non-isothermal ice. Particle tracking velocimetry provides the liquid velocity field, while thermal and kinematic boundary conditions constrain the inverse problem. The method couples advection--diffusion in liquid water with conduction in ice to reconstruct simultaneous mean temperature fields and quantify heat transport across the water--ice interface, while remaining minimally invasive and compatible with free-surface systems. We demonstrate the method in laboratory experiments in which the temperature range across the liquid water in contact with ice drives cabbeling-induced convection. This framework enables investigation of coupled thermo-fluid dynamics in cryospheric aquatic systems, including heat exchange at the ice-water interface and liquid-phase energetics, with broader applications to phase-change processes in food and energy industries.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 三个配置中存在共同模体，表明存在稳健的近壁动力学。经典SSP相关模体被重现之外，还发现了同样显著但不直接对应SSP的模体，说明近壁主导动力学超越经典再循环周期。条件分析显示：外层能量静止态时动力学与低雷诺数全通道相似；外层能量活跃态则促进高能爆发事件。外层活动会改变近壁动力学校准路径，但不足以消除这些路径。

Abstract: We identify dynamical processes in the near-wall region of turbulent wall-bounded flow by representing coherent-structure energy as trajectories in a low-dimensional state space and analyzing recurring trajectory patterns using network motifs. Direct numerical simulations (DNS) are performed for three configurations: a minimal flow unit (MFU) at $Re_τ\approx 180$ to isolate the self-sustaining process (SSP), a full-scale channel at the same Reynolds number to study interactions between near-wall structures, and an MFU at $Re_τ\approx 2200$ to investigate near-wall/outer-layer coupling. Proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) is used to identify modes corresponding to streaks, rolls, and meandering structures of the SSP, and the flow is projected onto these modes to track their energy over time. Motif analysis then identifies statistically significant dynamical pathways in the resulting state-space trajectories. Several motifs are common to all three configurations, indicating robust near-wall dynamics across different background flows. While motifs associated with the classical SSP are recovered, equally prominent motifs with no direct SSP analogue are also identified, demonstrating that preferred near-wall dynamics extend beyond the canonical regeneration cycle. Conditioning the $Re_τ\approx 180$ MFU on outer-layer energy further shows that quiescent outer-layer states produce dynamics resembling those of the low-Reynolds-number full channel, whereas energetic outer-layer states promote high-energy bursting events. These results demonstrate that near-wall turbulence evolves along preferred dynamical pathways whose prevalence is modified, but not eliminated, by outer-layer activity.

</details>

Abstract: A numerical simulation of an annular rotating detonation engine with stoichiometric hydrogen-oxygen is performed. A generic, well posed, and easily implemented approach using a quasi-two-dimensional method to model the area variations through the rotating detonation engine's injector and combustor is presented. The detonation--injector interaction is studied for the case with a ratio of four between the combustor and injector's throat areas. A shock wave is formed in the divergent portion of the injector due the high back pressure created by the detonation in the combustor. A Favre-averaged steady-state analysis of stream lines and particle paths reveals that the shock causes an irrecoverable loss of stagnation pressure. Stagnation pressure gain in the combustor is insufficient to make up for the loss and the flow leaves the engine with lower stagnation pressure than in the plenum.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性升力面的双向气动弹性（FSI）建模是航空航天领域的重要挑战，传统方法依赖昂贵的体积网格生成与结构/流体网格匹配问题。作者旨在开发一个能够准确预测耦合气动弹性响应、支持变保真度气动模型，且计算成本显著低于传统网格方法（如CFD）的模块化求解框架。

Method: 气动侧采用rVPM（无网格涡粒子方法）求解不可压缩NS方程，通过FLOWUnsteady框架支持可变保真度气动建模；结构侧使用Reissner-Mindlin板理论建立有限元模型，以广义-α方法进行时间积分，并通过Gauss-Newton程序在FEniCS框架内求解非线性平衡方程。流固耦合采用显式交错分区方法，通过公共中间界面结合功守恒的"力传递"与"逆几何传递"算子处理非匹配界面上的载荷与位移交换，以适配气动载荷和几何的多代表性本质。最终通过水洞实验验证，并进行了灵敏度分析与参数研究。

Result: 框架在水洞实验的对比中展示了准确的耦合气动弹性响应预测能力（而非仅独立验证各个求解器）。无网格气动求解器使计算成本大幅降低，同时保持精度。通过覆盖不同流动条件、结构特性和耦合参数的灵敏度分析与参数研究，进一步评估了求解器的鲁棒性与适用范围。

Conclusion: VarFlExI有效整合了无网格气动求解器与FEniCS有限元结构求解器，提供了一种可模块化、计算高效且准确的柔性升力面FSI求解方案。其在验证中证明了耦合系统层面的预测能力，适用于广泛的流动与结构参数范围，为高保真度气动弹性分析提供了替代昂贵CFD方法的可行途径。

Abstract: We present $\texttt{VarFlExI}$ (Variable Fidelity Unsteady Flow-FEniCS Exchange Interface), a modular aeroelastic solver for modeling two-way fluid-structure interaction (FSI) of flexible lifting surfaces. The framework employs a reformulated Vortex Particle Method (rVPM) to solve the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations without the need for computationally expensive volumetric meshing, while supporting variable-fidelity aerodynamic modeling using the $\texttt{FLOWUnsteady}$ framework. On the structural side, a Reissner-Mindlin plate formulation is discretized using the finite element method and integrated in time through the generalized-$α$ method, with the nonlinear equilibrium equations solved using a Gauss-Newton procedure within the $\texttt{FEniCS}$ framework. Fluid and structural solvers are coupled through an explicit staggered partitioned scheme, ensuring conservation of virtual work for load and displacement transfer across the non-matching interface. To accommodate the multi-representative nature of the aerodynamic loads and geometry, the interface coupling employs separate work-conservative force and reverse-geometry transfer operators via a common intermediate interface. The framework is validated against water-tunnel experiments, demonstrating accurate prediction of the coupled aeroelastic response rather than independent validation of the constituent solvers. The computational efficiency of the meshless aerodynamic solver enables simulations at significantly lower computational cost while maintaining accuracy. The solver is further evaluated through sensitivity analyses and parameter studies spanning different flow conditions, structural properties, and coupling parameters.

</details>

Abstract: The complex flow behavior of viscoelastic fluids emerges from a feedback between fluid stress and the out-of-equilibrium conformational dynamics of their constituent polymer chains. While constitutive models can predict flow and stress fields in simple geometries, a fundamental understanding of the mechanisms regulating viscoelastic flows with mixed kinematics and non-trivial polymer dynamics remains elusive. Quantifying the history-dependent conformation of polymer chains in flow is essential to resolve these complex systems. To address this knowledge gap, we employ direct Lagrangian tracking of individual DNA molecules to reveal their conformational dynamics in microfluidic viscoelastic flows for different polymer contour lengths and concentrations. Comparing the measured molecular extension and orientation reveals discrepancies with canonical constitutive models, which do not fully capture the transient dynamics. Our measurements show a distinct anisotropy in polymer relaxation and shape, which couple the polymer's rotation and extension through hydrodynamic drag, regulating their Lagrangian mechanics. These findings emphasize the importance of Lagrangian polymer history and the need to account for non-trivial intra-molecular mechanics to improve constitutive descriptions of viscoelastic flows.

</details>


### [67] [Transport fidelity and domain of validity of compact Gaussian kinetic representations for rarefied flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04606)
*Ehsan Roohi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种针对离散速度法（DVM）动力学状态的局域高斯（localized-Gaussian）紧凑表示策略，并将其应用于单原子正常激波和顶盖驱动腔流两个基准问题。结果表明，该方法在守恒量上误差低于1%，输运和高阶矩误差约1-2%，显著优于同等系数预算下的多线性网格方法。在空腔问题中，高斯映射将最大误差降低约7-24倍；在Mach条件化测试中，保持对应关系的局域基将保留Mach-6分布误差从42.86%降至11.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀薄流的紧凑表示必须保留非平衡输运信息，同时识别其有效范围。作者旨在解决如何以高效存储的方式表示非平衡动力学状态（如激波和空腔流动中的DVM状态），使其既能保持输运保真度，又能明确界定其参数适用范围的问题。

Method: 该策略针对两类运动学状态进行专门化处理：(1) 对于激波分布，采用正相空间混合（positive phase-space mixture）表示，通过求积法再生矩层级；(2) 对于空腔流动的20个场量，采用共享支撑的物理空间图（shared-support physical-space map）。方法核心包括：局域支撑、连续求值、输运保真度和系数计数核算。在Mach条件化测试中，使用保持对应关系的局域基（correspondence-preserving local basis），并以归一化坐标作为防护机制拒绝训练范围外的Mach数。

Result: 在分别拟合的Mach-3和Mach-5激波中，守恒量误差低于1%，输运和高阶矩误差约1-2%。在同为4608系数的预算下，多线性网格的相应非平衡量误差达89-98%。在空腔测试中，高斯映射优于多线性和奇异值分解基线，最大误差降低约7-24倍。Mach条件化测试中，保持对应关系的局域基将保留Mach-6分布误差从42.86±5.40%降至11.45±0.94%，而输运误差保持在30-40%；归一化坐标防护能拒绝训练范围外的Mach 12。独立网格研究确认这些趋势不受DVM离散化误差主导。

Abstract: Compact representations of rarefied flows must retain nonequilibrium transport information while identifying their range of validity. The strategy is specialized to the available kinetic state: a positive phase-space mixture represents shock distributions and regenerates their moment hierarchy by quadrature, whereas a shared-support physical-space map represents 20 cavity fields. Localized support, continuous evaluation, transport fidelity, and coefficient-count accounting therefore provide the common structure across the two benchmarks. For separately fitted Mach-3 and Mach-5 shocks, the method gives sub-percent errors in conserved quantities and approximately 1--2\% errors in transport and higher-order moments. At the same 4608-coefficient budget, the tested multilinear grids produce 89--98\% errors in these nonequilibrium quantities. For both cavity cases, the Gaussian map also outperforms matched bilinear and singular-value-decomposition baselines, reducing maximum errors by factors of approximately 7--24. In the Mach-conditioned tests, a correspondence-preserving local basis reduces the withheld Mach-6 distribution error from $42.86\pm5.40\%$ to $11.45\pm0.94\%$ at fixed storage, while its transport errors remain 30--40\%; a normalized-coordinate guard rejects Mach 12 outside the training range. Independent grid studies confirm that these trends are not dominated by DVM discretization error. The results establish localized Gaussian representations as storage-efficient transport-fidelity maps for fitted kinetic states and provide quantitative acceptance criteria for parametric use.

Abstract: Elastic turbulence (ET) in straight geometries has been extensively studied in idealised configurations such as periodic shear and planar channel flow, yet experiments are typically restricted to confined systems like ducts and pipes. While chaotic elastic fluctuations are observed in ducts, their onset in pipes is debated due to geometric constraints. Here we report sustained chaotic dynamics in both geometries and show they correspond to the same ET state identified in idealised setups. Conclusion: 稳态孤立波解的分支结构在进入超临界流后会呈现出复杂的螺旋与闭环拓扑，且地形衰减率会显著影响该结构的形态。同宿剥离现象揭示了孤立波在变振幅过程中动态发射的机制。

Abstract: Recently, an infinite set of steady solitary-wave solution branches was discovered for inviscid and irrotational flow over a localised topographic depression. These solutions were restricted to critical flow, when the Froude number is unity. Several questions were raised, including i) what happens to the solution structure as the flow speed is continued into the supercritical regime? and ii) how does the decay rate of the topography affect the solution structure? In this article, we answer these questions by examining exact steady solutions of the fKdV model and numerical solutions of the fully nonlinear Euler system over i) trench, ii) Gaussian and iii) generalised Agnesi topographies. We uncover a highly non-trivial bifurcation structure consisting of spirals and closed loops, characterised by the phenomenon of `homoclinic shedding', where pairs of solitary waves are emitted up- and downstream as the amplitude is varied.

Conclusion: 揭示了一种传统范式未能识别的预测性组织框架，为控制湍流流动以减少燃油消耗提供了新途径。

Abstract: The three-dimensional organization of coherent structures in the turbulent flow over aircraft wings remains elusive, limiting our ability to reduce fuel consumption. Here, we use explainable artificial intelligence to characterize these structures by predicted flow evolution rather than classical predefined kinematic criteria. After training a deep neural network to predict the short-term evolution of the flow, we identify the highest-relevance flow regions using Shapley-value attribution methods. We have found that as the flow decelerates towards the trailing edge, predictive importance shifts from near-wall low-speed structures to three-dimensional pairs of high- and low-speed fluid regions. Matching no single classical structure family, these pairs progressively dominate the dynamically relevant flow. Most stand spanwise side-by-side, enclosing a near-vertical momentum interface with the strongest velocity jump. Their geometry remains invariant in viscous units while their volume expands when approaching separation. Our results reveal a predictive organization that traditional paradigms overlook, opening new ways to control turbulent flows.

</details>

Method: 从连续性方程出发推导Fisher信息产生率的四项分解；扩展到Fokker-Planck方程分析扩散耗散；证明涡量无关定理；推导各变形项的几何符号判据；利用Burgers激波层精确解揭示点态恒等式；通过二维湍流模拟验证剪切-耗散平衡；研究Wasserstein梯度流变分稳态下的分解平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析非平衡系统中的排序动力学提供了具有诊断和预测能力的工具，实现了对排序生成或破坏方向的定量判定。

Abstract: We establish a kinematic framework for phase space ordering based on the Fisher information production rate. From the continuity equation we derive a four-term decomposition into isotropic contraction, traceless shear, divergence gradient, and boundary flux. Extending to the Fokker-Planck equation, we prove the strict non-positivity of the diffusive dissipation term. We prove a vorticity-independence theorem: purely rotational velocity fields do not alter the order measure. We derive explicit geometric sign criteria for each deformation term, enabling prediction of order generation or destruction. Through exact solutions of the Burgers shock layer, we uncover a pointwise identity between the compression and divergence-gradient contributions, underlying the three-term balance. Two-dimensional turbulence simulations verify the shear-dissipation balance. We further show that variational steady states of the associated Wasserstein gradient flow imply decomposition balance. This framework provides a diagnostic and predictive tool for analyzing ordering dynamics in non-equilibrium systems.

</details>

### [72] [Fukui-Kawakami chains: spectrum and hidden $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$-symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04378)
*Rob Klabbers,Antoine Lefebvre*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Fukui和Kawakami通过引入扭曲边界条件得到的三角函数Haldane-Shastry（HS）自旋链变形，称为Fukui-Kawakami（FK）链。作者利用与未扭曲HS链的直接联系来分析其谱，解释了为何部分谱可用扭曲Bethe方程描述并可由Yangian最高权态构造，这些态由"motifs"标记。同时发现存在其他不能用Bethe方程描述的descendants，其能量可表示为两个单粒子能量之和，暗示额外隐藏对称性。特别地，作者证明反周期边界条件情形与Basu-Mallick-Finkel-González-López最近提出的"最小极化"长程模型重合，揭示该反周期链具有隐藏的gl(1|1)超对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fukui和Kawakami早期引入了HS自旋链的扭曲边界条件变形，但该模型的完整谱结构尚未被充分理解。本文旨在通过建立与未扭曲HS链的直接联系，深入分析FK链的谱在扭曲参数下的依赖关系，解释扭曲Bethe方程的适用范围及局限性，并揭示反周期边界条件情形下隐藏的超对称结构。

Method: 作者建立FK链与未扭曲HS链之间的直接映射关系，利用这一联系分析谱结构。通过Yangian最高权态和motifs组合标记对可解部分进行系统分类；对于不可由Bethe方程描述的descendants，通过能量表达式分析其结构。在反周期情形下，证明FK链与Basu-Mallick-Finkel-González-López的"最小极化"长程模型等价，并利用该等价性建立gl(1|1)超对称性，将HS motifs映射到其超对称gl(1|1)对应物。

Result: 揭示了FK链谱中部分态可由Fukui-Kawakami扭曲Bethe方程描述且由Yangian最高权态构成，用motifs标记。发现存在其他descendants不遵循这些Bethe方程，其能量表现为两个单粒子能量之和，提示额外隐藏对称性。反周期链被证明与"最小极化"长程模型重合，从而揭示其隐藏的gl(1|1)超对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对扭曲HS链谱结构的理解，明确了扭曲Bethe方程的适用范围和局限，并通过反周期情形的等价性揭示了FK链与超对称长程模型的深层联系，暗示存在更丰富的隐藏对称代数结构，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Fukui and Kawakami showed that the trigonometric Haldane--Shastry (HS) spin chain can be deformed by introducing twisted boundary conditions. In this work we revisit the resulting twisted HS chains, which we call Fukui--Kawakami (FK) chains.
  We analyse their spectra in dependence of the twist parameter, utilising a direct connection with the (untwisted) HS chain. This allows us to explain why part of the spectrum can be described by Fukui and Kawakami's twisted Bethe equations, and can be constructed from Yangian highest weight states. These states can be labelled by combinatorial data called `motifs', as in the HS chain, which cover some of the (deformed) descendants. We furthermore show that there are other descendants which do not follow from these Bethe equations, but whose energy can be described as a sum of two single-particle energies, suggesting additional hidden symmetries.
  We then focus on the special case of antiperiodic boundary conditions, and show that this chain coincides with the `minimally polarised' long-range model recently introduced by Basu-Mallick, Finkel, and González-López. Remarkably, this connection implies that the antiperiodic chain has a hidden $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$-symmetry, which we use to relate the HS motifs to their `supersymmetric' $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$ counterparts.

</details>


### [73] [Breakdown of the Overdamped Approximation in Fluctuating Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04668)
*Kazuki Fukutani,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在摩擦系数随时间波动的复杂环境中，过阻尼近似对布朗运动的有效性。通过对比在相同波动摩擦和同一温度下满足涨落-耗散关系的欠阻尼与过阻尼朗之万动力学，发现当环境波动快于速度弛豫时，环境平均与惯性消去通常导致不同的长期输运行为。快速波动摩擦下，欠阻尼动力学由算术平均摩擦主导（D_eff^under=k_BT/⟨γ⟩），而过阻尼动力学给出调和平均（D_eff^over=k_BT⟨γ^{-1}⟩）。对于两态马尔可夫摩擦，作者精确推导了有限时间有效扩散系数，揭示了由速度弛豫与环境切换竞争控制的两个调控区间之间的完整交叉，并建立了通用平稳摩擦过程的快速波动结果，在连续log-OU环境中验证并独立确定特征交叉时间。结果揭示了环境平均与惯性消去之间的不可交换性，为时间异质环境中过阻尼动力学的有效性建立了依赖于时间尺度的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过阻尼近似广泛用于描述复杂环境中的布朗运动，但当摩擦系数本身随时间波动时，该近似的有效性不再平凡。理解环境涨落与惯性消去之间的关系对于正确建模时间异质环境中的输运至关重要，尤其是当摩擦波动快速时，标准的过阻尼描述可能失效。

Method: 作者对欠阻尼与过阻尼朗之万动力学进行解析对比，两者经受相同的波动摩擦并满足共同温度下的涨落-耗散关系。对于两态马尔可夫摩擦过程精确求解有限时间有效扩散系数，从而刻画完整交叉行为；同时针对一般平稳摩擦过程推导快速波动极限结果，并在连续log-Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（log-OU）摩擦环境中验证。

Result: 发现欠阻尼动力学的有效扩散系数由算术平均摩擦决定（D_eff^under=k_BT/⟨γ⟩），而过阻尼动力学则由调和平均摩擦决定（D_eff^over=k_BT⟨γ^{-1}⟩），且两者仅在平均摩擦倒数无波动时一致。两态Markov情形下建立了由速度弛豫时间与环境切换速率竞争控制的完整交叉。快速波动环境中，普遍结果是环境平均与惯性消去不可交换，且快速环境波动反而放大了惯性的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了环境平均与惯性消去在时间异质摩擦环境中的非交换性，建立了依据时间尺度判断过阻尼近似适用性的准则。快速环境波动并不抑制而是增强惯性效应，这对复杂介质中扩散建模和有效动力学的推导具有重要意义。

Abstract: The overdamped approximation is widely used to describe Brownian motion in complex environments, but its validity becomes nontrivial when the friction coefficient itself fluctuates in time. We investigate this problem by comparing underdamped and overdamped Langevin dynamics subject to the same fluctuating friction and satisfying the fluctuation--dissipation relation at a common temperature. We show that environmental averaging and inertial elimination generally lead to different long-time transport when environmental fluctuations are fast compared with velocity relaxation. For rapidly fluctuating friction, the underdamped dynamics is governed by the arithmetic mean friction and yields $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm under}=k_{\rm B}T/\langleγ\rangle$, whereas the overdamped dynamics gives $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm over}=k_{\rm B}T\langleγ^{-1}\rangle$. For a two-state Markov friction, we derive the finite-time effective diffusion coefficient exactly and identify the full crossover between these two regimes, controlled by the competition between velocity relaxation and environmental switching. We further establish the fast-fluctuation result for general stationary friction processes and verify it for a continuous log-Ornstein--Uhlenbeck environment, for which the characteristic crossover time can also be determined independently. Our results reveal a noncommutativity between environmental averaging and inertial elimination, establish a timescale-dependent criterion for the validity of overdamped dynamics in temporally heterogeneous environments, and show that rapid environmental fluctuations can enhance, rather than suppress, the consequences of inertia.

</details>


### [74] [Scaling laws and energy dissipation in the dynamics of SLE curve as a 1D turbulence model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04719)

Abstract: In this study, we demonstrate the characteristics of the stochastic Loewner evolution (SLE) as a one-dimensional (1D) turbulence model. Further mathematical analysis showed that this condition is seen as the condition on Loewner entropy $S_{\mathrm{Loew}}$, which includes the white Gaussian noise term $W_{s}$ in the Loewner driving function of the present model. In addition, the numerical simulations were performed to verify the scaling relations of this 1D turbulence model. These results suggest that the standard SLE becomes a beneficial model of 1D turbulence by introducing an appropriate time coordinate change and imposing the conditions on $κ$ and $S_{\mathrm{Loew}}$.

Abstract: Mean-field theory is a widely used approximation for describing phase transitions, particularly effective above the upper critical dimension. Its origins can be traced back to the van der Waals theory of the liquid-gas transition and Weiss's molecular field theory of the paramagnetic-ferromagnetic transition. However, it was Lev D. Landau who provided a unifying and general framework applicable to a broad class of physical systems. The mean-field approach typically involves neglecting thermal fluctuations, which is a reasonable assumption in many classical contexts. However, its application to quantum phase transitions at zero temperature is less common. The aim of this short pedagogical paper is to explore and clarify the use of the mean-field approach in the less familiar domain of quantum phase transitions. We specifically consider the Ising model and the Blume-Capel model.

Result: 得到T和L的边际分布、联合Laplace变换及完整混合矩层级；证明pE[T]+qE[L]=1以及pT+qL服从单位率指数分布（累积风险定律）。三维球体中p、q对φ和(T,L)统计量的调控作用得到显式刻画，Monte Carlo模拟验证了累积风险定律的普适性。

Abstract: We investigate reflected diffusion in a bounded domain subject to two independent, competing stopping mechanisms: an exponentially distributed bulk lifetime of rate $p$ and a surface reaction triggered when the boundary local time exceeds an independent exponential threshold of rate $q$. Denoting by $T$ the stopping time and by $L$ the acquired boundary local time at stopping, we derive their marginal distributions, joint Laplace transform, and complete hierarchy of mixed moments. These statistics are determined by the splitting probability $φ$ that surface reaction occurs before bulk decay. In particular, we establish the identity $p\expect{T}+q\expect{L}=1$ and show that the cumulative risk $pT+qL$ is exponentially distributed with unit rate. We further obtain equivalent representations of $φ$ in terms of the Robin-Laplacian and the generalized Steklov spectra. Explicit results for a three-dimensional ball reveal how competing rates $p,q$ control $φ$ and the $(T,L)$ statistics. Monte Carlo simulations test the universal cumulative-risk law.

</details>

Abstract: We discuss the possibility to train a thermodynamic system, whose micro-variables are fully determined through the Hamiltonian by the usual statistical mechanical rules, to perform tasks such as memorization and generalization. Training is achieved by the application of suitable external fields, playing the role of {\it data}. At variance with conventional machine learning, no other logical or algorithmic rules are introduced. The system is amenable, in principle, to exact analytical calculations. We specialize this general approach to a prototypical Ising system with annealed dichotomous couplings and study its learning ability. Results indicate excellent memorization and good generalization capacity already for small systems, and a tendency to improve with system size.

Abstract: Clocks' performance, especially their precision, is fundamentally limited by thermodynamic laws as they are physical devices. Yet, it is known that the established thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR), which imposes an upper bound on the uncertainty product of the precision of oscillations and entropy production, is violated for underdamped systems such as stochastic pendulum clocks. Here, we show that for a general class of stochastic pendulum clocks described as a weakly nonlinear oscillator the uncertainty product of the phase of a pendulum clock and entropy production takes a universal and simple form that depends solely on the degree of nonlinearity. Its validity and limitations are examined using several representative models of pendulum clocks. Our framework reveals a universal thermodynamic principle governing stochastic pendulum clocks beyond the conventional TUR and provides a foundation for designing optimal pendulum clocks that operate efficiently in stochastic environments.

</details>

Abstract: We suggest a model of a diffusive process inside a fractal sponge structure, which is a generalization of the diffusion processes on a comb and fractal mesh structure. It is shown that the corresponding one-dimensional diffusion process is governed by a generalized Fokker-Planck equation with a power-law memory kernel and a position-dependent diffusion coefficient. That is, the fractal structure of the medium induces memory effects and heterogeneity in the transport system. The considered model may be of interest to describe anomalous heat transport in porous fractal media.

</details>

Abstract: We apply tensor-network methods to a field-coupled XY model for long-only portfolio construction. Daily return statistics set asset-specific fields and correlation couplings. Correlation distance, four-point Gromov hyperbolicity, and Ward clustering are used to construct a sparse interaction path. A Fourier-Bessel expansion maps the continuous angular partition function to a finite-current tensor network; bottom-up and top-down contractions then give the one-site marginals and equilibrium cosine scores. A softmax map converts these scores into positive, fully invested portfolio weights. We study five equity markets over continuous ranges of inverse temperature beta and concentration gamma, and compare selected parameter pairs with long-only Markowitz frontiers and standard benchmarks. The same-sample comparisons demonstrate a tractable route from financial time series to network-adjusted allocations; predictive trading performance is outside their scope.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 建立了qubit Yang–Baxter门算符Schmidt秩的一致时间上界（常数界）及对应算符纠缠的常数上界；对非退化Yang–Baxter映射置换门及其相位修饰分别给出常数与对数纠缠上界；对合双幺正Yang–Baxter门给出多项式Schmidt秩增长（对数纠缠上界）。反例表明七态非双幺正对合Yang–Baxter门存在指数增长的精确算符Schmidt秩。一般Yang–Baxter门的纠缠增长规律仍未解决。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统刻画了Yang–Baxter门的算符纠缠边界，表明辫子关系约束能显著抑制算符复杂度增长（多数情形为常数或对数纠缠），但在非双幺正对合等情形下可突破至指数Schmidt秩。结果深化了对可积幺正门量子复杂度的理解，同时将一般Yang–Baxter情形下的纠缠增长问题留给未来。

Abstract: We study the operator entanglement of local operators in one-dimensional brickwork circuits whose two-site gate satisfies the braid relation; throughout this work, we call such a gate a Yang--Baxter gate. We establish upper bounds for several structured, overlapping classes of Yang--Baxter gates. We show that the operator Schmidt rank remains uniformly bounded in time for all qubit Yang--Baxter gates and, in arbitrary local dimension, for permutation gates obtained from non-degenerate Yang--Baxter maps. We also show that it grows at most polynomially for involutive dual-unitary Yang--Baxter gates and for arbitrary phase dressings of permutation gates obtained from non-degenerate Yang--Baxter maps. These results imply, respectively, constant and logarithmic upper bounds on the operator entanglement. Conversely, we construct a seven-state involutive Yang--Baxter gate without dual unitarity and a one-site operator whose exact operator Schmidt rank grows exponentially, although the corresponding operator entropies remain undetermined. Entanglement growth in the general Yang--Baxter case remains open. All proofs and selected examples were constructed by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol.

</details>

*Gustave Robichon,Cecile Monthus,Werner Krauth*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维路径图及其"提升图"（每个位点复制）上的可逆马尔可夫链如何被扰动并逐渐驱动为非可逆马尔可夫链，使用Green's矩阵形式计算Kemeny时间和平均首达时间，以分析提升带来的加速效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可逆马尔可夫链如何通过扰动转变为非可逆链，以及这种转变对特征值谱、混合性能和特征时间的影响，特别是在提升图结构上的加速效果。

Method: 从可逆马尔可夫链（可对角化、实特征值与特征向量、左右特征向量构成双正交系统）出发，对一维路径图及每位置点复制的提升路径图进行分析。通过具体实例讨论非可逆链转移矩阵的可对角化/不可对角化情形、实特征值与复共轭特征值对。针对多种稳态（平坦、方波、楔形、V形）计算两张图上的特征值谱，并发展Green's矩阵形式以计算Kemeny时间和平均首达时间。

Result: 在多种稳态配置下计算了两类图的特征值谱，确定了提升可实现的速度提升程度，并通过Green's矩阵形式获得了Kemeny时间和平均首达时间的解析/数值结果，从而解释了特征时间的物理含义。

Conclusion: Green's矩阵形式为分析提升非可逆马尔可夫链的特征时间提供了有效框架，能够解释提升在不同稳态下带来的加速效果及非可逆性与可对角化性之间的关系。

Abstract: For a one-dimensional path graph and a lifted path graph constructed from a duplication of each of its sites, we study how a reversible Markov chain can be perturbed and gradually driven into non-reversibility. The reversible Markov chain has a transition matrix that is diagonalizable and features real-valued eigenvalues and eigenvectors. The left and right eigenvectors form a biorthogonal system. We discuss in concrete examples how the transition matrix of a non-reversible Markov chain may be diagonalizable or non-diagonalizable, and it may have real eigenvalues and complex-conjugate pairs. For a number of steady states (flat, square-wave, wedge, V-shape), we compute eigenvalue spectra on both graphs and discuss the speedup that can be achieved through lifting. We develop a Green's matrix formalism, which we use to compute Kemeny times and mean first-passage times, and which provides valuable information and allows us to interpret the results for the characteristic times.

</details>


### [83] [Non-reciprocally interacting Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes: Exceptional points, Anomalous relaxation, Pseudo-equilibrium and Boundary refrigeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05391)
*Soumya Kanti Pal,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入了一类由非互易参数g调控的非互易Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（NROU）模型体系，涵盖二聚体、无序系统和多体链（精确映射到Hatano-Nelson模型）。在g=g*处，漂移矩阵不可对角化，实现不同阶数的例外点（EP）。研究发现无序模型中EP位置分布出现普适边缘奇异性且非自平均；EP处指数衰减被多项式前因子修饰，其阶数由EP阶数决定；完全不对称极限下多体链表现出"伪平衡"态，稳态分布可分解为平衡类单粒子测度。利用Harada-Sasa关系获得总稳态热耗散的闭式解，并揭示了边界制冷效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用广泛存在于活性物质、生物系统和无序系统中，普遍驱动体系偏离平衡。作者旨在建立一个统一的、可解析处理的模型体系，系统研究非互易性如何通过例外点改变系统的弛豫动力学、稳态性质与热力学行为，从而深化对非互易非平衡物理的理解。

Method: 作者构建了由非互易参数g控制的NROU模型层级，涵盖：(1)非互易耦合二聚体；(2)其无序对应物；(3)可精确映射到Hatano-Nelson模型的多体链。通过调控非互易性使漂移矩阵非对角化以产生不同阶数的例外点，并利用自相关/协方差函数、稳态分布分析以及Harada-Sasa关系等方法系统研究例外点附近的动力学与热力学性质。

Result: 核心结果包括：(1)无序模型中，EP位置g*的分布在不同无序实现间发展出恰好位于干净系统EP处的普适边缘奇异性，且表现为非自平均；(2)在EP处，指数弛豫被多项式时间前因子修饰，多项式阶数由EP阶数决定并编码链的空间结构；(3)完全不对称时，多体链呈现"伪平衡"态——稳态分布因子化为平衡类单粒子测度但存在非零稳态电流，且N粒子系统分解为N/2个独立复OU过程；(4)由Harada-Sasa关系得到稳态总热耗散的闭式解，揭示边界制冷效应：随非互易性调谐，边界粒子可在热源与冷源角色间切换。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个从简单两体到多体无序系统的统一NROU理论框架，展示了非互易性通过例外点对非平衡动力学和热力学产生的深刻且普适的影响，为理解活性及生物系统中非互易相互作用驱动的非平衡现象提供了新的解析工具和理论洞见。

Abstract: Non-reciprocal interactions are ubiquitous in active, biological, and disordered systems, generically driving them out of equilibrium. Here, we introduce a hierarchy of non-reciprocally interacting Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (NROU) models governed by a tunable non-reciprocity parameter $g$. At a special point $g=g^*$, the drift matrix becomes non-diagonalizable, realizing exceptional points (EP's) of different orders, where eigenvalues and eigenvectors simultaneously coalesce. The hierarchy encompasses non-reciprocally coupled dimers, their disordered counterparts, and a many-body chain exactly mapping onto the paradigmatic Hatano-Nelson model in the arena of non-Hermitian quantum systems. For the disordered model, we show that the distribution of the EP location $g^*$ across disorder realizations develops a universal edge singularity precisely at the clean-system EP, and is manifestly non-self-averaging. Across all models, we find that at the EP, the usual exponential relaxation of the autocorrelation and covariance functions is dressed by a polynomial-in-time prefactor whose degree is set by the order of the EP and whose detailed structure encodes the spatial architecture of the chain. At complete asymmetry, the many-body chain exhibits ``pseudo-equilibrium'': its steady-state distribution factorizes into equilibrium-like single-particle measures despite a nonzero steady-state current. Moreover, the $N$-particle interacting system decomposes into $N/2$ independent complex OU processes. Finally, using the Harada-Sasa relation, we obtain a closed-form expression for the total steady-state heat dissipation and uncover a boundary refrigeration effect, in which the boundary particles switch from acting as a hot to a cold reservoir as the non-reciprocity is tuned.

</details>

### [84] [Reduced Precision Diffusion Synthetic Acceleration for S$_N$ Neutron Transport in LLNL's ARDRA using Hypre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04451)
*Joanna Piper Morgan,Mario Ivan Ortega*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在双精度输运迭代中使用单精度（32位）扩散合成加速（DSA）求解的算法可行性。通过在独立代码和LLNL的ARDRA输运代码中实现降精度DSA，并与hypre库耦合进行混合精度线性求解，研究发现大多数情况下降精度DSA对源迭代的加速特性几乎没有影响；但当需要更紧的收敛容差时，降精度DSA可能无法成功加速输运求解，反而增加求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少输运计算的时间和内存成本。利用降精度预条件处理的优势，在不显著降低高精度线性系统收敛行为的前提下，通过降低DSA求解精度来减少每步迭代的时间和内存开销，从而加快整体求解速度。

Method: 首先在平板几何中建立一个独立代码来研究降精度DSA的理论限制；然后在LLNL的离散纵标中性粒子输运代码ARDRA中实现降精度DSA，并与支持混合精度线性求解的hypre可扩展线性求解器和多重网格方法库耦合；最后求解各种中子输运问题以评估性能。

Result: 在大多数情况下，降精度DSA与源迭代结合使用时，对DSA的加速特性几乎没有影响；然而当要求更紧的容差时，降精度DSA可能无法成功加速输运求解，导致求解时间增加。

Conclusion: 降精度DSA在多数输运问题中是一种可行的加速策略，可以在不明显损失收敛性能的情况下降低求解代价；但在高精度要求场景下需谨慎使用，因为可能反而带来性能退化。

Abstract: Fast solution times in production discrete ordinates transport codes often require the use of diffusion synthetic acceleration (DSA). A good implementation of DSA will substantially reduce the number of iterations to converge the transport solution at the cost of solving a diffusion-like linear system at each iteration. Often fewer transport iterations will lead to smaller times to solution. Recent work has shown the promise of reduced precision preconditioning. Time and memory cost can be decreased as long as the reduced precision does not substantially degrade the convergence behavior of the higher precision linear system being solved. In this paper, we discuss the algorithmic implications of accelerating transport iterations in double precision (64 bit) while using a reduced precision (32 bit) DSA solve. We initially implement a stand alone code to investigate any theoretical limitations of reduced precision DSA in slabs. Then we implement reduced precision DSA in ARDRA, Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory's (LLNL) discrete ordinance neutral particle transport code, coupled with {\it hypre}, LLNL's scalable linear solver and multigrid methods library capable of mixed precision linear solves Then we solve various neutron transport problems of interest. We find that in most circumstances, DSA acceleration in reduced precision has little to no impact on the acceleration properties of DSA when used with source iteration in most circumstances. However, as tighter tolerances are required, reduced precision DSA may fail to successfully accelerate the transport solve and increase time to solution.

</details>

### [85] [Spectral-Target Physical Latent Structuring for JEPA-Style World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04264)
*Penghao Zhu,Salvatore Penachio,Kaustav Mukherjee,Aneesh Jonelagadda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"Fourier auxiliary head"的训练时辅助监督方法，用于解决潜在世界模型（latent world models）中的"物理表示懒惰"（physical representation laziness）失效模式。该方法通过对潜在空间施加物理信息结构约束，在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升动态环境中的规划成功率，同时提升数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型（如LeWorldModel）虽然通过正则化技术（如SIGReg）避免了表示坍缩（representation collapse），但在高动态环境中仍存在一种新失效模式——物理表示懒惰。即潜在状态虽未坍缩，但未能编码关键物理属性，导致下游规划普遍失败。现有方法无法解决这一问题，需要一种能物理性结构化潜在空间的训练方案。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的"Fourier auxiliary head"作为训练时的辅助监督。该辅助头利用傅里叶特征对潜在空间施加物理信息约束，使编码器和预测器联合训练的潜在表征具备物理结构，且该过程不增加任何推理时计算开销，可泛化至任意环境。

Result: 实验表明：1) 在基线LeWM展现物理表示懒惰的动态环境中，辅助头显著提升规划成功率；2) 在基线不存在该问题的其他环境中也有小幅改进；3) 更好的规划性能伴随潜在空间与关键物理属性更高的相关性；4) 在低数据场景下，辅助监督对成功率提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: Fourier auxiliary head方法能够有效避免潜在世界模型中的物理表示懒惰问题，同时提升整体成功率和数据效率，支持训练时物理结构化的潜在表示对规划性能有积极作用的观点。

Abstract: Latent world models have become increasingly popular as a method to predict and plan in latent space rather than pixel space. Recent architectures, such as LeWorldModel (LeWM), jointly train the encoder and predictor using regularization techniques like SIGReg to prevent representation collapse. Even with such regularization preventing representation collapse, we identify a new world model failure mode of \textit{physical representation laziness}, particularly noted in highly dynamic environments. For these lazy cases, the learned latent states do not collapse but nonetheless fail to represent key physical properties, causing ubiquitous downstream planning failure. To resolve this issue, we propose training-time auxiliary supervision with a lightweight "Fourier auxiliary head", which enforces physically-informed structuring of the latent space with no additional inference-time cost and can be generalized to any environment. Experimentally, we show that the auxiliary head substantially improves planning success rates in dynamic environments where the baseline LeWM exhibits physical representation laziness. It also leads to modest improvements in other environments, even when the baseline does not exhibit physical representation laziness. We further observe superior planning performance being accompanied by higher latent space correlations with key physical properties, indicating both the ability of our method to physically structure latent states and the potential planning-side benefit to the learned representation being physically structured. We also see in low-data regimes, auxiliary supervision is particularly impactful in increasing success rate. These findings support the use of our Fourier auxiliary head method to improve both overall success rate and data efficiency, while avoiding representation laziness in latent world models.

</details>


### [86] [An Energy-Based Conservative-Dissipative Latent Neural Evolution Operator for Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04530)
*Sebastian Schaffer,Lukas Exl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的磁微磁动力学降阶模型，将卷积自编码器与结构化的潜空间神经常微分方程相结合。潜向量场由学习到的标量势的梯度通过反对称算子和对称半正定耗散算子生成，保留了Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的进动-耗散结构。模型仅使用短轨迹窗口的潜空间与解码回传损失联合训练，无需时间导数监督或物理能量标签。在NIST μMAG标准问题4的两个数据集上，反对称-耗散模型在长时间推演中显著优于纯耗散模型，且深度二次能量表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁微磁模拟计算成本高昂，迫切需要高保真且计算高效的降阶模型用于长时间轨迹预测。同时，现有数据驱动的动力学降阶模型往往忽略Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程固有的物理结构（进动与耗散的耦合），导致长时间外推时误差快速累积、物理一致性缺失。作者希望构建一个既保留物理结构、又能实现高效推理的潜空间动力学模型。

Method: 提出energy-based的潜空间动力学框架：(1) 用卷积自编码器将高维磁化状态映射到低维潜坐标；(2) 潜空间向量场由学习标量势的梯度分别经反对称算子（产生等能量面上的进动运动）和对称半正定耗散算子（使能量单调下降）组合生成；(3) 编码器、解码器、潜能量及两个算子由短轨迹窗口的潜损失和解码回传损失联合联合优化，無需时间导数监督；(4) 推理时仅编码一次初始状态，在潜空间连续演化，仅在需要输出时解码。实验中比较了二次、深度、加性深度-二次三种潜能量形式，在两个场方向的标准问题4数据集上评估长时间无中断推演精度。

Result: 在NIST μMAG标准问题4的两个场方向数据集上：纯耗散模型与反对称-耗散模型在短训练风格窗口内精度相当，但在无中断长时间 rollout 中差异显著——后者轨迹预测明显更准确。深度二次能量在两种场方向下均取得最佳总体精度，且当推演延长至两倍训练时域时表现出更慢的误差增长速率，表明其外推泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将物理结构（进动-耗散分解的向量场生成方式）嵌入潜空间神经ODE，能够在无物理能量标签、无时间导数监督的前提下，仅依靠轨迹数据学习出具有物理一致性（能量单调下降且等能面运动）的动力学模型。反对称-耗散耦合结构对长时间无中断推演的稳定性与准确性至关重要，而深度二次能量形式的表达能力足以捕捉复杂的磁化动力学，为高效磁微磁仿真提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We develop an energy-based reduced-order model for micromagnetic magnetization dynamics that couples a convolutional autoencoder to a structured latent neural ordinary differential equation. Motivated by the precessional-dissipative structure of the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, the latent vector field is generated from the gradient of a learned scalar potential through an antisymmetric operator and a symmetric positive-semidefinite dissipative operator.
