This potential is learned in nonunique latent coordinates and is not identified with the Gibbs free energy, but decreases monotonically along autonomous continuous-time solutions, while the antisymmetric component permits motion along its level sets. The encoder, decoder, latent energy, and operators are trained jointly on short trajectory windows using latent and decoded-rollout losses alone, without time-derivative supervision, physical-energy labels, or dissipation penalties. At inference, an initial state is encoded once, evolved in latent space, and decoded only at the requested output times, enabling substantially cheaper trajectory prediction than the micromagnetic solver used to generate the training data. We compare quadratic, deep, and additive deep-quadratic latent energies on two datasets parameterized by field amplitude and generated for the two applied-field directions of the NIST $μ$MAG Standard Problem 4. Dissipative-only and antisymmetric-dissipative models achieve comparable accuracy on short training-style windows but differ substantially on uninterrupted rollouts, for which the antisymmetric-dissipative models provide markedly more accurate trajectory predictions. The deep-quadratic energy gives the best overall accuracy for both field directions and exhibits slower error growth when rollouts are extended to twice the training horizon.

</details>


### [87] [ProToMEx: Rapid, Interpretable Explanations via Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04265)
*Athina Georgara,Adarsh Valoor,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProToMEx是一个基于概率主题模型（PTM）的模型无关可解释性框架，超越了传统特征归因方法，通过学习潜在"主题"来揭示模型决策背后的高级语义结构，同时提供全局和局部解释，且在保真度与SHAP/LIME相当的情况下实现了约30-40倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验解释方法（如SHAP、LIME）主要基于特征归因，无法有效表达驱动模型决策的复杂组合模式。作者旨在克服这一局限，提供一种能够揭示多个共同存在的决策原因及其底层语义结构的可解释性新范式。

Method: 提出ProToMEx框架，利用概率主题模型（PTM）学习表征分类决策的高级潜在"主题"。该框架是模型无关的，能够自然地同时提供全局解释（模型整体行为）和局部解释（特定预测的多个共存原因）。

Result: 在标准化表格数据集和合成数据集上的实验表明，ProToMEx生成的解释在保真度上与SHAP和LIME相当，但生成局部解释的摊销计算成本大幅降低，速度比SHAP和LIME快约30-40倍，适合实时应用场景。

Conclusion: ProToMEx证明了基于概率主题模型的解释范式是传统特征归因方法的有效替代方案，能在不牺牲解释质量的前提下显著提升计算效率，为实时机器学习应用中的可解释性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Existing post-hoc explainers for machine learning classifiers primarily focus on feature attribution, assigning importance scores to individual features. While valuable, this approach struggles to articulate the complex, combinatorial patterns that often drive a model's decision-making process. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProToMEx, a new paradigm for explainability that leverages Probabilistic Topic Models (PTMs). Our model-agnostic framework learns latent ''topics'' that represent distinct, high-level reasons for a classification, moving beyond simple feature importance to reveal underlying semantic structures. ProToMEx naturally provides both global explanations of a model's overall behaviour and local explanations that can disentangle multiple co-existing reasons for a specific prediction. We demonstrate empirically that ProToMEx not only produces explanations of comparable fidelity to popular methods like SHAP and LIME but also drastically reduces the amortised computational cost of generating local explanations, making it highly suitable for real-time applications. Specifically, we show that ProToMEx is ~30-40x faster than SHAP and LIME over standardised tabular datasets and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [88] [A Data Fusion Framework for Grounding Aerospace Surrogate Model via Experimental Wind-Tunnel Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04267)
*Nitin Nagesh Kulkarni,Dheeraj Vemula,Yin Yu,Peter Lyu,Juan J. Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于实验数据的校正框架，利用风洞PSP测量数据对基于CFD训练的深度学习代理模型进行纠偏，在不重新训练代理模型的前提下，学习CFD与实验之间的系统性差异，从而提升代理模型对实验数据的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于高保真CFD数据训练的代理模型虽然能精确复现CFD的数值预测（包括标量输出和流场），但由于CFD与实验观测之间存在系统性偏差，其预测保真度受到限制，无法直接匹配实验数据。

Method: 论文采用Geo-transolver架构的代理模型，基于NASA CRM翼身构型的2,300个高保真CFD仿真进行训练（覆盖几何变化、马赫数0.70-0.85、迎角0-4度）。随后在不重新训练代理模型的前提下，训练一个校正网络，该网络在空间配准的PSP（压力敏感涂料）测量数据上学习代理模型预测与实验测量的表面压力分布之间的差异（两个自由流马赫数0.70和0.85，覆盖相同迎角范围）。最终将校正网络叠加到预训练代理模型上形成"接地"的代理模型。

Result: 代理模型在CFD集成气动力与俯仰力矩上R2>0.99。在Mach 0.85下，校正网络显著改善了与PSP数据的一致性，特别是在机翼吸力峰、激波位置及后续压力恢复区域，降低了预测误差的幅值和Cp误差超过0.05的浸润表面积比例。在校出迎角上，接地代理模型与测量的偏差在测量Cp范围的2.3-2.7%以内，并且在所有测试状态下都优于在测量条件之间的直接插值方法。

Conclusion: 实验测量可以通过学习系统性的CFD与实验差异来"接地"大规模仿真训练的代理模型，同时保持其泛化能力和计算效率，无需修改预训练代理模型的参数，仅需有限的实验数据即可实现显著的精度提升。

Abstract: Aerodynamic surrogate models trained on high-fidelity CFD data reproduce numerical predictions of both scalar outputs and entire fields accurately, yet their predictive fidelity is limited by systematic discrepancies between CFD and experimental observations. We present an experimentally grounded correction framework that adapts a CFD-trained deep learning surrogate using wind-tunnel PSP measurements. A Geotransolver surrogate trained on 2,300 high-fidelity CFD simulations of the NASA CRM wing-body configuration, spanning geometric variation, Mach 0.70-0.85, and angles of attack 0 to 4 degrees, reproduces the CFD integrated aerodynamic forces and pitching moment to R2 > 0.99 but does not match the experimental data. To incorporate experimental information without retraining the surrogate, a correction network is trained on spatially registered PSP measurements at two freestream Mach numbers (0.70 and 0.85) across the same angle-of-attack range, learning the discrepancy between the surrogate-predicted and experimentally measured surface-pressure distributions. At Mach 0.85 the correction substantially improves agreement with PSP, particularly at the wing suction peak, shock location, and subsequent pressure recovery, reducing both the magnitude of the prediction error and the fraction of wetted surface on which it exceeds 0.05 in Cp, and it does so from a limited experimental dataset without modifying the pretrained surrogate parameters. On held-out angles of attack the grounded surrogate agrees with measurement to within 2.3-2.7% of the measured Cp range, and outperforms direct interpolation between the measured conditions at every state tested. Experimental measurements can therefore ground a large-scale simulation-trained surrogate by learning systematic CFD-to-experiment discrepancies while preserving its generalization capability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [89] [Quantum-Assisted Memory-Efficient Training for Parameter-Intensive Wi-Fi-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04271)
*To Truong An,Jie Zhang,Guolin Yin,Junqing Zhang,Yanjiao Li,Trung Q. Duong,Simon L. Cotton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-MET框架，一种量子辅助的混合量子经典神经网络训练方法，用于Wi-Fi人体活动识别（HAR）系统，通过间接参数生成和结构化剪枝，显著减少了训练和推理阶段的资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Wi-Fi的HAR系统大多依赖深度学习模型，在训练和推理阶段都存在计算和内存开销大的问题，传统训练需同时更新百万级参数，内存消耗惊人，难以实际部署。亟需一种更高效、资源友好的训练框架。

Method: Q-MET利用混合量子经典神经网络间接生成HAR模型参数，相比直接优化大幅减少可训练参数数量；同时在训练阶段集成结构化剪枝，以支持在资源受限设备上的轻量级推理部署。

Result: Q-MET相比传统反向传播DL训练减少了90%至95%的可训练参数，同时保持或超越经典分类精度；通过结构化剪枝实现75%至85%的模型稀疏度，分类精度损失低于2%。

Conclusion: 这是首个同时解决HAR系统训练和推理阶段内存效率问题的量子辅助方法，为资源受限环境下的智能感知与通信一体化提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition (HAR) has become an important part of integrated sensing and communications, paving the way for a range of context-aware services. However, most existing Wi-Fi-based HAR systems rely on deep learning (DL) models that are computationally and memory intensive in both training and inference, which poses significant challenges for real-world deployment. Conventional training requires simultaneous updates of millions of parameters, leading to prohibitive memory consumption. In this paper, we propose a novel quantum-assisted memory-efficient training framework (Q-MET) designed to improve efficiency in both training and inference. Q-MET utilizes a hybrid quantum classical neural network to indirectly generate parameters for HAR models, significantly reducing the trainable parameter count compared to direct optimization. To further support the deployment on resource-constrained devices, we integrate structured pruning during the training phase. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-MET achieves a 90% to 95% reduction in trainable parameters compared with conventional backpropagation-based DL training while maintaining or even exceeding classical classification accuracy. Additionally, Q-MET supports lightweight inference through structured pruning, achieving 75% to 85% model sparsity with less than 2% loss in classification accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this work represents the first quantum-assisted approach to simultaneously tackle memory inefficiencies in both the training and inference stages of HAR systems.

</details>


### [90] [Interpretability for Turing Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04661)
*Billy Snikkers,Rumi Salazar,Daniel Murfet,Will Troiani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一种针对神经网络的可解释性技术——敏感性（susceptibilities）——能够通过探测学习问题的局部损失景观，识别噪声图灵机中的算法结构。作者理论上证明算法中的对称性和路径分离会在敏感性矩阵中诱导排列对称性和低秩块，并在确定性有限自动机（DFA）上实证展示了通过主成分分析和聚类方法可以在敏感性空间恢复算法特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络内部是否以及如何编码算法结构是机器可解释性研究的核心问题。以往研究主要在神经网络自身层面进行分析，而本文希望借助一个理论可控的框架（Murfet和Troiani提出的噪声图灵机学习问题），严格证明可解释性技术（特别是敏感性分析）能否揭示算法层面的结构。

Method: 作者（1）理论上证明图灵机实现算法中的对称性会诱导敏感性矩阵中的排列对称性，且路径分离（算法的不同处理分支）会诱导低秩块结构；（2）实证上在一组确定性有限自动机（DFA）上进行实验，通过计算敏感性矩阵，然后应用主成分分析（PCA）和聚类方法在敏感性空间中恢复算法特征。

Result: 实验结果表明，敏感性确实包含关于底层DFA算法结构的信息，可以通过主成分分析和聚类方法从敏感性空间中提取出可辨识的算法特征，验证了理论断言的有效性。

Conclusion: 敏感性作为一种可解释性工具，不仅适用于分析神经网络的内部表征，还能在噪声图灵机学习问题中作为一种探针，揭示学习算法所实现的抽象算法结构，为机器可解释性研究提供了理论与实证结合的桥梁。

Abstract: We show that susceptibilities, an interpretability technique developed for neural networks, can identify the presence of algorithmic structure in Turing machines by probing the local loss landscape of a learning problem for noisy Turing machines introduced by Murfet and Troiani (arXiv:2504.08075). We prove that symmetries and path separation in the algorithm implemented by a Turing machine induce permutation symmetries and low-rank blocks in its susceptibility matrix. We study this empirically on a set of deterministic finite automata (DFAs) and demonstrate that algorithmic features can be recovered by principal component analysis and clustering methods in susceptibility space.

</details>


### [91] [Variational Continuation for Double Pendulum Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05337)
*Leo Yao,Ziming Liu,Max Tegmark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Hessian矩阵的方法来数值延拓动力系统中的周期轨道，通过自动微分替代手工推导的雅可比矩阵，利用损失函数平坦方向（零特征值方向）确定延拓方向，实现无积分器、高效引导的周期轨道搜索，并在双摆系统中展示了完整的周期轨道延拓及分岔检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的周期轨道数值延拓方法依赖于手工推导的雅可比矩阵，过程繁琐且难以自动化；同时现有方法需要积分器，初始化不稳定固定点附近的震荡较为困难。该论文旨在利用机器学习领域的自动微分技术，自动化地构建延拓所需的微分结构，从而高效、精确地搜索和延续周期轨道。

Method: 将周期轨道候选表示为傅里叶级数形式（一个环），基于该环偏离物理微分方程的程度定义损失函数。利用自动微分计算损失函数的Hessian矩阵，通过寻找损失景观中的平坦方向（零特征值方向）来确定延拓方向。该方法无需使用积分器，通过Hessian的特征结构精确初始化不稳定固定点附近的震荡，并能有效检测轨道族交点和次谐波分岔。

Result: 以双摆系统为例，从固定点出发完成了周期震荡的完整延拓，展示了沿轨道族发生的分岔并分类了周期轨道分支。特别地，发现了两个摆锤质量从未同时静止的周期轨道，这在现有文献中尚未被报道。

Conclusion: 该Hessian驱动、基于自动微分的周期轨道延拓方法无需积分器，能够自动化地引导延拓过程并有效检测分岔，展示了在复杂动力系统中发现新型周期轨道的潜力，为动力系统周期轨道数值分析提供了新的自动化工具。

Abstract: We present a Hessian-based approach to numerically continue periodic orbits in dynamical systems. A loop (periodic orbit candidate) is parametrized as a Fourier series; a loss function is defined based on the deviation of the loop from the physical differential equations. Unlike previous work relying on hand-derived Jacobians, our method automates the process by leveraging automatic differentiation, a common machine learning technique. The continuation direction can be determined by the flat directions of the loss landscapes (directions with zero eigenvalues), making the search of periodic orbits efficient and guided. Our method is integrator-free, precisely initializes oscillations around unstable fixed points, and efficiently detects orbit family intersections and subharmonic bifurcations. As a demonstration, we present full continuations of periodic double pendulum oscillations from fixed points, showing bifurcations along orbit families and categorizing branches of periodic orbits. In particular, we find periodic orbits where both pendulum masses are never simultaneously at rest, which to our knowledge has been missing in the literature.

</details>


### [92] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Forced Outage Risk Prediction: Benefits and Comparison to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04272)
*Christos Petridis,Zoran Obradovic,Mladen Kezunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在零样本框架下评估大型语言模型（LLMs）预测配电网天气相关强制停电风险的能力，与监督学习方法对比。结果显示监督模型在宏F1和精确率上优于LLMs，但新一代LLM达到竞争性分数，且LLM在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面具有互补优势，建议将两者结合作为最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM是否能在无需标注训练数据的零样本框架下有效预测配电网因天气引发的强迫停电风险，为电网运维提供一个可快速部署且具地理可扩展性的风险评估方案，弥补传统监督学习依赖大量标注数据的局限性。

Method: 将问题形式化为跨三个预测时域（3h、6h、12h）的二分类严重性任务；使用德克萨斯州中部某公用事业服务区域六年的停电记录和高分辨率天气预报数据；对四种零样本LLM与两种监督分类器进行了两个输入配置（当前天气观测和天气预报数据）的基准比较。

Result: 监督模型在宏F1分数和精确率方面优于LLM，而新一代LLM达到了具有竞争力的分数。LLM在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面展现出互补优势。

Conclusion: 鉴于LLM和监督模型各有优势——前者擅长零样本推理与可扩展性，后者在预测精度上更优——将两者结合使用可能是配电网停电风险预测的最佳实践。

Abstract: This study examines the ability of large language models (LLMs) to predict the risk of weather-related forced outages in the distribution grid in a zero-shot framework, without labeled training data. The problem is formulated as a binary severity classification task across three forecast horizons (3h, 6h, 12h), using six years of outage records and high-resolution weather data for a utility service area in central Texas. Four zero-shot LLMs are benchmarked against two supervised classifiers across two input configurations: one using current weather observations and the other using weather forecast data. Results show that supervised models outperform LLMs on macro-F1 and precision, while newer LLM generations achieve competitive scores. Beyond accuracy, LLMs offer complementary strengths in actionable reasoning and geographic scalability, suggesting that combining them with supervised models may be the best practice.

</details>


### [93] [Confounding-Valid Conformal Inference for Counterfactual KPIs in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05073)
*Abdessamed Qchohi,Jessica Moysen Cortes,Matteo Zecchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CV-CCI方法，结合丰富的观测遥测数据与有限的随机化数据，在存在隐藏混淆的条件下实现具有有限样本覆盖率保证的反事实推理，同时在效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商需要根据日志遥测数据回答"what-if"反事实问题，但日志数据可能遗漏控制器使用的变量，导致隐藏混淆并使反事实分析的统计保证失效。虽然随机化遥测可以解决混淆问题，但它可能干扰正常运营，因此数据稀缺，单独使用会产生信息量不足（不informative）的预测集。需要一种方法同时利用观测数据的高效性和随机化数据的有效性。

Method: 提出Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI)方法。该方法融合大量可能混淆的观测遥测数据与有限的随机化数据，基于General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI)原则：观测数据用于提升预测效率（efficiency），随机化数据用于在任意隐藏混淆下保留有限样本覆盖率（finite-sample coverage guarantees）。

Result: 在两个代表性的无线接入网络（RAN）控制任务上进行的实验表明，CV-CCI在隐藏混淆下保持统计有效性（valid），同时生成的预测集比现有最先进的反混淆（confounding-valid）基线方法更高效（efficient）。

Conclusion: CV-CCI成功解决了网络反事实推理中"观测数据丰富但可能存在混淆"与"随机化数据无混淆但稀缺"的两难问题，在保证覆盖率有效性的同时提升了预测效率，为网络运营中的关键性能指标（KPI）反事实预测提供了可靠且实用的方案。

Abstract: Conformal counterfactual inference enables network operators to use logged telemetry to reliably answer 'what-if' questions about network operation. These answers typically take the form of prediction sets that contain, with a user-defined probability, the key performance indicators (KPIs) that would have been observed under alternative control actions. A key challenge is that logged telemetry may omit variables used by the controller, resulting in hidden confounding and invalidating the statistical guarantees of counterfactual analysis. In principle, this issue can be addressed using randomized telemetry, collected by assigning control actions independently of the network state. However, because such randomization may disrupt normal operation, randomized telemetry is typically scarce, causing counterfactual analysis based solely on it to produce uninformative prediction sets. To address these challenges, we propose Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI), which combines abundant, potentially confounded observational telemetry with limited randomized data through the General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI) principle. CV-CCI leverages observational data to improve efficiency while using randomized data to retain finite-sample coverage guarantees under arbitrary hidden confounding. Experiments on two representative radio access network (RAN) control tasks show that CV-CCI remains valid under hidden confounding while producing more efficient prediction sets than state-of-the-art confounding-valid baselines.

</details>


### [94] [BER-PEF: Unified Human Mobility Predictability Evaluation via Bayes Error Rate Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04292)
*En Xu,Jingtao Ding,Zhiwen Yu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BER-PEF框架，将贝叶斯错误率（BER）估计转化为移动性可预测性估计，为缺乏真实可观测ground truth的可预测性估计器提供统一的比较与评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动可预测性研究关注给定目标和输入信息下可获得的最佳预测性能，但真实可预测性的ground truth无法在真实移动数据上直接观测，导致不同估计器之间缺乏可比较的统一基准。

Method: BER-PEF框架将BER估计转换为移动性可预测性估计。它将符号序列、数值轨迹、上下文特征和学习表示映射到统一的特征-标签空间，然后沿受控扰动曲线评估估计器输出，通过测量低于参考区间、高于参考区间以及跨越整个参考区间的偏差，与共享的可预测性参考区间进行比较。

Result: 在Foursquare NYC和TKY、GeoLife以及T-Drive数据集上的实验表明，多个基于BER的估计器在符号序列和数值轨迹上比现有可预测性方法取得更低参考偏差，且其估计值能在扰动下追踪经验预测性能的变化。额外分析显示上下文输入和多种结构化表示可在同一协议下评估，多扰动水平的证据聚合比单一未扰动观测更可靠。

Conclusion: BER-PEF为异构移动数据上缺乏ground-truth可预测性的可预测性估计器提供了一条统一且可验证的评估路径。

Abstract: Human mobility predictability concerns the best prediction performance attainable from a given target and input information, but its ground truth is not directly observable on real mobility data. We present BER-PEF, a Bayes-error-rate-based framework that converts BER estimation into mobility predictability estimation and provides a unified protocol for comparing estimators without observable ground truth. The framework maps symbolic sequences, numeric trajectories, contextual features, and learned representations into a common feature--label space, then evaluates estimator outputs along controlled perturbation curves against a shared predictability reference interval by measuring deviations below the interval, above the interval, and across the full interval. Experiments on Foursquare NYC and TKY, GeoLife, and T-Drive show that several BER-based estimators achieve lower reference discrepancy than existing predictability methods on symbolic sequences and numeric trajectories, while their estimates track changes in empirical prediction performance under perturbation. Additional analyses show that contextual inputs and multiple structured representations can be evaluated under the same protocol, and that aggregating evidence across multiple perturbation levels provides a more reliable basis for estimator selection than relying on a single unperturbed observation. BER-PEF therefore offers a unified and verifiable path for evaluating predictability estimators on heterogeneous mobility data when ground-truth predictability is unavailable.

</details>


### [95] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于β变分自编码器（VAE）的ECG表征能否用于区分心肌病患者的LGE+（心肌疤痕）与LGE-状态。在300例本地队列中，对比了基础ECGx.AI模型的32维特征与在正常PTB-XL ECG上训练的较浅β-VAE特征。ECGx.AI配合随机森林达到0.686的AUC，β-VAE配合梯度提升达到0.577的AUC（灵敏度0.775）。值得注意的是，12导联中有10个导联的DTW重建误差在类别间存在显著差异（Mann-Whitney U检验），配合逻辑回归可达到0.643的AUC，支持其作为疤痕相关ECG改变的潜在标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振中的晚期钆增强（LGE）是心肌疤痕的关键标志物，但MRI检查的可及性有限，因此需要基于常规ECG的筛查方法，以提高心肌疤痕检测的可及性和效率。

Method: 使用300名心肌病患者的本地队列，比较两种ECG表征方法：（1）基础模型ECGx.AI提取的32维特征；（2）在正常PTB-XL ECG上训练的较浅β-VAE特征。采用下游分类（随机森林、梯度提升、逻辑回归）和基于动态时间规整（DTW）的重建误差进行评估。

Result: ECGx.AI配合随机森林实现AUC=0.686；β-VAE配合梯度提升实现AUC=0.577和灵敏度0.775；DTW重建误差在12个导联中的10个表现出显著类别差异（Mann-Whitney U检验），配合逻辑回归达到AUC=0.643。

Conclusion: ECGx.AI特征优于浅层β-VAE特征用于LGE分类，但DTW重建误差显示出作为疤痕相关ECG改变标记物的潜力，可能成为有前景的补充特征用于心肌疤痕筛查。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [96] [Data-Driven Learning of Unknown Nonlinear Differential Equations Using Functional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04329)
*Seyyed Shaho Alaviani,Yongzhi Qu,Gregory W. Vogl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的可解释机器学习方法，用于从单一状态轨迹数据中发现非线性常微分方程（ODE）的未知向量场。该方法基于泛函分析和算子理论在函数空间中构造代价函数（以积分形式度量两函数间距离），并采用增量学习算法在线处理新训练样本，可同时发现未知外部力（时间的函数）和未知底层动力学，适用于受迫/非受迫、自治/非自治（时变）动力系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动ODE发现方法通常基于离散误差求和的最小化策略，缺乏函数空间的严格理论框架；且大多需要系统的物理先验知识，难以同时处理外部强迫项和时变系统。

Method: 1) 基于泛函分析和算子理论重新表述非线性ODE的数据驱动发现问题；2) 在函数空间中构建代价函数，以两函数间距离的积分形式替代传统离散误差求和；3) 提出增量学习算法以在线方式处理新训练样本。

Result: 数值实验展示了该方法在受迫与非受迫、自治与非自治（时变）动力学系统中的优势，能够同时识别作为时间函数的未知外部力和未知底层动力学。

Conclusion: 该方法是数据驱动ODE发现领域的新范式，通过函数空间中的积分代价函数和增量学习机制，无需系统物理先验知识即可从单个轨迹中学习未知向量场，拓展了现有方法的适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, the problem of data-driven discovery of nonlinear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) is recast, and a new interpretable machine learning (ML) method is proposed. The proposed method aims to learn the unknown vector field of nonlinear dynamics without prior knowledge of the system's physics from only one single state trajectory's data. The proposed method has two fundamental differences with existing methods: 1) the formulation presented in this method is derived based on Functional Analysis and Operator Theory, and 2) the cost function is constructed in the function space as a distance between two functions as an integral, instead of the discrete-sum of errors used in existing ML approaches. An incremental learning algorithm is proposed to learn the unknown vector field to handle new training samples in an online manner. The proposed method can discover the unknown vector field from both forced and unforced autonomous and non-autonomous (or time-varying) dynamical systems. The proposed method is able to simultaneously discover unknown external forces as a function of time and unknown underlying dynamics. Finally, numerical examples are given to demonstrate the advantages of the proposed method.

</details>


### [97] [Modular Deep Recurrent Neural Network: Application to Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04339)
*Nima Mohajerin,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化的深度循环神经网络(RNN)，可自动计算梯度并便于部署不同架构。通过在多層RNN中加入前馈层间连接，显著提升了网络学习高阶动力学和非线性的能力，同时缓解了空间维度上梯度消失/爆炸问题。在四旋翼飞行器高度动力学建模案例中，该方法比现有方法泛化更快甚至完全有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度RNN在多層结构中存在梯度消失/爆炸问题，且可部署的架构有限、梯度计算繁琐。作者旨在引入模块化设计以简化不同架构RNN的部署和自动求导，同时通过改进网络结构提升对高阶动力学和非线性建模的能力。

Method: 提出一种模块化深度RNN框架，允许灵活组合不同架构并自动计算梯度。在此基础上，提出一种包含前馈层间连接（feedforward inter-layer connections）的新架构，将浅层信息直接传递到更深层，以改善信息流动并缓解空间维度上的梯度问题。

Result: 实验表明，加入前馈层间连接的多層RNN在建模四旋翼飞行器高度动力学方面表现优异，能够快速泛化并准确学习高阶非线性的动力学模型，而现有方法要么泛化慢，要么根本无法泛化。

Conclusion: 前馈层间连接是改善深度RNN学习能力的一种有效手段，模块化框架则为探索和部署新型RNN架构提供了灵活性。该工作对深度递归网络的结构设计和在动力学系统建模中的应用具有参考价值。

Abstract: A modular deep Recurrent Neural Network (RNN) is introduced to facilitate the process of deploying various architectures of RNNs, and to automatically compute derivatives for gradient-based learning methods. The modularity leads to a set of new architectures, one of which includes feedforward inter-layer connections. By adding feedforward inter-layer connections in a multi-layer RNN, it is observed that the capability of the RNN to learn and model high-order dynamics and nonlinearities is significantly improved. The problem of vanishing/exploding gradient in space for a multilayer RNN is also alleviated using feedforward connections. These results are demonstrated using a quadrotor case study, for which a model of the altitude dynamics is learned with our particular network structure, while existing methods are unable to generalize as quickly or at all.

</details>


### [98] [SharedSAE: One Feature Dictionary Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04344)
*Daniil Ognev,Célian Vasson,Lijie Hu,Kentaro Inui,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SharedSAE 方法，用一个共享稀疏自编码器（SAE）替代多个模型各自专用的 SAE。该方法结合共享字典与模型专属的编码器-解码器对，仅对选择分数进行归一化而保留激活幅度，并通过模型 dropout 支持单模型推理。在四个不同家族的 1B 规模语言模型上，SharedSAE 保留了专用 SAE 96.6% 的平均解释方差，潜变量跨模型相关性比事后对齐的独立 SAE 高 1.8 倍，且字典冻结后新模型可高效适配并复用共享潜变量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 SAE 训练和潜变量标注需要在每个模型上重复进行，成本高昂且效率低下。作者希望探索是否可以用一个共享的 SAE 替代每个模型各自的专用 SAE，从而避免针对不同模型的重复训练与标注工作。

Method: SharedSAE 结合一个共享字典与模型专属的编码器-解码器对。与先前方法不同，先前方法丢弃激活幅度且推理时需要所有模型在场，SharedSAE 仅对选择分数做归一化以保留激活幅度，并采用模型 dropout 机制实现单模型推理。实验在四个来自不同家族和分词器的 1B 规模基础语言模型上进行训练。

Result: SharedSAE 保留了专用 SAE 平均解释方差的 96.6%；其潜变量激活的跨模型相关性比事后对齐的独立 SAE 高 1.8 倍；潜变量描述可跨模型迁移。字典冻结后，新模型可高效适配，实现接近专用 SAE 的重构质量，同时复用共享潜变量描述。

Conclusion: 共享的 SAE 字典可以替代每个模型各自的专用 SAE，在保持较高重构质量的同时实现潜变量解释的跨模型复用与迁移，为大规模模型可解释性研究提供了一种更高效的共享方案。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret language model activations, but SAE training and latent labelling are typically repeated for every model. Here, we show that a single shared SAE can replace a collection of dedicated per-model SAEs. Our method, SharedSAE, combines a shared dictionary with model-specific encoder-decoder pairs. Unlike the closest prior method, which discards activation magnitudes and requires all models at inference, SharedSAE instead normalizes only selection scores, preserving magnitudes, and uses model dropout for single-model inference. We train SharedSAE on four 1B-scale base language models spanning distinct families and tokenizers. Despite sharing its latents across models, SharedSAE retains 96.6% of dedicated SAEs' mean explained variance; its latent activations exhibit cross-model correlations 1.8 times as high as separate SAEs aligned post-hoc, and its latent descriptions transfer across models. After the dictionary is frozen, new models can be efficiently adapted to it, achieving near-dedicated-SAE reconstruction quality while reusing the shared latent descriptions.

</details>


### [99] [A Quantum Variational Approach to Prototypical Recurrent Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04354)
*Mahyar Sadeghi Garjan,Tommaso Cesari,Michel Barbeau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的量子原型循环单元（QPRU），其参数量显著少于经典循环架构（LSTM、GRU）及量子变体（QLSTM、QGRU），同时保持与最先进基线相当的预测性能，并具备更好的可扩展性和更少的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经典循环神经网络（如LSTM、GRU）和量子变体（QLSTM、QGRU）参数量庞大，在资源受限环境下面临训练开销大和扩展性差的问题，亟需一种更轻量且性能不降的循环架构。

Method: 设计了一种轻量级量子原型循环单元（QPRU），通过精简量子电路结构和参数化策略，大幅减少模型可训练参数数量，同时保持循环时序建模能力。

Result: QPRU在参数量远少于经典LSTM/GRU及量子QLSTM/QGRU的情况下，取得了与最先进基线相当的预测性能，并在可扩展性和参数效率方面展现出优势。

Conclusion: QPRU在保持高性能的同时显著降低了参数开销，为量子循环网络提供了更轻量、更具可扩展性的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a lightweight Quantum Prototypical Recurrent Unit (QPRU) that requires significantly fewer parameters than both classical recurrent architectures, such as Long Short- Term Memory (LSTM) and Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), and quantum variants, including Quantum LSTM (QLSTM) and Quantum GRU (QGRU). Despite its compact design, the QPRU achieves competitive forecasting performance, matching state-of-the-art baselines while offering important structural and practical advantages, including enhanced scalability and a reduced number of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [100] [On the Abundance of Critical Points of the t-SNE Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04379)
*Nakul Haridas,Ryan Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对t-SNE算法的能量景观进行了理论分析。针对包含原始t-SNE及其大数据极限在内的一般能量族，在特征空间密度具有连续对称性的条件下，通过识别原始特征空间与目标嵌入空间中成对的离散对称性，构造了一族无穷多个不同的临界点。这些临界配置表现出拓扑破坏和虚假聚类等与实际观测一致的特征，为t-SNE算法产生的复杂能量景观现象提供了初步的严格解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE算法已被广泛采用，但其能量函数的非凸性使得严格理解算法捕获的内容变得困难。许多已知的数值实例表明其能量景观非常复杂，存在大量不尊重底层数据拓扑或聚类结构的局部极小值。本文旨在为这些现象提供严格的解释，填补理论分析的空白。

Method: 针对包含原始t-SNE和大数据极限的一般能量族，假设特征空间中的密度满足连续对称性，通过寻找原始特征空间与目标嵌入空间中保持梯度动力学不变的成对离散对称性，系统地构造无穷多族互不相同的临界点/临界配置。

Result: 构造了无穷多族不同的临界点，这些临界配置展现场景驱动的拓扑破坏和虚假聚类特征，与实验观测相吻合；文中通过数值和解析示例验证了该方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为t-SNE能量景观中大量与数据拓扑/聚类结构不一致的局部极小值的出现提供了严格的数学解释，是理解t-SNE算法行为的重要第一步。

Abstract: This paper considers the energy landscape of the t-SNE algorithm. While this algorithm has enjoyed broad adoption, the non-convexity of the associated energy has made it difficult to rigorously understand what the algorithm captures in many settings. In particular, a number of well-known numerical examples, several of which are reproduced in this article, suggest a complicated energy landscape with many local minimizers that do not respect the topology or clustering structure of the underlying data. This work seeks to provide first steps towards a rigorous explanation of these phenomena. Specifically, for a general family of energies, which include both the original t-SNE algorithm and recently identified large data limits, and for densities in feature space which obey a continuous symmetry, we construct infinite families of distinct critical points. These critical points are based upon identifying pairs of discrete symmetries, one in the original feature space and the other in the target embedding space, which are preserved under gradient dynamics. These critical configurations exhibit many characteristics, such as topology breaking and spurious clustering, which are often observed empirically. Finally, numerical and analytical examples are given throughout as a means of illustrating the approach.

</details>


### [101] [Fast Gauss Sums via Flash Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04910)
*Nicolaj Rux,Sebastian Neumayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Flash Attention来高效计算高斯核求和（支持任意带符号权重），通过对输入进行两种简单的增广，将归一化的softmax归约转化为非归一化的高斯和，无需编写任何自定义GPU代码，在特征维度D>8的fp16场景下，在速度、内存开销和精度上均优于编译的PyTorch代码和PyKeOps内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核求和是MMD、核梯度流、Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）等许多核方法的计算核心，效率至关重要。同时，softmax attention（特别是Flash Attention）已经经过了大量的硬件感知代码工程优化。本文的核心动机是利用成熟的Flash Attention基础设施来高效计算高斯核求和，避免编写自定义GPU内核。

Method: 通过对输入施加两种小的增广变换，将归一化的softmax归约转换为非归一化的高斯核求和。这样可以直接复用现成的Flash Attention实现，无需任何自定义GPU代码。该方法的理论优势在于能够利用Flash Attention已经深度优化的硬件感知IO调度机制。

Result: 在特征维度D>8且使用fp16精度的条件下，该方法在速度、内存开销和精度三个方面均优于编译的PyTorch代码以及PyKeOps内核（后者经常被显著超越）。其内存扩展性保持为线性。

Conclusion: 高斯核求和可以通过Flash Attention高效实现，证明了核方法计算与注意力机制之间可以建立直接的桥梁，借助硬件感知的注意力优化基础设施为核方法带来显著的性能收益。

Abstract: Gaussian kernel sums are the computational core of maximum mean discrepancies (MMDs), kernel gradient flows, Stein variational gradient descent (SVGD), and many other kernel methods. At the same time, softmax attention has received an extraordinary amount of hardware-aware code engineering, culminating in flash attention. We show that Gauss kernel sums with arbitrary, signed weights can be evaluated via flash attention: two small input augmentations turn the normalized softmax reduction into the unnormalized Gauss sum, without writing a single line of custom GPU code. For feature dimension D>8 in fp16, this approach beats compiled PyTorch code as well as PyKeOps kernels (often significantly) in speed, memory-overhead and accuracy. Indeed, its memory scaling remains linear.

</details>


### [102] [Disentangling Attention in Deep Operator Learning: A Controlled Study of Data-Driven and Physics-Informed Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04407)
*Amar Alem Koric,Qibang Liu,Seid Koric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对五种具有不同注意力机制的DeepONet变体进行了受控的比较研究，在数据驱动和物理信息训练模式下，系统隔离了交叉注意力、自注意力、token化和注意力深度的影响。结果表明，带交叉注意力的逐传感器token化在所有基准中可将经典DeepONet的相对L2误差降低2.4-28.0倍，查询相关的交叉注意力是最可靠的机制，而分支自注意力对大而复杂的空间函数输入最有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度神经算子研究在引入注意力机制时往往同时改变多个架构组件，使得难以确定究竟是哪个组件真正提升了精度。作者旨在通过受控且系统的消融研究来隔离各个注意力相关组件的独立效应，从而更清晰地理解注意力机制在DeepONet中的实际贡献。

Method: 设计五种具有不同注意力机制的DeepONet变体，在数据驱动和物理信息（physics-informed）两种训练范式下，系统性地隔离并评估交叉注意力、自注意力、token化策略和注意力深度等组件的效果。在三个基准问题（一维非线性扩散-反应方程、一维粘性Burgers方程、二维Poisson热传导问题）上进行测试。

Result: 逐传感器token化加交叉注意力在所有基准-训练组合中将经典DeepONet的均相对L2误差降低2.4-28.0倍，最佳注意力配置达到3.5-32.3倍的提升。仅带点积融合的分支自注意力效果不稳定（降低一维问题的精度但帮助二维问题）；叠加在交叉注意力上可改善全部六个案例但增益较小。增加交叉注意力深度可进一步提升精度但收益递减，且物理信息训练下成本大幅增加。

Conclusion: 查询依赖的交叉注意力是最可靠的注意力机制，适合普遍使用；分支自注意力则对于大规模、空间复杂的函数输入最为有效。该研究为神经算子中注意力组件的设计选择提供了清晰的实证指导。

Abstract: Deep neural operators learn mappings between input functions and complete PDE solution fields, enabling forward evaluations of new problem instances orders of magnitude faster than conventional numerical solvers. Attention mechanisms have recently been introduced into neural operators, but most studies change several architectural components at once, making it difficult to identify what actually improves accuracy. This work presents a controlled and systematic study of five deep operator network (DeepONet) variants with distinct attention mechanisms, trained under both data-driven and physics-informed regimes, to isolate the effects of cross-attention, self-attention, tokenization, and attention depth. We evaluate them on a source-driven transient one-dimensional nonlinear diffusion-reaction equation, a transient one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with variable initial conditions, and a two-dimensional Poisson heat-conduction problem with heterogeneous source fields. Per-sensor tokenization with cross-attention reduces the mean relative L_2 error of the classical DeepONet in all benchmark-training combinations by factors of 2.4-28.0, while the best attention configurations reach 3.5-32.3. Branch self-attention paired only with dot-product fusion is inconsistent, degrading the one-dimensional problems while helping the more complex two-dimensional source field; added on top of cross-attention it improves all six cases, though by less than cross-attention fusion alone. Global pre-mixing provides no consistent benefit. Increasing cross-attention depth further improves accuracy, but with diminishing returns and a substantially higher cost under physics-informed training. Overall, query-dependent cross-attention is the most reliable mechanism, whereas branch self-attention is most useful for large, spatially complex functional inputs.

</details>


### [103] [PRICE: A Systematic Study of LLM Adaptation Choices for Bitcoin Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05235)
*Maryam Fakhari,Mehran Safayani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRICE，一种系统化的大语言模型（LLM）自适应方法，用于短期比特币价格预测。PRICE基于4比特量化的LLaMA-3 8B模型，集成了LoRA参数高效微调、递归多步推理、整数取整数值表示、CTF提示和零温度解码等策略。消融实验和对比实验表明，每个组件均对预测精度有贡献，且PRICE在验证集和测试集上均优于8种Transformer基线和时间序列基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场具有极端波动性和非平稳动态特性，传统预测方法面临挑战。尽管LLM在时间序列预测方面展现出潜力，但不同自适应选择（微调、数值表示、提示、推理和解码）在金融场景中的联合影响尚未被系统探索。现有研究多关注LLM的基础能力，缺乏对自适应策略协同效应的结构化分析。

Method: PRICE方法包含五个核心组件：(1) 使用LoRA进行参数高效微调，以适应有限的硬件资源；(2) 递归多步推断策略；(3) 整数取整的数值表示以减少预测误差；(4) Context-Task-Format (CTF)提示模板；(5) 精确零温度解码以保证递归预测的稳定性。通过系统的消融实验评估每个组件的独立贡献。

Result: 消融实验表明每个组件（LoRA、递归推断、整数取整、CTF提示、零温度解码）均对预测精度和可靠性有贡献。与8种基于Transformer和时间序列基础模型的对比中，PRICE在验证集和测试集上均取得最低的预测误差，并在不同评估周期内保持稳健性能。尽管基于主要为文本而非时间序列数据预训练的模型，PRICE的性能仍可与专门的基础模型竞争甚至超越。

Conclusion: 自适应选择（微调方法、数值表示、提示策略、推理方式和解码参数）对LLM在数值时间序列预测中的准确性和鲁棒性具有决定性的影响。PRICE证明了通过精心设计的适配策略，即使基于文本预训练的LLM也能在金融时间序列预测任务中达到甚至超越专门模型的表现。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets exhibit extreme volatility and non-stationary dynamics that challenge conventional forecasting methods. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for time series forecasting, the combined effects of adaptation choices remain largely unexplored in financial settings. This study introduces PRICE, a structured approach for adapting LLMs to short-term Bitcoin price forecasting. Built on a 4-bit quantized LLaMA-3 8B model, PRICE investigates how fine-tuning, numerical representation, prompting, inference, and decoding jointly influence forecasting performance. PRICE integrates Parameter-efficient fine-tuning with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Recursive multi-step inference, Integer-rounded numerical representation, Context-Task-Format (CTF) prompting, and Exact zero-temperature decoding. Ablation studies show that each component contributes to forecasting accuracy and reliability. LoRA enables efficient training on limited hardware, recursive inference improves accuracy, integer-rounded values reduce errors, CTF prompting outperforms Chain-of-Thought, Implicit Chain-of-Thought (iCoT), and few-shot prompting, and zero-temperature decoding improves stability during recursive forecasting. Comparative evaluation against eight transformer-based and time-series foundation models shows that PRICE achieves the lowest forecasting errors on both validation and test sets while maintaining robust performance across evaluation periods. Despite being based on a model primarily pretrained on text rather than time-series data, PRICE achieves competitive or superior performance relative to specialized foundation models. These findings demonstrate that adaptation choices critically determine the accuracy and robustness of LLMs for numerical time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [104] [Coarse-Graining Hidden Representations: Unsupervised Neuron Selection via Mapping Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05126)
*Margherita Mele,Andrea Castagna,Roberto Menichetti,Raffaello Potestio,Alessandro Ingrosso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用映射熵（mapping entropy, ME）作为无监督准则，对过参数化神经网络的隐藏层进行粗粒化（即选择代表性的神经元子集），以识别哪些神经元是任务本质所需的。在师生网络、非线性高斯过程任务及增强MNIST上验证，ME选择的子网络性能优于等规模的随机子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络包含远超任务需求的隐藏单元，但"哪些神经元是关键的"这一区分是否能在无标签、无梯度的纯表示层面被读出，仍不清楚。作者希望找到一种完全无监督的方法，仅依据隐藏激活统计来判定神经元的本质性。

Method: 将神经元选择形式化为对隐藏层的粗粒化问题——保留其中一个神经元子集。用映射熵（mapping entropy, ME）为每个候选选择打分，该量衡量丢弃部分神经元所损失的判别能力（discriminatory power）。使ME最小化的选择被视为最具信息量。该准则完全无监督，仅依赖隐藏激活的统计量。

Result: 在师生网络中，ME优化能恢复最小师生一致表示，并依据隐藏层的残差变异性按比例保留额外单元。在非线性高斯过程任务中，能选出功能类别映射一致的子集，且其偏好类别随训练过程迁移。在任务和增强MNIST上，ME选择的子网络在性能上优于等规模随机子集，在强压缩情形下优势最明显，将构型可区分性与预测性能联系起来。

Conclusion: 映射熵提供了一种无监督、仅依赖激活统计的神经元选择准则，能识别关键神经元并构建性能优于随机子集的压缩子网络，说明表示的构型可区分度与预测能力存在内在关联。

Abstract: Overparameterized neural networks carry far more hidden units than a task nominally requires, raising the question of which neurons are essential and whether that distinction is legible in the representation itself, without labels or gradients. We cast neuron selection as the problem of coarse-graining the hidden layer by retaining a subset of its neurons, and score each putative selection by the mapping entropy (ME). This quantity measures the loss of discriminatory power inherent in discarding part of the network neurons, and the selection that minimises the ME is taken as particularly informative. This criterion is fully unsupervised, in that it depends only on hidden-activation statistics. In teacher-student networks, ME optimisation recovers the minimal teacher-consistent representation and retains extra units in proportion to the hidden layer's residual variability; in a non-linear Gaussian process task, it selects coherent functional-class mappings whose preferred class shifts across training. On this task and on translation-augmented MNIST, ME-selected subnetworks outperform random subsets of equal size, most clearly under strong compression - linking configurational distinguishability to predictive performance.

</details>


### [105] [REFINE: LLM Refinement over Budgeted Text-Attributed Graphs for Personalized Medical Concept Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04415)
*Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFINE是一个面向电子健康记录（EHR）预测的KG感知、预算受限的LLM图精炼框架，用于实现患者个性化的医学概念编码。它从全局文本属性知识图谱（TKG）出发构建患者特定的时序图，通过序列强化学习策略为每个观测代码选择个性化的KG扩展预算，再结合异构图神经网络（GNN）与冻结LLM的图感知软提示，实现关系感知且语义增强的概念表示。在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上的实验表明，REFINE能一致性地提升多种EHR骨干模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数医学概念编码器对所有患者均采用统一处理方式，忽略了医学代码的含义与预测价值高度依赖患者特定的临床情境和轨迹这一事实。从文本属性知识图谱学习患者个性化的概念表示面临两大挑战：(1) 如何为每个观察到的代码决定纳入多少KG上下文；(2) 如何将语义信息与患者特定的关系结构对齐。

Method: REFINE框架包含以下关键组件：首先从全局TKG构建患者特定的时序图；然后采用序列强化学习策略为每个观测代码选择个性化的、预算受限的KG扩展范围；随后利用异构图神经网络（GNN）处理患者图以捕获关系感知的结构依赖；最后使用冻结的LLM配合图感知的软提示来对概念表示进行语义精炼。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上的实验表明，REFINE一致性地提升了多种EHR骨干模型的性能，超越强基线方法，并在组件消融实验、知识图谱选择以及数据不足场景下均展现出稳健的性能提升。

Conclusion: REFINE通过将预算受限的KG扩展策略与LLM驱动的语义精炼相结合，成功实现了患者个性化的医学概念编码，证明了该框架在不同EHR任务和骨干模型上的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning rich medical concept representations is essential for EHR prediction. Text-attributed knowledge graphs (TKGs) provide a natural foundation by organizing heterogeneous medical relations together with textual semantics. However, most existing encoders process concepts uniformly across patients, despite the fact that a code's meaning and predictive value depend on patient-specific clinical context and trajectory. Learning patient-personalized concept representations from TKGs introduces two key challenges: (1) deciding how much KG context to incorporate for each observed code, and (2) aligning semantic information with the patient-specific relational structure. We propose REFINE, a KG-aware budgeted LLM graph refinement framework for patient-personalized medical concept encoding. Starting from a global TKG, REFINE constructs patient-specific temporal graphs. A sequential reinforcement learning policy selects a personalized KG expansion budget for each observed code. The resulting patient graph is processed by a heterogeneous GNN to capture relation-aware structural dependencies, while a frozen LLM uses graph-aware soft prompts to semantically refine concept representations. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV show that REFINE consistently improves diverse EHR backbones, outperforms strong baselines, and demonstrates robust gains across component ablation, KG selection, and data insufficiency.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond a Universal Forecasting Selector: Demand-Conditioned Model Selection across Demand Patterns and Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04425)
*Adolfo González*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预测模型选择器本身是否应被视为依赖于上下文的组件。通过比较五种选择机制（RMSSE、ERA、OWA、CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD），在24个优化预测模型、9个数据集、3种训练-测试划分及1至12个周期跨度的条件下进行评估。结果表明没有任何选择器在所有条件下占据主导地位，选择器的适用性随需求结构、数据可用性和预测跨度而变化，支持采用上下文依赖而非通用的预测模型选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构需求环境下预测模型选择仍然困难，因为最合适的决策规则可能随需求结构、数据可用性和预测跨度而变化。当前缺乏对选择机制本身是否应被视为上下文依赖组件的系统研究。

Method: 在24个优化预测模型、9个数据集、3种训练-测试划分以及1至12周期跨度的条件下，比较五种选择机制：RMSSE、ERA、OWA、CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD。使用事后Global Relative Accuracy（GRA）、统计检验和最佳可及模型参照来评估选择器性能。

Result: 没有任何选择器在所有条件下占据主导地位。CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD在平滑需求和若干不规则配置下更具竞争力，而OWA和ERA在间歇性和块状（Lumpy）需求场景表现更好。选择器的适用性随历史数据可用性和预测跨度而变化。

Conclusion: 预测模型选择器应当被视作上下文依赖的组件，而非采用一刀切（universal）的普适选择方法。选择机制的最优性取决于需求环境的具体特征。

Abstract: Forecasting-model selection remains difficult in heterogeneous demand because the most suitable decision rule may vary with demand structure, data availability, and forecasting horizon. This study examines whether the selector itself should be treated as a context-dependent component of the forecasting process. Five selection mechanisms - RMSSE, ERA, OWA, CCG-AHSC, and CCG-AHSCD - are compared across 24 optimized forecasting models, nine datasets, three training-testing partitions, and horizons from 1 to 12 cycles. Selector performance is evaluated ex post using Global Relative Accuracy (GRA), statistical tests, and a best-attainable-model reference. No selector dominates across all conditions. CCG-AHSC and CCG-AHSCD are more competitive for Smooth demand and several Erratic configurations, whereas OWA and ERA perform better in Intermittent and Lumpy settings. Selector suitability also changes with historical data availability and horizon, supporting a context-dependent rather than universal approach to forecasting-model selection.

</details>


### [107] [A Repeated-Measurement Study for Cultural Analytics of English Song Lyrics Using Five Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04428)
*E. Cho Smith,Samuel Ho,Dawn Laux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种大语言模型（LLM）作为零样本标注器，对英文歌曲歌词中四种社会建构（自尊、自我控制、寻求归属、寻求认可）进行标注的可靠性。通过重复标注分析发现，LLM标注在不同建构间可靠性并不一致：自尊的重复测量信度最强，寻求认可最不稳定，其余两者居中且依赖具体模型。下游分类实验表明共识标签包含可学习信号，但迁移性本身不能确立构念效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正被越来越多地用于在人类编码员无法实现的规模上标注文化文本，但其输出在作为潜在社会建构的测量之前，必须先验证这些测量的可靠性。当前缺乏系统性检验LLM作为零样本标注器在不同社会建构上的测量属性的研究。

Method: 研究设计了三个维度的可靠性检验：（1）重复运行的一致性——同一模型多次标注同一语料的稳定性；（2）跨模型收敛性——不同模型标注结果的一致性；（3）共识标签向监督分类的迁移性——用LLM共识标注训练分类器的可得性。研究对象为五个LLM对四种社会建构在英文歌词语料上的零样本标注。

Result: LLM测量在不同建构间并非均匀可靠。自尊在所有模型中展现出最强的重复测量信度；寻求认可普遍较不稳定；自我控制和寻求归属处于中间水平但依赖具体模型。下游分类显示共识LLM标签包含可学习的信号。

Conclusion: 重复测量稳定性和跨模型收敛性应在LLM标注被视为文化分析中的可规模化测量之前予以报告。LLM标注的迁移性本身并不确立构念效度，需谨慎对待其在文化分析中的应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate cultural texts at scales that are impractical for human coders. However, before their outputs are treated as measurements of latent social constructs, it is necessary to establish whether those measurements are reliable. This study evaluates five LLMs as zero-shot annotators of four social constructs expressed in English song lyrics: self-esteem, self-control, seeking belonging, and seeking recognition. Using repeated annotations of a large lyric corpus, we examine three properties of LLM-based measurement: consistency across repeated runs, convergence across models, and transferability of consensus labels to supervised classification. The findings show that LLM-based measurement is not uniformly reliable across constructs. Self-esteem exhibits the strongest repeated-measurement reliability across models, while seeking recognition is generally less stable; self-control and seeking belonging show intermediate but model-dependent reliability. Downstream classification further indicates that consensus LLM labels contain learnable signal, although transferability does not itself establish construct validity. Repeated-measurement stability and cross-model convergence should therefore be reported before LLM annotations are treated as scalable measurements in cultural analytics.

</details>


### [108] [Conformity Breaks Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04445)
*Yibo Hu,Hanyu Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示多智能体LLM系统中peer pressure会改变模型的评分机制，导致基于单独校准（clean calibration）的共形预测（conformal prediction）失效。在统一错误peer影响下，覆盖率从校准的90%跌至74%，且在低置信度子组上攻击者可将覆盖率从87%锐减至47%，并可能使系统在不确定时反而自信行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中，模型在独立回答时校准的置信度证书可能在受到peer影响后失效，因为模型的评分机制发生了改变而非问题分布改变，导致现有共形预测的可靠性保障被静默打破，需引起对该安全漏洞的关注。

Method: 通过实验对比模型单独回答与面对一致错误答案peer时的评分行为，在多个开放权重模型和多选题QA任务上评估共形预测覆盖率，并针对低置信度子组分析攻击者定向攻击的效果。该研究识别出peer pressure引发的"得分机制偏移"（score-mechanism shift），并以此解释共形预测在分布不变条件下仍失效的原因。

Result: 覆盖率在标准alpha=0.10操作点从校准的90%降至74%；对低置信度子组定向攻击时覆盖率从87%降至47%；决策层也受影响——本应升级（escalate）的系统可能变得自信而采纳攻击者的错误答案。标准共形修正方法无效，因为问题分布未变而评分行为已变。

Conclusion: 共形预测在单一模型上的校准不能迁移到多智能体交互场景；peer pressure导致的评分机制偏移构成新的安全威胁，需重新设计面向多智能体系统的校准与不确定性量化方法。

Abstract: A conformal certificate can be valid when an LLM answers alone and invalid when the same LLM sees peers that unanimously assert a wrong answer. The question is unchanged; the model's score for the correct answer changes. We call this a score-mechanism shift: clean calibration certifies how the model scores answers alone, but not how it scores them under peer pressure. We show that this shift silently breaks conformal prediction in multi-agent LLM systems. Across open-weight models and multiple-choice QA tasks, coverage falls from a calibrated 90% to 74% under unanimous-wrong peers at the standard alpha = 0.10 operating point. The average hides a sharper failure: by targeting the low-confidence items the certificate still covers, an attacker nearly halves coverage on that subgroup, from 87% to 47%, while the monitored average remains much higher. The failure also reaches the decision layer: a system that should escalate when uncertain can instead become confident enough to act on the attacker's wrong answer. Standard conformal fixes do not solve the problem, because the question distribution has not changed; the model's scoring behavior has.

</details>


### [109] [When Load-Balancing Goes Too Far: Expert Pruning in Over-Dispersed Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04453)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Connor Pryor,Sangwoo Cho,Supriyo Chakraborty,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Milind Naphade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Mixture-of-Experts(MoE)模型的专家剪枝问题，发现过分散路由（over-dispersed routing）状态下，路由器概率提供的专家重要性信号失效，导致困惑度无法预测下游任务准确度。作者提出Minimax Expert Score Allocation (MESA)方法，通过迭代提升当前受损害最严重领域所依赖专家的重要性评分，以最小化最坏领域性能下降而非平均准确率损失。在25%专家剪枝率下，MESA实现了跨领域最小的最坏情况性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家剪枝方法假设路由器概率能可靠反映专家重要性，但这一假设在过分散路由（由训练中激进负载均衡导致）下失效——此时token在各专家间近似均匀分布，重要性信号崩塌。作者观察到在此状态下困惑度无法预测下游任务准确度，剪枝还会暴露能力权衡问题（不同评分指标在不同领域各有优劣），因此需要一种领域感知的剪枝方法。

Method: 提出Minimax Expert Score Allocation (MESA)，一种领域感知的专家剪枝方法。其核心机制是迭代地提升当前受伤害最严重领域所依赖专家的重要性分数，通过最小化最坏领域退化（worst-case domain degradation）而非平均准确率来指导剪枝决策。

Result: 在gpt-oss-20B上发现过分散路由下困惑度与下游准确度脱钩；激活感知评分虽保留数学推理但使GPQA上的科学知识严重下降（18点差距），频率评分则表现相反。在25%剪枝率下，MESA实现最小的最坏领域退化，在11个基准中7个超越激活感知基线，并泛化到gpt-oss-120B、Gemma-4-26B-A4B和OLMoE-1B-7B。

Conclusion: 过分散路由构成一种与标准路由定性不同的剪枝机制，标准假设在其中失效。识别这一状态是对负载均衡MoE模型进行原则化专家剪枝的前提条件。MESA通过极小平坦损失实现跨领域的鲁棒剪枝，为过分散路由模型提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Expert pruning reduces the memory and serving cost of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models by removing low-importance experts identified by the router, assuming router probabilities provide a reliable importance signal. We observe that this assumption breaks down under over-dispersed routing, a regime associated with aggressive load-balancing during training, in which tokens are distributed nearly uniformly across experts and importance signals collapse. In this regime, perplexity does not predict downstream task accuracy: on gpt-oss-20B, the lowest-perplexity pruning configuration yields the worst mathematical reasoning, while the highest-perplexity configuration preserves it. This does not occur under standard routing (e.g., Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct), where perplexity and accuracy degrade together. Pruning under over-dispersed routing also exposes a capability trade-off in which no single scoring metric dominates: activation-aware scoring preserves mathematical reasoning but severely degrades knowledge-intensive science (an 18-point gap on GPQA), whereas frequency-based scoring exhibits the reverse. We propose Minimax Expert Score Allocation (MESA), a domain-aware method that iteratively boosts importance scores for experts serving whichever domain is currently worst-affected, minimizing worst-case domain degradation rather than average accuracy. At 25% expert pruning MESA achieves the smallest worst-case degradation across domains, outperforming activation-aware baselines on 7 of 11 benchmarks at a correspondingly reduced memory footprint, and it generalizes to gpt-oss-120B, Gemma-4-26B-A4B, and OLMoE-1B-7B. Our results indicate that over-dispersed routing is a qualitatively distinct pruning regime in which standard assumptions fail, and that recognizing it is a prerequisite for principled expert pruning of load-balanced MoE models.

</details>


### [110] [On-board ML for Trace Gas detection in Imaging Spectroscopy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04458)
*Vít Růžička,Adam Chlus,Andrew Thorpe,David R. Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在2026年3月东京野外活动中，利用边缘机器学习技术在飞机上实时处理AVIRIS-5成像光谱数据，首次实现了甲烷点源排放的机载实时检测，通过仅下行传输潜在事件的预测结果来克服通信瓶颈，大幅缩短了从检测到信息获取的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空基和天基成像光谱数据处理流程依赖地面上的慢速处理，导致事件检测到信息获取之间存在延迟，无法进行即时后续行动。由于通信带宽限制，完整数据立方体无法在飞行中即时下行传输，因此需要一种高效的机载处理方法来实现事件实时检测。

Method: 在飞行过程中利用配备AVIRIS-5传感器的平台进行机载成像光谱数据处理，采用一个高效且小型的机器学习模型（Edge ML）在机上预测潜在事件，仅将预测的事件结果下行传输，而非传输完整的数据立方体，从而绕开通信瓶颈。

Result: 在2026年3月的东京野外活动中，首次展示了利用成像光谱数据的边缘机器学习实现甲烷点源排放的机载实时检测。

Conclusion: 证明了基于边缘ML的机载处理方案可以显著缩短成像光谱数据中瞬态事件（如痕量气体排放）的检测与响应时间，为未来即时环境监测和快速干预行动铺平了道路。

Abstract: Data collected during aerial and spaceborne imaging spectroscopy campaigns enables the detection of transient events such as trace gas emissions. However, current processing pipelines depend on slow, on-the-ground processing, which delays the time to information of each detected event and prohibits immediate follow-up actions. During the Tokyo Field Campaign of March 2026, we explored on-board processing of Imaging Spectroscopy data from the equipped AVIRIS-5 sensor. Due to communication bottlenecks, full datacubes cannot be downlinked immediately during the flight. Instead we downlink the potential events predicted by our efficient and small machine learning model. We show the first on-board detection of methane point source emission with Imaging Spectroscopy data using Edge ML.

</details>


### [111] [Resilience Beyond Stationary Client Unavailability: Unlocking Efficient and Unbiased Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04763)
*Ming Xiang,Stratis Ioannidis,Edmund Yeh,Carlee Joe-Wong,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedSWE算法，用于联邦学习系统中客户端不可用性动态变化（异构且非平稳随机）的问题，通过补偿错过的计算、稳定并扩散全局更新、以及通过隐式gossiping均匀混合局部更新，在仅需少量额外内存和计算开销的情况下，证明其收敛到非凸目标的驻点并具备线性加速性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实联邦学习系统中客户端常为间歇性可用的边缘设备，客户端可用性动态高度复杂且参数服务器缺乏先验实时知识，传统联邦学习算法难以应对。复杂客户端可用性会引入显著偏差并损害模型性能，而现有工作或未考虑非平稳的客户端可用性动态，或需要大量的内存与计算开销。

Method: 提出FedSWE算法，包含三种新型算法结构：(i)补偿错过的计算（compensate for missed computations），(ii)稳定并跨轮扩散全局更新（stabilize and diffuse global updates），(iii)通过隐式gossiping均匀混合局部更新（evenly mix local updates），且无需知晓非平稳动态的先验信息。

Result: 相比标准FedAvg，FedSWE仅引入较轻的额外内存与计算开销。证明FedSWE在非凸目标下收敛到驻点，并在特定特殊情形下达到线性加速（linear speedup）性质。通过在真实数据集上对不同客户端不可用性动态的数值实验，验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: FedSWE在无需知晓非平稳客户端可用性动态的前提下，以轻量开销实现了对异构、非平稳随机客户端可用性的可证明鲁棒性，保住了收敛性与线性加速性质，为动态联邦学习环境提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Due to resource constraints or external and internal uncertainties, clients in real-world federated learning systems are often intermittently available edge devices. In highly dynamic environments, the parameter server lacks prior real-time knowledge of clients' availability, making it challenging to adapt traditional federated learning algorithms to be resilient to uncertainties in client availability. If not carefully addressed, complex client availability can introduce significant bias, potentially harming the performance of the trained model. Most prior work either fails to account for non-stationary client availability dynamics or demands significant memory and computational overhead. This paper aims to develop efficient federated learning algorithms that are provably resilient to heterogeneous and non-stationary stochastic client availability. We propose FedSWE, which admits novel algorithmic structures to (i) compensate for missed computations, (ii) stabilize and diffuse the global updates over rounds, and (iii) evenly mix the local updates through implicit gossiping, despite being agnostic to non-stationary dynamics. Compared with the standard FedAvg, FedSWE introduces light additional memory and computation overhead. We show that FedSWE converges to a stationary point of non-convex objectives while achieving the desired linear speedup property in certain special cases. We corroborate our analysis with numerical experiments over diversified client unavailability dynamics on real-world data sets.

</details>


### [112] [Nested Inductive Bias Framework for SPD Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04466)
*Tushar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种嵌套归纳偏置框架（Nested Inductive Bias），通过两阶段微分同胚组合将非欧几里得目标几何拉回到SPD流形上，构造曲率对齐的黎曼分类器，同时满足矩阵约束与数据潜在关系先验。此外还提出Rational Conformal Metric (RCM)用于向量化架构中对离群值的几何鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPD流形上的表示学习通常依赖如Log-Euclidean Metric等拉回欧氏度量来施加矩阵约束，但这类度量诱导平直几何，无法捕捉数据的固有关系先验。虽然Poincaré等度量被广泛用于诱导域对齐的关系先验，但将其从标准向量表示推广到SPD流形仍具挑战性。

Method: 引入Nested Inductive Bias框架，利用两阶段微分同胚组合将非欧氏目标几何形式化地拉回到SPD流形上，从而构建曲率对齐的黎曼分类器，同时尊重矩阵约束与数据的潜在关系几何。此外针对标准向量化架构，提出Rational Conformal Metric (RCM)，通过有界表示空间实现离群值鲁棒性。

Result: 在运动学与信号处理基准测试及合成实验上的经验评估表明，除非度量曲率与内在数据分布对齐，否则深度流形网络会出现类别可分性退化。RCM则在离群值鲁棒性方面达到了最先进的几何鲁棒性水平。

Conclusion: 度量曲率必须与数据的内在分布对齐才能保证深度流形网络的类别可分性；RCM作为向量化架构的几何度量，通过有界表示空间提供了对离群值的最先进鲁棒性。

Abstract: In Geometric Deep Learning, inductive biases serve two primary functions: enforcing manifold constraints and embedding relational priors. Currently, representation learning on SPD manifolds frequently relies on pullback Euclidean metrics, such as the Log-Euclidean Metric, to satisfy the former. While computationally efficient in avoiding domain boundary violations, these metrics induce a flat geometry that may fail to capture the intrinsic relational priors of datasets. While metrics such as the Poincaré metric are widely utilized to induce domain-aligned relational priors, generalizing them from standard vector representations to the SPD manifold has remained a challenge. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Nested Inductive Bias framework that utilizes a two-stage diffeomorphic composition to formally pull back non-Euclidean target geometries onto the SPD manifold. This framework enables the construction of curvature-aligned Riemannian classifiers that simultaneously respect matrix constraints and the latent relational geometry of the data. Empirical evaluations on kinematic and signal processing benchmarks, together with synthetic experiments, demonstrate that deep manifold networks experience degradation in class separability unless the metric curvature aligns with the intrinsic data distribution. Furthermore, for standard vectorized architectures, we propose the Rational Conformal Metric (RCM), designed to establish state-of-the-art geometric robustness against outliers by bounding the representation space.

</details>


### [113] [Hakken: Predicting future discoveries to fill the gaps in today's knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04494)
*Tarek R. Besold,Uchenna Akujuobi,Pablo Sanchez,Alessandra Toniato,Kana Maruyama,Jihun Choi,Samy Badreddine,Frederick Gifford,Daniel Evans-Yamamoto,Sucheendra K. Palaniappan,Miquel Ferrer,Kae Nagano,Iris Rossell,Tom Joy,Hatem ElShazly,Chrysa Iliopoulou,Christoph Wehner,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Susana Nunes,Pedro G. Cotovio,Peter Wurman,Peter Stone,Hiroaki Kitano,Michael Spranger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hakken，一个领域无关的知识预测与解释系统。它利用基于 transformer 的预测模型，结合时间序列知识图谱与 LLM 语义知识，预测科学概念间尚未被记录的关系及其类型，并调用模型无关的解释框架为每个预测提供依据。在生物医学领域验证中，Hakken 在时序多标签关系预测上建立了新基准，识别了 150 万条与衰老相关的高置信假说，其中两条药物相关预测（TP53-BAMBI、RAF1-TNF）经湿实验证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学发现依赖演绎推理，只能从已有知识的逻辑推论中产生，难以建立超出既有知识范畴的新关系。Hakken 旨在实现"知识预测"——即发现并非受限于先前知识演绎封闭集的新颖科学关系，从而加速科学知识的增长，并为科学家提供可解释的预测依据。

Method: Hakken 由三个核心组件构成：(1) 从大量研究出版物中提取时间序列知识图谱，构建基于 transformer 的预测模型；(2) 融合 LLM 的语义知识以增强对概念间关系的理解；(3) 预测尚未记录的关系是否存在并定义其类型，随后调用模型无关（model-agnostic）的解释框架为每个预测提供可评估的补充信息。系统设计为领域无关，但在生物医学领域进行了重点展示。

Result: 在生物医学领域，Hakken 的预测模型在时间感知多标签关系预测任务上建立了新基准，且在历史数据的长时间跨度上保持输出的一致性与信息量。系统生成了 150 万条超过置信阈值的与衰老相关的假说，经生物学家定性验证并推进三项进行湿实验验证。其中两个在药物发现与再利用方面具有潜在重要影响的预测得到确认，揭示了 TP53 与 BAMBI、RAF1 与 TNF 之间先前未被记录的相互作用。

Conclusion: Hakken 证明了 AI 驱动系统能够超越现有知识的演绎边界，自主提出新颖、可解释且可被实证验证的科学假说。通过与领域专家协作和湿实验验证，系统在加速科学发现方面展现出实际价值，尤其在新药发现与药物再利用等高风险场景中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: We present Hakken, a domain-agnostic prediction and explanation system performing knowledge prediction, i.e., growing scientific knowledge by establishing novel relationships, ones that are not limited to the deductive hull of previous knowledge. Hakken uses a transformer-based prediction model built on temporal sequences of knowledge graphs extracted from vast bodies of research publications, fused with an LLM's semantic knowledge, to predict the presence and define the type of as-yet undocumented relationships between scientific concepts. It then calls a model-agnostic explanation framework to provide accompanying information for each prediction that allows scientists to evaluate the suggested new relationship. While general purpose, we demonstrate Hakken's practical capabilities by applying it to the biomedical domain. There, Hakken's prediction model establishes a new benchmark for time-aware multi-label relation prediction, and we show that the model's output stays coherent and informative over extended time spans in historic data. In addition, we scored 1.5 million above-confidence-threshold hypotheses related to aging, qualitatively validated batches of these predictions with biologists and progressed three of them for empirical validation in wet-lab. Two predictions with potentially significant impact in the context of drug discovery and repurposing were confirmed, introducing previously undocumented interactions between TP53 and BAMBI, and between RAF1 and TNF, to biomedical science.

</details>


### [114] [Distilled Continuous Diffusion Language Models Can Write Code in Few Steps---or One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04531)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Kaiwen Zheng,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlaidQ，一个0.7B参数的连续扩散语言模型用于代码生成。通过将预训练自回归模型改造为连续嵌入上的双向去噪器，并用分布匹配（few-step）和成对轨迹监督（one-step）进行蒸馏，可将推理压缩到少数甚至单步去噪。16步学生模型在HumanEval和MBPP+上pass@10分别达31.78和40.49，超过512步采样的教师模型；单步蒸馏在HumanEval获7.07 pass@1，生成了功能正确的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言生成普遍被当作序列（自回归逐token生成）或长迭代轨迹（扩散模型）处理。本文动机在于探索能否将连续扩散语言模型的去噪轨迹激进蒸馏为极少步甚至单步，从而在不牺牲质量的前提下大幅加速代码生成，并验证连续扩散作为语言模型加速与蒸馏机制接口的通用性。

Method: 方法包括三部分：(1)将预训练自回归模型重利用为作用于连续token embedding的双向去噪器，构建PlaidQ；(2)采用分布匹配蒸馏实现few-step生成；(3)引入成对轨迹监督实现单步生成。通过连续扩散框架赋予语言模型借力连续扩散的加速与蒸馏机制。

Result: 与同规模离散扩散模型相比，PlaidQ在代码生成上竞争性相当。蒸馏显著改变质量-计算前沿：16步学生模型在HumanEval和MBPP+上分别达31.78和40.49 pass@10，超过512步采样的教师PlaidQ。极端的成对轨迹蒸馏在HumanEval上以单步去噪达到7.07 pass@1，可生成功能正确的程序。

Conclusion: 连续扩散为few-step和one-step代码生成提供了可行路径。连续扩散不仅是语言的另一种表示，更提供了一个接口，使语言模型能够继承连续扩散建模中的加速与蒸馏机制，在质量-计算权衡上取得突破。

Abstract: Language generation is almost universally treated as a sequential process: autoregressive models emit one token at a time, while diffusion language models replace token-level seriality with a long trajectory of iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce PlaidQ, a 0.7B continuous diffusion language model for code generation, and show that its trajectory can be aggressively distilled into only a few denoising steps---or even one, enabling efficient code generation. PlaidQ repurposes a pretrained autoregressive model as a bidirectional denoiser over continuous token embeddings. We distill PlaidQ with distribution matching for few-step generation and paired-trajectory supervision for one-step generation. At matched model scale, PlaidQ is competitive with discrete diffusion language models on code generation. Distillation then shifts the quality--compute frontier: a 16-step student reaches 31.78 and 40.49 pass@10 on HumanEval and MBPP+, surpassing the same PlaidQ teacher sampled for 512 steps. At the extreme, paired-trajectory distillation achieves 7.07 pass@1 on HumanEval with a single denoising step, producing functionally correct programs. Together, these results establish continuous diffusion as a viable path to few-step and one-step code generation. Broadly, continuous diffusion is not merely another representation for language: it provides an interface through which language models can inherit the acceleration and distillation machinery of continuous diffusion modeling. Training and inference code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/plaidq.

</details>


### [115] [Mitra-v2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04540)
*Yefan Tao,Xiyuan Zhang,Xinyi Liu,Boran Han,Danielle Maddix,Haoyang Fang,Zhen Han,Jiading Gai,Xuanqing Liu,Michael Bohlke-Schneider,Yuyang,Wang,Gerald Friedland,Kevan Mah,Chris Lee,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mitra-v2是一个基于合成数据训练的表格基础模型，参数量仅77M，却在TabArena和TALENT基准（300多个真实数据集）上达到行业级性能，可与1.6B参数的TabFM匹敌，并在分类和回归任务上大幅超越TabPFN-3，成为目前最强、最广泛适用的开源表格基础模型之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型在真实世界分类和回归任务上的性能仍有提升空间，且大型模型（如1.6B参数的TabFM）计算成本高昂。作者旨在构建一个在更广泛真实场景下达到前沿性能、同时保持小规模和高效性的开源表格基础模型。

Method: Mitra-v2基于小型2D Transformer骨干网络构建，仅在合成数据上训练，预训练分布比Mitra-v1更大更多样化。模型支持更长上下文和更大特征空间，并通过改进的优化方法从更大规模的任务分布中学习。

Result: 在完整TabArena基准上，Mitra-v2达到与行业级TabFM和EXAONE Tabular相当的最先进性能，在分类和回归上均大幅超越TabPFN-3。仅用77M参数（TabFM的5%）即匹敌1.6B参数的TabFM。在TALENT基准上保持领先，明确超越TabPFN-3和TabICLv2，并在超过十类的分类任务中排名第一（尽管只在最多十类的任务上预训练）。

Conclusion: Mitra-v2证明了小型表格基础模型通过更大更多样的合成数据预训练和优化改进，可以以极低计算成本达到行业级性能，是目前发布的最强且最广泛适用的开源表格基础模型之一。模型权重、推理与微调代码及评估结果均在Apache-2.0许可证下开源。

Abstract: We introduce Mitra-v2, a tabular foundation model that delivers state-of-the-art performance on real-world classification and regression problems, from credit-risk scoring and clinical prediction to equipment-failure detection and house-price estimation. Mitra-v2 is trained only on synthetic data, with a pretraining distribution that is much larger and more diverse than Mitra-v1's. Built on a small 2D Transformer backbone, Mitra-v2 supports longer contexts and larger feature spaces. Improved optimization lets it learn from this larger task distribution. We evaluate Mitra-v2 on the TabArena and TALENT benchmarks, comprising more than 300 real-world datasets under two evaluation protocols. On the full TabArena benchmark, Mitra-v2 delivers state-of-the-art performance at the level of the industry-scale TabFM and EXAONE Tabular models, while surpassing TabPFN-3 by a wide margin in both classification and regression. Mitra-v2 matches the 1.6B-parameter TabFM with only 5% of its size (77M parameters), delivering frontier performance at a fraction of the cost. On TALENT, Mitra-v2 remains among the leading models, clearly outperforming TabPFN-3 and TabICLv2. It also ranks first on classification tasks with more than ten classes, even though it was pretrained only on tasks with at most ten classes. These results make Mitra-v2 one of the strongest and most broadly applicable open tabular foundation models released to date. We release the model weights, the inference and fine-tuning code, and our evaluation results under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [116] [Fast Surrogate Modeling of Excitable and Oscillatory FitzHugh-Nagumo Dynamics with Parametric Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04549)
*Andrew Franck,Justin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNOs）作为FitzHugh-Nagumo（FHN）神经元模型的快速可微代理，利用FiLM机制将物理参数输入每个傅里叶层，分别在振荡和可激发两种动力学区域训练两个算子，实现了亚0.1%相对L2误差及近三个数量级的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHN模型模拟神经元电压动力学，探索其5D生理参数空间对神经调控以及将电压记录映射回生物物理参数具有重要意义，但传统有限差分求解器使得快速参数扫描代价高昂。

Method: 使用参数条件化傅里叶神经算子（FNOs）作为FHN电压场和恢复场的一维空间代理模型，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）将参数向量λ=(D_u,D_v,a,b,τ)调制到每个傅里叶层；通过分岔分析将参数空间划分为振荡（强直放电）和可激发（动作电位传播）两个区域，分别训练一个网络。

Result: 在振荡区域，代理模型在两个场上均达到亚0.1%的相对L2误差，运行速度比有限差分基线快约三个数量级，在参数空间上均匀泛化，且在训练边界之外能以低个位数百分比误差外推。在可激发区域，该算子准确再现了放电阈值和c∝√D_u传导速度定律，能复现完整的行波脉冲，充分捕获了可激发分岔结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的参数条件化FNO能够以极高的精度和速度充当FHN系统两个动力学区域的神经代理，不仅平滑插值场，还能捕获潜在的分岔结构，为快速参数扫描和将电压记录映射回生物物理参数提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The FitzHugh-Nagumo (FHN) system serves as a simplified model of neuronal voltage dynamics, capturing the activator-inhibitor structure behind both isolated action potentials and the rhythmic spiking seen across the brain. Exploring its 5D physiological parameter space is important for neuromodulation and mapping voltage recordings back to biophysics, yet classical finite-difference solvers make rapid parameter sweeps expensive. We train parameter-conditioned Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) as fast, differentiable surrogates for the FHN voltage and recovery fields on a one-dimensional spatial domain, conditioning each Fourier layer on the parameter vector $λ= (D_u, D_v, a, b, τ)$ via feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM). We apply a single bifurcation analysis that delimits the two distinct regimes the model spans, oscillatory (tonic firing) and excitable (action-potential propagation), and we train one operator in each. In the oscillatory regime the surrogate attains sub-$0.1\%$ relative $L^2$ error on both fields, runs nearly three orders of magnitude faster than the finite-difference baseline, generalizes uniformly across the parameter space, and extrapolates to low single-digit percentage errors outside of the training bounds. In the excitable regime the same operator accurately reproduces the firing threshold and the $c \propto \sqrt{D_u}$ conduction-velocity law and replicates full traveling pulses, fully capturing the excitable bifurcation structure rather than just smoothly interpolating fields.

</details>


### [117] [Training-Free Halving of Activated Experts in Fine-Grained Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04575)
*Xing Chen,Hengshuai Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究细粒度MoE模型在推理时降低top-k（专家激活数）的效果，发现标准重归一化会隐式影响专家分支的输出增益。作者通过解耦"激活专家数k1"与"重归一化的参考专家数k2"，引入一个无需训练和额外计算开销的整数参数，显著缓解了降k带来的性能损失：在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上从8降到4专家时，标准方法MMLU下降4.65点而k2=16时仅降0.35点；在11倍大的Qwen3.5-397B-A17B上也得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE模型在推理时减少专家激活数（降低k）是压缩计算的有效手段，但研究者发现标准重归一化会同时改变专家权重强度，导致性能急剧下降。现有方法无法分清"激活哪些专家"与"如何归一化"这两个独立因素的影响，因此需要一种解耦方法来隔离并缓解降k的性能损失。

Method: 作者提出解耦方案：激活top k1个专家，但使用top k2个专家的概率质量进行归一化（k2>k1）。该方法仅引入一个整数超参数k2，不增加参数、无需训练、无额外计算开销。通过在两种规模的Qwen3 MoE模型上对比不同k1/k2组合、有无重归一化、困惑度与下游准确率指标的一致性、以及专家身份与权重的重要性分析来系统验证。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上，标准重归一化将k从8降到4导致MMLU下降4.65点，而设k2=16时仅下降0.35点，同时路由专家计算量减半。该结果在11倍大的Qwen3.5-397B-A17B（k从10降到5）上复现，仅损失0.55点。完全去除重归一化会灾难性失败。此外发现困惑度与下游准确率偏好的k2不同，不能仅凭无标注文本选择压缩设置；专家身份比专家权重对性能影响更显著，均衡与领域专业化路由使专家剪枝空间有限。

Conclusion: MoE模型推理时降低k的性能损失主要来自重归一化改变专家分支强度而非专家选择本身，通过设定合适的参考概率质量k2可以有效补偿该损失。但k2的选取需根据下游任务而非困惑度来确定，且模型的专家剪枝潜力受限于路由机制本身。

Abstract: Modern fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a small number of experts and renormalize their router probabilities. We show that this renormalization implicitly calibrates expert output gain to the training top-$k$: reducing $k$ at inference changes not only which experts are used but also the strength of the expert branch. We separate these effects by activating the top $k_1$ experts while normalizing by the probability mass of the top $k_2$ experts, introducing one integer with no parameters, training, or measurable compute overhead. On Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, reducing from 8 to 4 experts causes a 4.65-point MMLU drop under standard renormalization but only 0.35 points with $k_2=16$, while halving routed-expert compute. The result replicates on the $11\times$ larger Qwen3.5-397B-A17B, where reducing from 10 to 5 experts loses only 0.55 points with an appropriate reference set. Removing renormalization entirely is catastrophic, showing that preserving a suitable reference mass is crucial. We further find that perplexity and downstream accuracy favor different $k_2$, cautioning against selecting MoE compression settings using unlabeled text alone. Analyses also show that expert identity matters substantially more than expert weighting, while balanced and domain-specialized routing leaves limited room for expert pruning.

</details>


### [118] [Optimizer Memory Schedules for Outscaling the Overtraining Axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04577)
*Katie Everett,Shikai Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了优化器在过训练（overtraining）轴上的扩展行为，发现优化的相对性能和最佳超参数会随训练时长发生显著变化。作者对比了四种优化器（AdamW、Muon、SOAP和ADANA）在51M到253M参数模型及1x到256x过训练因子下的表现，发现学习率调度偏好可以反转、最佳权重衰减系数约随sqrt(OT)增长，ADANA的缩放优势在超参数调优后依然存在，而Muon和SOAP则提供大致恒定的token效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化器评估通常在固定训练预算下进行，忽略了训练水平（过训练因子）对优化器相对表现的影响。本文旨在揭示训练时长的变化如何改变优化器的相对排名和最佳超参数选择，从而将训练时程确立为优化器评估与设计的必要维度。

Method: 系统性地在过训练因子1x到256x范围内训练51M至253M参数的模型，对每个设置扫描基础学习率。比较AdamW、两种矩阵预条件方法（Muon和SOAP）以及动量调度方法（ADANA），并引入对数时间权重衰减和动量冷却技术，基于DANA理论进行缩放优势分析。

Result: 学习率调度偏好在过训练轴上可发生反转；最佳权重衰减系数约随sqrt(OT)缩放；ADANA的缩放优势经AdamW固定记忆单独调优后依然成立，且远超过AdamW的指数优势接近DANA理论预测；Muon和SOAP提供大致恒定的token效率优势；ADANA在最高的过训练因子下超越Muon并与SOAP竞争。

Conclusion: 训练时程应被视为优化器评估和设计中不可忽视的关键轴。不同优化器在不同过训练水平下的相对排名变化显著，ADANA适合长时间训练场景，而矩阵预条件方法提供稳定的中期优势。

Abstract: We investigate how optimizers scale across the overtraining axis and show that relative optimizer performance and optimal hyperparameters change substantially with training horizon. In particular, we study how matrix-preconditioned methods (Muon and SOAP) and a momentum-scheduled method (ADANA) scale relative to AdamW. We compare these four optimizers across models from 51M to 253M parameters and overtraining (OT) factors from 1x to 256x, sweeping the base learning rate at every setting. The preferred learning rate schedule can reverse across the overtraining axis, the best weight decay coefficient scales approximately as sqrt(OT), and longer horizons generally favor longer fixed memory. ADANA's scaling advantage over AdamW persists after tuning AdamW's fixed memory separately at each horizon. Log-time weight decay and momentum cooldown provide substantial gains for ADANA that compound as training increases. With this treatment, ADANA outscales AdamW with an exponent advantage close to that predicted by DANA theory on power-law random features. Muon and SOAP instead provide roughly constant token-efficiency advantages over AdamW across most of the measured range, although SOAP may gain further at the highest overtraining factors. ADANA begins behind both matrix-preconditioned optimizers but closes the gaps as training increases, surpassing Muon and becoming competitive with SOAP at our highest OT factors. These results establish training horizon as an essential axis for optimizer evaluation and design.

</details>


### [119] [Representation Redundancy and Structural Complexity in Finite-Field Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04583)
*Zheng Zhang,Na Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域 F_{2^n} 上乘法逆元运算在不同有序 F_2-基表示下的代数结构与学习难度变化。证明两个有序基诱导相同坐标逆映射当且仅当它们属于同一 Galois 轨道，而每个轨道大小为 n，因此有序基到不同逆映射的对应关系恰好是 n 对一。随后分析三种布尔形式的逆运算：参考形式代数度 n-1 且联合 ANF 跳为 1，混合表示形式度为 2(n-1) 且联合 ANF 跳为 2，完整原始形式度至多 3(n-1) 且联合 ANF 跳至少 n。穷举计算与理论一致，多层感知机实验显示学习难度遵循相同排序，而 Galois 轨道冗余在测试条件下仅提供有限的泛化收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数学运算的表示选择如何影响其代数形式和经验学习难度，特别是在 F_{2^n} 上求逆运算的本原性（primitive）较难问题。理解表示冗余（Galois 轨道）与布尔结构变化之间的关系，对神经网络的表示学习和密码学中的布尔函数设计均有意义。

Method: 理论证明方面，作者证明了两个有序基诱导相同坐标逆映射当且仅当属于同一 Galois 轨道，轨道大小为 n，得出 n 对一的对应关系。接着对三种布尔公式化方案（参考形式、混合表示形式、完整原始形式）分析其代数度和联合 ANF 跳（joint ANF leap）等度量，并通过穷举计算验证理论结果与界限，最后用多层感知机进行受控实验比较学习难度。

Result: 理论分析确定了三种公式化方案的代数度分别为 n-1、2(n-1)、至多 3(n-1)，联合 ANF 跳分别为 1、2、至少 n。穷举计算与理论结果一致。MLP 受控实验显示学习难度与理论排序一致（即代数度和 ANF 跳越大的公式更难学习），Galois 轨道冗余在测试条件下只能提供有限的泛化收益。

Conclusion: 表示中的精确冗余（Galois 轨道）可以与布尔结构和学习行为的变化共存，当表示作为输入的一部分被暴露时尤其如此。这表明在研究表示学习问题时，不仅需要考虑表示之间的等价性，还需考虑表示在代数结构和学习难度上的差异。

Abstract: The representation chosen for a mathematical operation can affect both its algebraic form and its empirical learning difficulty. We study this phenomenon for inversion over \(\mathbb F_{2^n}\), with field elements expressed in varying ordered \(\mathbb F_2\)-bases. We prove that two ordered bases induce the same coordinate inversion map if and only if they belong to the same Galois orbit. Since every orbit has size \(n\), the correspondence between ordered bases and distinct inversion maps is exactly \(n\)-to-one. We then analyze three Boolean formulations of inversion. The reference formulation has algebraic degree \(n-1\) and joint ANF leap \(1\), the mixed representation formulation has degree \(2(n-1)\) and joint ANF leap \(2\), and the complete raw formulation has degree at most \(3(n-1)\) and joint ANF leap at least \(n\). Exhaustive computations agree with the theoretical results and bounds in the cases considered. Controlled experiments with multilayer perceptrons show the same ordering in learning difficulty, while Galois orbit redundancy provides only a limited generalization benefit under the tested conditions. These results show that exact redundancy among representations can coexist with changes in Boolean structure and learning behavior when the representation is exposed as part of the input.

</details>


### [120] [GNN-Guided Graph Coarsening and Adaptive QUBO Penalties for the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows on a Quantum Annealer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04593)
*Youssef Kamel Rezk,Paweł Gora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究图粗化（graph coarsening）在量子退火求解带时间窗的容量约束车辆路径问题（CVRPTW）中的应用。作者通过自适应惩罚校准将原始约束违反数从33.0降至0.06，并用图神经网络（GNN）替代手工调参的合并评分，在N=10时跨所有Solomon族实现100%可行性。硬件实验在固定逻辑变量数下将可行样本从0.02%提升至39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图粗化的CVRPTW求解方法需要针对特定数据族手动调参且不可靠，尤其在随机实例上表现不佳。作者希望克服这一局限，使量子退火求解在不同家族上均能稳定可行。

Method: (1) 自适应惩罚校准：移除非绑定约束、归一化绑定约束并缩放剩余惩罚，控制QUBO内部系数范围；(2) 用图神经网络GNN替代手工设计的合并评分函数，单一配置跨所有Solomon家族使用；(3) 在模拟退火和D-Wave Advantage2处理器上验证，并用带局部搜索的经典修复作为基线。

Result: 惩罚校准使平均原始约束违反从33.0降至0.06（p=3.7e-11）；GNN在N=10时跨家族达成100%可行性（对比手工调参的80%），N=10-100范围内可行性83% vs 69%；N=80,100时差异显著（p=0.002）。硬件实验中可行样本从0.02%提升至39%。

Conclusion: 约束条件的内部系数范围（conditioning）比惩罚规模或问题尺寸更关键；GNN粗化可提供跨家族泛化的统一策略，而经典局部搜索修复仍是端到端求解成本上的参照基准。

Abstract: Graph coarsening reduces the large Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) formulations arising when vehicle-routing problems are solved by quantum annealing. Nearby customers with compatible time windows are merged into super-nodes, the reduced problem is solved, and the solution is expanded to the original graph. For the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW), existing coarsening heuristics require family-specific tuning and remain unreliable on random instances. We address these limitations on the Solomon benchmark using simulated annealing and a D-Wave Advantage2 processor.
  We first introduce adaptive penalty calibration. Uniform penalty scaling has little effect, whereas controlling the internal coefficient range substantially improves raw samples. Removing non-binding constraints, normalising binding ones, and scaling the remaining penalties reduces mean raw constraint violations from 33.0 to 0.06 at the same solver budget (p=3.7e-11, n=56). A variable-count-preserving control attributes this gain to conditioning rather than problem size.
  Second, we replace the hand-tuned merge score with a graph neural network (GNN) using one configuration across all families. At N=10, it achieves 100% feasibility across all Solomon families, including R-type (100% vs. 80% for the tuned heuristic). Across N=10,...,100, feasibility is 83% vs. 69%, with the GNN better or tied on 85/90 instance-size pairs. At N=80,100, the difference is significant (p=0.002; 25/25 pairs), while the QUBO remains approximately 5-6 times smaller.
  Finally, hardware experiments reproduce the conditioning effect at fixed logical variable count: feasible samples increase from 0.02% to 39% across 13 instances. Classical repair with local search remains a reference bound for end-to-end solution cost.

</details>


### [121] [Too Rare to Learn: Prescribed Cyclone Tracks Degrade a Bay of Bengal Ocean Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04635)
*Sumaiya Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在孟加拉湾区域海洋预报中，向U-Net网络显式注入气旋轨道作为预设输入反而有害。相比纯海洋模型，气旋条件模型的技能在所有种子和风暴测试中均表现更差，原因是气旋通道在训练数据中仅7.9%的日期非零，导致激活时超出分布，网络学会了对罕见信号的自信错误响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经海洋模拟器正被用于气旋暴露沿海海域的区域预报，一种自然的网络设计选择是将气旋作为预设输入传给网络。作者希望验证这一设计选择是否合理，即在孟加拉湾背景下测试'给网络提供显式气旋输入'是否会提升预报性能。
