Method: 从GLORYS12再分析数据中扣留15个完整气旋（强度65-150kt），构建两个仅在4个预设气旋轨道通道上不同的U-Net模型。对比三种子下的气旋条件模型与纯海洋模型的预报技能；通过配对检验比较均方根技能与持久性预报；通过将推断时真实气旋图替换为无风暴图来诊断误差来源。

Result: 纯海洋模型在每一次运行中都优于持久性预报，而气旋条件模型在每一次运行中都输给持久性预报，两组技能范围不相交（p=3.1e-5）。额外误差落在预设风暴影响区域内。在推断时将真实气旋图替换为无风暴图，使扣留风暴预报在每个种子下改善7.5%至16.4%。

Conclusion: 气旋条件模型的失败源于暴露频率而非信号内容：由于气旋通道在仅7.9%的训练日期非零，这些通道大多数时间处于分布外状态，一旦激活即超出分布。网络实际学到的是对罕见信号的自信但错误的响应。该结果表明，在气旋预报中显式引入气旋输入可能不是合理的神经网络设计选择。

Abstract: Neural ocean emulators are being proposed for regional forecasting in cyclone-exposed coastal seas, and a natural design choice is to hand the network the cyclone as a prescribed input. We test that choice in the Bay of Bengal and find it harmful. We withhold 15 whole cyclones spanning 65 to 150 kt from GLORYS12 reanalysis and compare two U-Nets that are identical except for four prescribed cyclone-track channels. Across three seeds the ocean-only model beats persistence in every run and the storm-conditioned model loses to it in every run, with the two skill ranges disjoint (p = 3.1e-5, paired across storms). The cause is exposure frequency rather than signal content: the channels are non-zero on only 7.9% of training days, so they are out of distribution the moment they activate. The extra error falls inside the prescribed storm footprint, and replacing the real cyclone map with a no-storm map at inference improves held-out storm forecasts by 7.5 to 16.4% in every seed. The conditioned network has learned a response to a rare signal that is confidently wrong.

</details>


### [122] [SMILE: Bridging Continuous Optimization and Discrete Symbolic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04639)
*Mansooreh Montazerin,Antonio Ortega,Ajitesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMILE（Sine, Multiplication, Identity, Logarithm, Exponential）混合框架，将连续梯度优化与离散符号恢复统一起来，通过结构分析、连续优化和符号恢复三阶段来发现数学表达式。在SRBench基准上，SMILE在最大噪声水平下达到最高的符号解法率，并始终位于精度-复杂度Pareto前沿上，能以极短时间恢复更简洁的表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法在组合搜索空间中收敛缓慢，且缺乏利用数据中组合结构的机制，导致效率和鲁棒性不足。作者希望通过融合连续优化与离散符号方法来解决这些问题。

Method: SMILE采用三阶段混合框架：(1) 对数据进行结构分析以识别目标表达式的组合层次结构；(2) 使用具有可解释激活函数的网络进行连续梯度优化以学习编码目标表达式的参数；(3) 通过结构化剪枝、系数优化和舍入进行符号恢复，将学到的网络蒸馏为紧凑且具有精确符号常数的表达式。

Result: 在SRBench的地面真值和黑盒数据集上，SMILE在最大噪声水平下取得最高的符号解法率，展现出竞争对手方法大幅退化时的强鲁棒性。SMILE一致地位于精度-复杂度Pareto前沿上，能以远少于竞争方法的时间恢复显著更简洁的表达式。消融实验验证了各组件的作用。

Conclusion: SMILE验证了将连续优化与符号恢复相结合的混合策略在符号回归中的有效性，尤其在噪声环境下表现出显著的鲁棒性和效率优势，能在保持高精度的同时输出更加简洁、可解释的表达式。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) discovers closed-form mathematical expressions from data, offering interpretability beyond black-box models. Existing methods suffer from slow convergence in combinatorial search spaces and lack mechanisms to exploit compositional structure in the data. We introduce SMILE (Sine, Multiplication, Identity, Logarithm, Exponential), a hybrid framework that unifies continuous gradient-based optimization with discrete symbolic recovery through three stages: structural analysis of the data to identify the compositional hierarchy of the target expression, continuous optimization to learn parameters of a network that encodes the target expression using interpretable activations, and symbolic recovery through structured pruning, coefficient optimization, and rounding. This final stage distills the learned network into a compact expression with exact symbolic constants. We evaluate SMILE on SRBench across ground-truth and black-box datasets, with ablation studies validating each component. SMILE achieves the highest symbolic solution rate at the largest noise levels, demonstrating strong robustness where competing methods degrade substantially. It consistently lies on the Pareto front of accuracy versus complexity, recovering significantly simpler expressions in a fraction of the time required by the competing methods.

</details>


### [123] [WEECFP-SuRGE: Wide Embedded Extended Connectivity Fingerprint with Substructure Rotary Graph-distance Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04672)
*Robert Epps*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WEECFP（一种无参数、1024维的连续分子指纹）和WEECFP-SuRGE（一种基于自注意力的Transformer架构），在TDC ADMET榜单上实现平均回归排名最低，成为无外部预训练方法中的第一名，并在多项指标上超越经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子表征方法（如经典指纹和预训练模型）在性能、参数复杂度和外部预训练依赖方面存在局限，作者希望设计一种无需外部预训练、参数自由的连续分子指纹表达，并配合轻量级Transformer架构，以在药物ADMET和性质预测任务上取得领先性能。

Method: WEECFP将每个Morgan子结构散射到单一向量的约32个带符号位置，生成1024维连续指纹；WEECFP-SuRGE在Transformer的自注意力中引入SuRGE（以分子最短路径图距离参数化的类RoPE旋转编码）作用于WEECFP子结构token。此外提出三参考最远优先编码来实现O(S)位置内存的近似图距离编码。

Result: 7模型集成的WEECFP-SuRGE在TDC ADMET排行榜取得平均回归最低排名、总体排名第二（仅次于预训练的MapLight+GNN），在无外部预训练方法中排名第一；在Pgp、Lipophilicity等5项基准获得#1。在MoleculeNet上于ESOL、Lipophilicity、QM9三项回归任务超越所有经典指纹基线。token化近乎无损：贪心重叠重建恢复99.9%分布内分子的规范SMILES，图距离编码皮尔逊相关r=0.901。

Conclusion: WEECFP的token化方案几乎无损，配合SuRGE距离编码的Transformer无需外部预训练即可在多项ADMET与性质预测任务中达到领先水平，展示了参数自由指纹+轻量架构作为外部预训练方法替代方案的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce WEECFP, a parameter-free 1024-dimensional continuous molecular fingerprint that scatters each Morgan substructure across roughly thirty-two signed positions of a single vector, and WEECFP-SuRGE, a transformer architecture whose self-attention applies SuRGE (Substructure Rotary Graph-distance Encoding) -- a RoPE-like rotation parameterized by molecular shortest-path graph distance -- to WEECFP substructure tokens. A 7-model blend of this architecture (the WEECFP-SuRGE Blend) achieves the lowest average regression rank on the TDC ADMET leaderboard; is #2 overall on the TDC ADMET leaderboard (behind only pretrained MapLight+GNN), and is #1 overall among methods that use no external pretraining; takes leaderboard #1 finishes on Pgp, Lipophilicity, CYP2D6 Substrate, Clearance Microsome, and LD50 (with the WEECFP-NoSuRGE Blend separately reaching #1 on HIA) across the full 22-benchmark suite -- without any external pretraining. On MoleculeNet, WEECFP-SuRGE beats every classical-fingerprint baseline on 3 of 4 regression tasks (ESOL, Lipophilicity, QM9). We further show that WEECFP tokenization is near-lossless: a greedy overlap reconstruction recovers the exact canonical SMILES of 99.9% of in-distribution molecules across 9 MoleculeNet datasets and 98.93% of molecules in a cross-dataset holdout (HIV->Lipophilicity), and that a three-reference farthest-first encoding of graph distance correlates at Pearson r = 0.901 with the true pairwise distance, enabling O(S) positional memory at matching accuracy.

</details>


### [124] [Simulation-free Unbalanced Dynamic Optimal Transport with General Growth Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04710)
*Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Bowen Yang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SUDO（Simulation-free Unbalanced Dynamic Optimal Transport），一种免模拟的不平衡动态最优传输框架，支持一般非二次凸增长惩罚，无需昂贵的NeuralODE模拟或仅限WFR测地线的解析解，即可高效推断细胞动态并匹配WFR基准精度，同时支持编码增殖主导先验的非对称惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从不成对的单细胞快照中推断细胞动态，需要对状态转换和群体增殖/死亡进行联合建模。UDOT通过沿传输路径惩罚增长来处理此问题，但现有求解器要么依赖昂贵的NeuralODE模拟，要么受限于二次惩罚（WFR测地线）的解析解。因此需要一种高效且通用的UDOT求解器，支持一般增长惩罚。

Method: 首先证明凹增长惩罚会导致退化解（增长与传输分离）；随后提出SUDO框架：学习条件路径与传输代价并求解诱导的半耦合问题，然后利用不平衡流匹配（unbalanced flow matching）实现免模拟求解。该方法支持一般非二次凸增长惩罚。

Result: 在WFR基准上，SUDO的准确度与高效的解析解算法相当，计算速度优于基于模拟的方法；在WFR之外的场景，SUDO支持编码增殖主导先验的非对称惩罚，在合成数据与单细胞数据上产生更合理的轨迹和增长估计。

Conclusion: SUDO为一般增长惩罚下的UDOT提供了免模拟的高效求解框架，拓宽了生物学先验（如增殖/凋亡）的编码方式，在精度和计算效率上均表现出优势。

Abstract: Inferring cellular dynamics from unpaired single-cell snapshots requires modeling both state transitions and population growth or death. Unbalanced dynamic optimal transport (UDOT) addresses this by penalizing growth along transport paths, making the choice of growth penalty a key way to encode biological priors on proliferation and apoptosis. However, existing UDOT solvers either rely on computationally expensive NeuralODE simulations or depend on analytical solutions of conditional paths, restricting their efficiency solely to quadratic penalties, i.e. Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao (WFR) geodesics. To enable an efficient UDOT solver for general growth penalties, we first show that concave growth penalties lead to degenerate solutions where growth and transport are separated. We then introduce \textbf{S}imulation-free \textbf{U}nbalanced \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{O}ptimal transport (SUDO), a simulation-free framework for UDOT with general non-quadratic convex growth penalties. SUDO learns the conditional paths and transport costs, solves the induced semi-coupling problem, and subsequently leverages unbalanced flow matching to achieve a simulation-free solution. On WFR benchmarks, SUDO matches the accuracy of efficient, analytical solution-driven algorithms while outperforming simulation-based methods in computational speed. Beyond WFR, SUDO supports asymmetric penalties that encode proliferation-dominant priors and produce more plausible trajectories and growth estimates on synthetic and single-cell datasets.

</details>


### [125] [Locating and Steering Refusal Beyond Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04721)
*Preethi Carmel Bosco,Gopalakrishnan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"拒绝"(refusal)表征是否在不同架构（Transformer、SSM、递归、混合）间保持一致。作者发现，通过单一刚性旋转即可对齐不同模型的表征空间，表明拒绝方向在不同架构间是共享的。架构差异不在于方向被"引导"到哪里，而在于它必须在何处被"读取"——即在残差流的"写入位"（每层输出）处。基于此方向的探测器和门控干预能有效降低四种架构上的越狱成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明Transformer中拒绝行为由残差流中的一个单一方向控制，且安全与可解释性工具已依赖此发现。但SSM（状态空间模型）通过递归更新而非注意力路由信息，与Transformer没有共享的token混合机制。核心问题在于：这种安全表征是否在不同架构间存留，还是必须逐架构重新发现。

Method: 使用刚性旋转（rigid rotation，只能重定向空间不能重塑）对齐不同模型的表征空间以验证方向共享性；用Transformer训练的有害性探针检测SSM的有害输入；通过在残差流中添加或移除对齐方向进行干预实验，并设置保持干预强度固定的对照组以区分"估计位置"与"应用位置"的影响；采用检测器触发门控方式在SSM、Transformer、递归和混合四类架构中测试越狱防御。

Result: 拒绝方向在架构间共享，单一刚性旋转即可实现表征对齐。基于Transformer训练的harm探针能识别SSM的有害输入，移除对齐方向会使模型回应本应拒绝的攻击，而同等大小的随机方向影响远小。重要的是，决定效果的是方向被估计的位置（读位，即写入位）而非应用位置。门控干预在全部四类架构中降低了越狱成功率，在SSM上能抵御根据防御而调优的攻击者。对照实验表明跨架构转移的是方向本身而非防御强度。

Conclusion: 基于拒绝的安全工具可以跨架构移植：只需在新架构的"写入位"处重新估计方向，而无需重建整个方法。架构特异性不在于表征本身，而在于读取表征的管线上位置。

Abstract: Where inside a language model does refusal live, and does that place change when the architecture does? In a transformer, refusal is governed by a single direction in the residual stream, a finding that safety and interpretability tooling now depend on. State-space models (SSMs) route information through a recurrent update instead of attention, sharing no token-mixing mechanism with a transformer. Does the same safety representation survive this shift, or must it be rediscovered per architecture? It survives. A single rigid rotation, which can only reorient a space and not reshape it, aligns one model's representation space with another's, so the two genuinely share the representation. A harm probe trained on a transformer then flags an SSM's harmful inputs, and removing the aligned direction makes a model answer attacks it would otherwise refuse, while a random direction of the same size does far less. What is architecture-specific is not where the direction is steered but where it must be read. Each layer computes a fresh output that is then added into the residual stream, and harm is cleanly readable at this output, the write site, before the addition. A control that holds the intervention's strength fixed shows that what matters is where the direction is estimated, not where it is applied. Applied through a detector-triggered gate, this direction lowers jailbreak success in all four architecture families we test (SSM, transformer, recurrent, hybrid), and on the SSM it holds against an attacker that tunes its prompt against the defense. The gate only matches a trivial rule that returns a fixed refusal whenever the same detector fires, so what transfers across architectures is the direction itself, not defense strength. Safety tooling built on refusal therefore ports to a new architecture by re-estimating the direction at that architecture's write site, not by rebuilding it.

</details>


### [126] [Training Large Language Models for Small-Molecule Design with Synthetic Task Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04735)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,Zichen Wang,Patricia Suriana,Bodhi Vani,Kirill Shmilovich,Kangway Chuang,Colin Grambow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用大规模语言模型（LLMs）在化学空间中设计可行的候选药物。由于许多化学评分函数评估成本高昂（每次数小时至数天），无法直接在在线训练中使用。作者探索了LLMs是否能从更廉价的合成任务中学习分子设计策略，并将其泛化到昂贵的分子先导化合物优化场景中。研究发现，基于课程学习的训练策略（逐步引入更具挑战性的合成设计任务）能使模型性能超越更大的前沿模型在基于结构的先导化合物优化上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物设计需要在组合爆炸且崎岖不平的化学空间中搜索满足多个（通常相互竞争的）目标的分子。LLMs具备强大的表征能力、推理能力和灵活性，可作为生成先验。虽然基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）可提升LLM能力，但许多化学相关的评分函数评估成本极高（每次需要数小时甚至数天），直接用于在线训练过于昂贵，因此需要寻找替代训练策略。

Method: 作者提出使用基于课程学习的训练策略（curriculum-based training recipes），通过逐步引入更复杂的合成分子设计任务来进行后训练，使模型从廉价合成任务中学习设计策略，然后泛化迁移到昂贵的药物先导化合物优化场景中。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于课程学习的训练方案能使模型性能在结构-based先导化合物优化任务上超越更大的前沿模型。这说明通过合成任务进行后训练扩展（scaling post-training using synthetic tasks）是有效的策略。

Conclusion: 利用合成任务进行课程化后训练是使LLMs适应高成本实验场景的有效策略，这些场景中直接训练太昂贵而不可行。这为将LLMs应用于实际药物设计提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Designing viable drug candidates requires searching a combinatorially large and rugged chemical space for molecules that satisfy multiple, often competing, objectives. Large language models (LLMs) provide a useful generative prior for this problem because of their representational capacity, reasoning ability, and flexibility when incorporating information from the external environment. While reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) can be used to improve the capabilities of LLMs, many chemically relevant scoring functions require hours or even days per evaluation, making them prohibitively expensive to use directly during online training. Here, we investigate whether LLMs can learn molecular design strategies from cheaper synthetic tasks that generalize to expensive molecular lead optimization settings. We find that curriculum-based training recipes that gradually incorporate more challenging synthetic design tasks enable strong performance that surpasses that of much larger frontier models on structure-based lead optimization. Our results suggest that scaling post-training using synthetic tasks is an effective strategy for adapting LLMs to high-cost experimental scenarios that are too expensive to directly train on.

</details>


### [127] [A Fairness Audit of the Duckworth-Lewis-Stern Method: Format-Specific and Gender-Differential Bias, with an Interpretable Calibration Layer for Cricket Target Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04754)
*Soumyadeep Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对DLS方法进行了迄今最大规模的实证审计，基于8,150场国际板球比赛和233,550个合成中断场景，发现DLS存在两处系统性偏差（跨比赛状态137分范围误差和性别差异偏差），并提出了轻量级可解释校准层DLS-Cal，将绝对偏差在ODI降低31%、T20I降低19%，性别感知变体可将女子ODI残余偏差从+6.19降至+0.65分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLS方法自1999年以来一直是板球雨中断目标分数修订的国际标准，运营使用超过20年，但从未有大规模实证审计评估其预测偏差。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统量化DLS的结构性偏差并探索改进方案。

Method: 使用Cricsheet的8,150场国际比赛（3,095场ODI、5,055场T20I）数据，通过时间分割生成233,550个合成中断场景。对DLS预测误差按（剩余轮数、失分wicket数）分桶分析，并对五种现代替代模型（Bi-LSTM、XGBoost、增强XGBoost、深度上下文感知模型、堆叠集成）进行基准比较。提出DLS-Cal，一个仅27K参数的轻量级可解释校准层，输出状态条件修正项加到DLS预测上，并开发性别感知变体。

Result: DLS预测误差在比赛状态桶中跨越137分范围；在训练分割上，男子ODI平均过度预测+1.51分，女子为+7.63分，性别差距+6.13分（F=195.16，p<10^-43）。DLS-Cal在ODI上减少绝对偏差31%，在T20I上减少19%；性别感知变体将女子ODI残余偏差从+6.19降至+0.65分，同时不影响男子校准效果。

Conclusion: DLS方法存在此前未量化的大规模和性别差异化预测偏差，DLS-Cal作为轻量级、可解释的校准层能有效修正这些偏差，研究者已发布代码、模型和数据以支持复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: The Duckworth-Lewis-Stern (DLS) method has been the international standard for revising target scores in rain-interrupted limited-overs cricket since 1999. Despite over two decades of operational use, no large-scale empirical audit of its prediction bias has been published. We conduct such an audit on 8,150 international matches (3,095 ODIs, 5,055 T20Is) from Cricsheet, generating 233,550 synthetic interruption scenarios with temporal splits. We document two structured biases. First, DLS prediction error spans a 137-run range across (overs-remaining, wickets-lost) match-state buckets. Second, DLS exhibits a gender-differential bias on ODIs that has not previously been quantified: on the training split, mean over-prediction is +1.51 runs for men but +7.63 runs for women, a gap of +6.13 runs (F = 195.16, p < 10^-43). We benchmark DLS against five modern alternatives: Bi-LSTM, XGBoost, an enriched XGBoost variant, a deep context-aware model, and a stacking ensemble, and propose DLS-Cal, a lightweight interpretable calibration layer (27K parameters) outputting a state-conditioned correction added to DLS. DLS-Cal reduces absolute bias by 31% on ODI and 19% on T20I, and a gender-aware variant reduces women's ODI residual bias from +6.19 to +0.65 runs while leaving men's calibration unchanged. We release code, models, and data.

</details>


### [128] [A Robust Watermark-based Fingerprint Framework for GNNs Ownership Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04772)
*Han Zhang,Yan Wang,Guanfeng Liu,Pengfei Ding,Huaxiong Wang,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REMARK是一个面向图神经网络（GNN）的鲁棒所有权验证框架，通过生成分布内（in-distribution）水印图来最大化不同GNN模型间的输出差异，并从中提取鲁棒指纹用于模型所有权验证，克服了现有水印和指纹方法的三大局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN训练成本高昂，模型所有权被侵犯（如模型窃取和未授权滥用）的问题日益严重。现有两类GNN所有权验证方法（基于水印和基于指纹）存在三个关键缺陷：(1) 分布外水印图会导致被保护模型性能退化；(2) 不切实际地假设替代模型在含水印训练集上训练；(3) 指纹提取过度依赖特定输出层次。

Method: REMARK框架包含两个核心步骤：（1）精心生成分布内（in-distribution）水印图，这些图能够最大化不同GNN模型之间的输出差异，从而避免分布外数据导致的模型性能退化；（2）从模型输出差异中提取鲁棒指纹用于GNN所有权验证，从而无需假设替代模型必须在水印数据集上训练或依赖特定输出层次。

Result: 在广泛使用的真实世界数据集和多种GNN架构上的大量实验表明，REMARK在所有权验证（OV）准确率和鲁棒性方面达到最先进水平，同时保持了被保护模型的效用（utility）。

Conclusion: REMARK通过将水印与指纹思想结合并聚焦于分布内图生成和输出差异指纹提取，有效解决了现有GNN所有权验证方法的局限性，在验证可靠性和模型保真度之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: The high training cost of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) has raised growing concerns regarding model ownership infringement, such as model stealing and unauthorized misuse. To verify model ownership and prevent significant economic losses, two groups of GNN Ownership Verification (OV) methods have been proposed: watermark-based methods and fingerprint-based methods. However, these methods typically face three limitations: (1) the performance degradation of protected models caused by out-of-distribution (OOD) watermark graphs with respect to the training set; (2) the unrealistic assumption that surrogate models have been trained on a watermark-containing training set; and (3) over-reliance on specific output levels for fingerprint extraction. In this paper, we propose a Robust watErMArk-based fingeRprint frameworK for GNNs, named REMARK. REMARK first generates carefully crafted in-distribution watermark graphs that maximize output differences between GNN models, thus mitigating OOD-induced performance degradation. REMARK then extracts robust fingerprints from these output differences to verify GNN ownership, thereby removing the assumptions that surrogate models must be trained on a watermark-containing dataset or expose specific output levels. Extensive experiments across widely used real-world datasets and GNN architectures demonstrate that REMARK achieves state-of-the-art OV accuracy and robustness while preserving the utility of protected models.

</details>


### [129] [Persistent Teacher Anchoring for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04773)
*Hyun Bin Park,Kyungho Song,Sangmin Lee,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Persistent Teacher Anchoring (PTA)，一种面向工具使用场景的LLM知识蒸馏rollout构建方法，通过教师对整轮turn级文本的承诺验证来缓解on-policy知识蒸馏中师生分布漂移问题，并引入persistent lookahead提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM后训练中，on-policy知识蒸馏(OPKD)让教师监督学生自生成的轨迹以适配下游RL。然而，在学生rollout进入教师本不会访问的状态时，师生分布差距会累积。在工具使用场景中，这一差距尤为严重，因为学生编写的工具调用在监督之前就已被执行，其返回的观察会塑造后续前缀。现有proposer-verifier生成方法虽然能通过教师验证减轻漂移，但只覆盖文本而将工具执行排除在范围之外。

Method: PTA包含两个核心设计：(1) turn级承诺(turn-level commitment)与chunk级验证相结合——在工具调用到达环境之前，教师必须先验证完整turn，只有通过验证的turn才能进入执行环节；(2) persistent lookahead——将已验证的chunk视为原子生成单元，利用空闲rollout容量推进未来样本，并在固定验证器下将未完成的样本跨学生更新流传，以提高吞吐效率。

Result: 在Search-R1风格的检索任务和DeepEyes风格的感知RL任务上，在相同的下游RL预算下，PTA相比OPKD使macro best@4分别提升2.5和2.8个百分点；lookahead机制将吞吐量提升24%。

Conclusion: PTA通过将工具执行纳入教师验证范围，有效解决了工具使用场景下OPKD的分布漂移问题，同时persistent lookahead在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升了rollout吞吐效率，是面向工具驱动的下游RL训练前准备阶段的有效蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Distillation is common in LLM post-training, where on-policy knowledge distillation (OPKD) uses student-generated trajectories to prepare the student for downstream RL. At each state, the student matches a next-token distribution supplied by the teacher. As the rollout enters states the teacher would not visit, the teacher-student distribution gap can accumulate. In tool use, this gap becomes consequential because student-written calls execute before supervision and their observations shape later prefixes. Proposer-verifier generation addresses this drift by letting the teacher decide which student-proposed text is retained during generation. Existing formulations govern text but leave tool execution outside their scope. We propose Persistent Teacher Anchoring (PTA), a student-induced but teacher-committed rollout construction. PTA retains chunk-level verification and adds turn-level commitment, allowing a call to reach the environment only after the teacher has verified the entire turn. Treating verified chunks as atomic generation units, we introduce persistent lookahead, which fills idle rollout capacity by advancing future samples and carrying unfinished ones across student updates under the fixed verifier. Across Search-R1-style retrieval and DeepEyes-style perception RL, applying PTA before downstream RL improves macro best@4 by 2.5 and 2.8 points over OPKD under the same downstream RL budget, while lookahead improves throughput by 24%.

</details>


### [130] [Dynamic Heterogeneous Graph Representation Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04779)
*Huan Liu,Pengfei Jiao,Jie Yin,Hongjiang Chen,Zhidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇系统综述动态异质图（DHG）表示学习方法的论文，提出统一的形式化定义，涵盖离散时间和连续时间两种时间粒度视角下的DHG，并提出了一个以算法为中心的分类法，将现有文献分为基于嵌入、基于GNN和基于Transformer的三类方法，同时总结了应用、数据集和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的AI系统本质上是演化中的异质实体及其复杂交互，静态或同质建模难以捕捉其结构语义与时间动态。现有综述缺乏对动态异质图表示学习方法的系统梳理，亟需一个统一框架来形式化定义、分类和比较这一领域的研究成果。

Method: 作者首先从时间粒度的视角提出了统一的形式化定义，涵盖离散时间和连续时间两类动态异质图；在此基础上构建了一种以算法为中心的新型分类法，将现有方法划分为早期基于嵌入的方法、基于图神经网络（GNN）的方法以及近期基于Transformer的方法，并明确揭示了各类方法在动态粒度上的内在建模偏好；最后归纳了典型应用、常用数据集和基准。

Result: 提供了一个系统的DHG表示学习综述，包含统一的DHG形式化框架、算法分类体系（嵌入/GNN/Transformer三类），以及应用、数据集和基准的汇总，并指出了未来有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: DHG表示学习是处理现实复杂动态系统的重要范式，该综述为该领域构建了清晰的基础框架与研究方向指南，有助于推动该快速演进领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) serves as a canonical paradigm for modeling complex networks. However, real-world AI systems inherently manifest as evolving heterogeneous entities with complex interactions, posing significant challenges to static or homogeneous modeling. To address these complexities, representation learning for Dynamic Heterogeneous Graphs (DHGs) has emerged as a vital approach for learning low-dimensional representations that simultaneously preserve structural semantics and temporal dynamics. This survey presents the first systematic review of DHG representation learning methods. We first introduce a unified formal definition that encompasses both discrete-time and continuous-time DHGs from the perspective of temporal granularity. Building upon this formulation, we propose a novel algorithm-centric taxonomy that categorizes existing literature, including early embedding-based approaches, graph neural network (GNN)-based models, and relatively recent Transformer-based DHG methods, while explicitly highlighting their intrinsic modeling biases with respect to dynamic granularity. Furthermore, we summarize representative applications of DHG representation learning, along with commonly used datasets and benchmarks. Finally, we discuss promising research directions that guide future advances in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [131] [Learning-Augmented Algorithms: Guarantees, Construction Mechanisms, and System-Level Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04787)
*Hailiang Zhao,Peng Chen,Xueyan Tang,Jianwei Yin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文对学习增强算法领域进行了系统性综述，整合了预测接口、误差度量、一致性-鲁棒性权衡以及五种代表性的构造机制，跨越在线优化、缓存、学习型数据结构、图问题和机制设计等多个领域。作者在定理层面区分了可达到的上界与匹配的渐近依赖关系，将形式化保证与实证系统证据分开，并明确处理了预测成本、反馈和组合问题，最终提出端到端推理的充分条件并列出开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习增强算法在利用不可靠预测的同时保留正式性能保证，这一领域迅速发展但缺乏统一的综合视角。现有研究分散在不同子领域，缺乏对预测界面、误差度量和构造机制的系统梳理，也缺少对理论保证与实证证据之间关系的清晰界定。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，系统性地综合五个维度：预测接口、误差度量、一致性-鲁棒性权衡、五种代表性构造机制，以及一个正交的定理层面轴来区分获得的上界与匹配的渐近依赖。同时将形式化保证从实证系统证据中分离，并明确处理预测成本、反馈和组合问题。

Result: 提出了端到端推理成立的充分条件，识别了四个开放问题领域：成本感知预测、内生误差、语义预测器和基准测试。

Conclusion: 该综述构建了一个统一框架以理解学习增强算法，明确区分了理论保证与实证表现，并为未来的研究提供了清晰的方向——特别是在成本感知预测、内生误差建模、语义预测器设计以及标准化基准测试等方面。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms use fallible predictions while retaining formal performance guarantees. This survey synthesizes prediction interfaces, error measures, consistency--robustness trade-offs, and five representative construction mechanisms across online optimization, caching, learned data structures, graph problems, and mechanism design. An orthogonal theorem-level axis distinguishes achieved upper bounds from matched asymptotic dependence. Formal guarantees are separated from empirical systems evidence, with explicit treatment of prediction cost, feedback, and composition. The resulting synthesis states sufficient conditions for limited end-to-end reasoning and delineates open problems in cost-aware prediction, endogenous error, semantic predictors, and benchmarking.

</details>


### [132] [How Faithful Is Attribution for Sales Forecasting? A Counterfactual Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04797)
*Glib Kechyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为多序列WaveNet销量预测模型添加了一种与架构无关的事后反事实可解释性层，将每个预测分解为精确求和至预测值的贡献，并通过删除/插入协议验证了其忠实性（删除间隙0.22, p<0.001；插入间隙0.27, p<0.01），同时诚实刻画了归因的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WaveNet风格的深度销量预测模型精度高但不可解释，当单一模型预测多个序列时无法解释预测依据。现有加性SHAP类归因方法存在分配伪影问题，需要一种无需分配假设、架构无关且可严格验证忠信度的事后可解释方法。

Method: 在Corporacion Favorita全量杂货数据集（174,685个序列、1,688天）训练的多序列WaveNet预测器上，添加反事实可解释层。该方法将每个预测分解为各贡献之和精确等于预测值，避免加性SHAP的分配伪影；采用删除/插入协议评估忠信度，并在5个背景采样种子上进行评估。

Result: 删除间隙0.22（p<0.001）、插入间隙0.27（p<0.01），统计上显著且在不同背景采样种子上稳健。归因在促销信号上的依赖在不同序列间具有异质性（中位比值约1.0，约20%序列表现出强效应）；模型捕捉周销售周期形状（日间周相关性r=0.78）但系统性低估其幅度。

Conclusion: 本文贡献不在于提升预测精度，而在于提供了一种带有严格忠信度评估的可解释层，并诚实地刻画了归因有效与失效的场景，为深度序列预测模型的可信部署提供了方法论支撑。

Abstract: Deep models for sales forecasting, such as WaveNet-style dilated convolutional networks, are accurate but opaque: when a single model predicts sales for one of many series, it offers no account of why. We add a post-hoc, architecture-agnostic counterfactual interpretability layer to a multi-series WaveNet forecaster trained on the full Corporacion Favorita grocery dataset (174,685 series over 1,688 days). The method decomposes each forecast into contributions that sum exactly to the predicted value, avoiding the allocation artifacts we observed with additive SHAP-style attribution. We evaluate faithfulness with a deletion/insertion protocol and find a statistically significant effect on both tests (deletion gap 0.22, p<0.001; insertion gap 0.27, p<0.01; robust across five background-sampling seeds), establishing that the attributions reflect genuine model behavior rather than plausible-looking artifacts. We then characterize, honestly, where attribution is and is not informative: reliance on the promotion signal is heterogeneous across series (median ratio approximately 1.0, with roughly 20% of series showing a strong effect), and the model captures the shape of the weekly sales cycle (day-of-week r=0.78) while systematically under-predicting its amplitude. Our contribution is not improved accuracy but an interpretability layer with a rigorous faithfulness evaluation and a candid account of its limits.

</details>


### [133] [Federated Attack Campaign Detection via Contrastive Encoding of Threat Indicators in Gradient Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04815)
*Manuel Röder,Bibin Babu,Frank-Michael Schleif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedIoC是一个模块化联邦学习框架，通过有监督对比学习将本地威胁指标（IoCs）编码进梯度更新中，利用客户端间对齐的梯度分量在服务器端通过余弦相似度聚类来恢复跨组织的协同网络攻击活动，无需直接共享敏感遥测或威胁指标数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测跨多个组织的协同网络攻击活动传统上需要跨机构边界和国家边境共享敏感的遥测数据和威胁情报，这构成了严重的隐私和合规障碍。联邦学习虽然可以在不共享原始数据的情况下训练共享检测器，但如何在不直接交换威胁指标的情况下发现跨组织的一致攻击活动模式仍然是开放问题。

Method: 提出FedIoC模块化框架：客户端使用有监督对比损失（基于IoC匹配流）将本地威胁指标嵌入梯度更新；训练批次中匹配已知指标模式的流构成正样本集，对比目标将它们的嵌入拉近并推开非IoC嵌入，使得攻击活动相关结构体现在梯度方向上；服务器按梯度更新的余弦相似度对客户端聚类，恢复全局攻击活动模式，无需直接传输IoC。

Result: 在两个公开威胁检测基准上评估，数据集分布式部署于FL客户端，每个客户端仅观察到每个活跃攻击活动的部分片段并持有不相交的本地指标集。在此设置下，FL服务器能够直接从梯度几何结构中恢复跨组织的攻击活动群体（campaign cohorts）。

Conclusion: FedIoC作为该场景的模块化框架被提出，研究指出非IID梯度结构是恢复攻击活动的主要驱动因素，并将设计改进该结构的编码器定义为开放性研究问题。

Abstract: Detecting orchestrated cyberattack campaigns that span multiple organizations traditionally requires sharing sensitive telemetry and threat intelligence across institutional boundaries and country borders, a barrier that Federated Learning removes by training shared threat detectors directly on local data. We propose FedIoC, a modular framework in which clients fold locally available structured threat indicators into their gradient updates; we instantiate the client-side encoder with a supervised contrastive loss over IoC-matched flows. Within each training batch, flows that match any known indicator pattern form the positive set; the contrastive objective pulls their learned embeddings together and pushes non-IoC embeddings away, so that campaign-relevant structure is, by design, expressed in the gradient direction. Clients sharing indicators for the same attack campaign then produce aligned gradient components, which the server clusters by the cosine similarity of their updates to recover global campaign patterns without any direct IoC transmission. We evaluate FedIoC on two public threat-detection benchmarks distributed across FL clients that each observe only a fragment of every active campaign and hold disjoint indicator sets derived from their local telemetry. In this regime the FL server recovers cross-organizational campaign cohorts directly from gradient geometry. We contribute FedIoC as a modular framework for this setting, and use it to pinpoint the non-IID gradient structure as the main driver of recovery and to define the open problem of designing encoders that improve on it.

</details>


### [134] [Communication-Efficient Personalized Federated Learning via Layer-Wise Multi-Threshold Random Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04830)
*Xu Zhang,Xingyu Hou,Jiacheng Cheng,Kaiyuan Feng,Maoguo Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于逐层多阈值随机素描的通信高效个性化联邦学习框架，通过为每层分配独立的量化阈值集并通过多区间提供更精细的低比特描述，以解决传统单阈值一位压缩方法忽略层间分布差异和粒度不足的问题，改善通信-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习虽能应对数据异构性，但在带宽受限系统中交换高维参数时通信成本高昂。现有的一位压缩方法仅对整个模型采用单一阈值规则，这有两个缺陷：一是忽略了各层参数分布与量化敏感性的差异；二是单一阈值只能提供粗粒度的二值信息，无法捕捉参数分布的细微变化。

Method: 提出一种逐层多阈值随机素描框架。具体而言，该方法为模型的每一层分配独立的量化阈值集合，使压缩表示能够适配层特定统计特性；同时利用多个量化区间提供更精细的低比特描述，以增强对素描参数的表示能力。该方法支持双向通信，使用紧凑的低比特素描进行参数交换。

Result: 与现有的一位压缩方法相比，该方法在双向通信中使用紧凑的低比特素描，显著改善了通信-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 逐层多阈值策略相较于全局统一阈值能够更好地适应各层参数的分布特性并提供更细粒度的量化表示，从而在保持低通信开销的同时提升模型精度，为带宽受限场景下的个性化联邦学习提供了更优的压缩通信方案。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is a promising paradigm for collaborative learning over distributed devices, where edge nodes collaboratively train personalized models without sharing raw data. Although PFL addresses data heterogeneity by learning client-specific models, it still suffers from substantial uplink and downlink communication costs when exchanging high-dimensional parameters in bandwidth-constrained systems. Recent one-bit methods achieve extreme compression, but they usually rely on a single thresholding rule applied to the whole model. This design has two limitations. First, it overlooks layer-wise differences in parameter distributions and quantization sensitivities. Second, a single threshold provides only coarse binary information and cannot capture fine-grained variations in parameter distributions. To address these issues, we propose a communication-efficient PFL framework via layer-wise multi-threshold random sketching. In the proposed method, each layer is assigned its own set of quantization thresholds, so that the compressed representation can adapt to layer-specific statistics while using multiple intervals to provide a finer low-bit description of sketched parameters. The proposed method supports bidirectional communication using compact low-bit sketches and improves the communication-accuracy tradeoff compared with existing one-bit compression approaches.

</details>


### [135] [PACE: Propagation-Aware Collaborative Correction for One-Shot Personalized Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04832)
*Ruizhe Huang,Chengran Li,Xiaochuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE提出了一种单次通信的个性化联邦图学习方法，将协作知识视为对完整本地预测器的紧凑校正而非替代品。客户端上传秩-r更新载体和传播消息矩的对角草图，服务端构建传播感知、接收端锚定的校正，通过凸负对数似然校准（CNLL）在保留完整本地模型的基础上选择本地与外部逻辑的系数。在六个数据集的评估中，秩-6时个性化返回仅占稠密张量字节的9.6-17.6%，校正能同时提升准确率和加权F1，而在缺乏证据的数据集上校准会将校正权重置零，完全保留本地预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端异质性在个性化联邦图学习中既是机遇也是风险：其他子图的知识可能补充本地模型，但不兼容的迁移会覆盖可靠预测；单次通信机制下不合适的服务端返回无法事后纠正，使这种权衡更加尖锐。现有方法缺少一种既能利用外部知识又不损害本地可靠预测的机制。

Method: PACE将协作知识视为紧凑校正面非本地预测器的替代品。每个客户端上传秩-r更新载体和传播消息矩的对角草图；服务端据此构造传播感知、接收端锚定的校正；接收端保留完整本地模型并利用凸负对数似然校准（CNLL）在验证节点上选取本地与外部logits之间的单一系数。模型参数保持固定且无需反馈。通过CNLL实现接收端证据驱动的选择性修正。

Result: 在六个评估数据集中，秩-6时个性化返回仅占稠密张量字节的9.6-17.6%。校正获得非零权重并在五个数据集上同时提升准确率和加权F1；在ogbn-arxiv上CNLL将校正权重赋为零、精确保留本地预测。将CNLL规则应用于三个引文数据集上的匹配基线并不能解释这些增益，表明增益来自PACE设计的校正本身。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：当接收端证据支持时，一个小的传输校正可以增强完整本地模型；否则本地预测保持不变。PACE通过接收端锚定与凸校准机制，实现了外部知识的有条件吸收与本地可靠预测的无损保留之间的平衡。

Abstract: Client heterogeneity creates both an opportunity and a risk in personalized federated graph learning. Knowledge held by other subgraphs may complement a receiver's Local model, but an incompatible transfer can override reliable predictions. One-shot communication sharpens this tension because an unsuitable server return cannot be corrected later. We introduce PACE, which treats collaborative knowledge as a compact correction to a complete Local predictor rather than as its replacement. Each client uploads a rank-r update carrier and a diagonal sketch of propagated message moments. The server uses them to construct a propagation-aware, receiver-anchored correction, while the receiver retains its full Local model. Convex negative-log-likelihood calibration (CNLL) then selects one coefficient between Local and External logits using validation nodes; model parameters remain fixed and no feedback is sent. At Rank-6, personalized returns occupy 9.6-17.6% of dense tensor bytes across the six evaluated datasets. The correction receives nonzero weight and improves both Accuracy and weighted-F1 over Local on five datasets; on ogbn-arxiv, CNLL assigns zero predictive weight to the correction and preserves Local predictions exactly. Applying the same CNLL rule to matched baselines on three citation datasets does not account for these gains. The central result is therefore that a small transported correction can augment a complete Local model when receiver evidence supports it while leaving the Local prediction unchanged otherwise.

</details>


### [136] [KVMem: Virtualizing Million-Token Agent Workspaces on a Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04852)
*Di Chai,Leye Wang,Zeshen Su,Zhiguo Xia,Zhihang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVMem是一个KV上下文虚拟化系统，将智能体的工作区历史以分页KV状态保存在GPU内存、主机内存和NVMe中，通过模型原生的注意力空间索引选择相关历史块，生成受原生上下文窗口限制的查询相关执行视图，使智能体工作区可扩展至远超模型原生上下文窗口的长度（最高1M tokens），同时提升任务效用和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体在持久化工作区中运行，累积的历史可能超出GPU KV容量和模型原生上下文窗口。现有系统要么将旧上下文压缩为摘要（丢失细粒度执行证据），要么稍后以文本形式检索（重复预填充已处理内容），均无法同时保留完整执行信息和高效响应。

Method: KVMem采用KV上下文虚拟化策略：将溢出工作区历史以分页KV状态跨GPU内存、主机内存和NVMe三层存储；使用轻量级且模型原生的注意力空间索引来选择相关历史块；依据查询动态物化受模型原生上下文窗口约束的执行视图，实现历史状态的按需调度与重建。

Result: 在LongMemEval、MemoryAgentBench和AgentLongBench等长上下文智能体基准测试（历史最高达1M tokens）上，KVMem相比压缩方法（处理上下文溢出的事实标准）通常获得更高的任务效用和推理效率。在DeepSWE长上下文测试中（Qwen3.8-27B），任务成功率从压缩方案的43.8%提升至48.4%。本地部署评估中，在配备24GB RTX 5090 Laptop GPU的普通笔记本上支持最高1M tokens的智能体工作区（超出Qwen3.6/3.8-27B原生256K上下文窗口4倍），单会话场景下每秒约生成50 tokens。

Conclusion: 通过将可寻址工作区大小与LLM原生上下文窗口解耦，KVMem为长期运行的智能体提供了一条实用路径，使其工作区可超出原生上下文窗口持续增长，兼顾任务性能与本地交互式响应能力。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents operate in persistent workspaces whose accumulated history can exceed both GPU KV capacity and the model's native context window. Existing systems typically compact older context into summaries or retrieve it later as text, either losing fine-grained execution evidence or repeatedly prefilling content that the model has already processed. We present KVMem, a KV-context virtualization system that preserves overflowed workspace history as paged KV state across GPU memory, host memory, and NVMe. KVMem uses lightweight, model-native attention-space indexes to select relevant historical blocks and materializes a query-dependent execution view bounded by the model's native context window. Extensive evaluations on long-context agent benchmarks spanning histories up to one million tokens, including LongMemEval, MemoryAgentBench, and AgentLongBench, show that KVMem generally achieves higher task utility and greater inference efficiency than compaction-based approaches, the de facto standard for handling context overflow. In the DeepSWE long-context test with Qwen3.8-27B, KVMem improves task success from 43.8% with compaction-only context management to 48.4%.
  In our local-deployment evaluation, KVMem runs Qwen3.6/3.8-27B NVFP4 with MTP on an off-the-shelf laptop equipped with a 24\,GB RTX 5090 Laptop GPU, virtualizing agent workspaces of up to 1M tokens-four times the model's native 256K-token context window. In a single-session setting, KVMem generates $\sim$50 tokens/s, providing interactive responsiveness for local agent execution. More broadly, by decoupling addressable workspace size from the LLM's native context window, KVMem provides a practical path toward long-running agents whose workspaces can grow beyond that window.

</details>


### [137] [When Genomic Masking Priors Fail to Transfer: Strong Variant Prediction, Weak Functional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04861)
*Susu Hu,Preetam Gattogi,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati,Stefanie Speidel,Julien Vibert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GenDA（Genomic Density-optimized Absorbing Diffusion），一种用于基因组建模的双向离散扩散模型，基于熵引导的span放置假设来集中重建压力于组成复杂的区域。结果表明该模型在微调后的ClinVar SNV AUROC达到0.774，超过同规模的自回归模型0.103，但熵引导并未带来额外提升；更出人意料的是，模型在零样本功能修复压力测试中未能稳定优于保持3-mer组成不变的控制组，揭示强变体预测、合理的损坏先验与功能性生成是需分别验证的独立声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组建模中，双向离散扩散模型因能从两侧重建缺失序列而被认为天然适合基因组数据。作者假设熵引导的span放置能集中重建压力于组成复杂的区域，从而提升下游变异效应预测和功能性序列生成。该研究希望验证这一假设是否成立，并澄清不同建模目标（预测、生成、修复）之间的关联性。

Method: GenDA采用双向吸收式离散扩散模型，202M参数规模，结合熵引导的span放置进行训练。训练spans上限为300bp，使用1-mer tokenization。通过监督微调在ClinVar上进行SNV变异效应预测评估，并设计零样本功能性修复压力测试（涵盖启动子、增强子、外显子边界和内含子边界），以保持3-mer组成不变的控制组（shuffle原生gap）作为对照基准。

Result: 微调后GenDA的ClinVar SNV AUROC为0.774，比同规模自回归模型高0.103。但随机span版本达到0.777，表明熵引导并未造成ClinVar提升。更意外的是，GenDA在功能性修复压力测试中未能一致超越3-mer组成保持的控制组，即使对50-500bp的短gap也是如此；增强子区域在较长gap下退化更明显。

Conclusion: 实验结果仅部分支持熵引导假设：强微调变体预测能力并非来自熵引导，且模型未展示出真正的功能性零样本修复能力。诊断指出多个边界条件：熵度量的序列复杂度而非功能重要性、1-mer tokenization限制物理上下文、训练span上限300bp、高绝对AlphaGenome保真度可与负控制归一化修复共存。核心结论是强预测、合理损坏先验和功能性生成是需要独立验证的不同声明。

Abstract: Bidirectional discrete diffusion model appears naturally suited to genomic modeling because it can reconstruct missing sequence from both flanks. We developed GenDA (Genomic Density-optimized Absorbing Diffusion) under the additional hypothesis that entropy-guided span placement would concentrate reconstruction pressure on compositionally complex regions, improving both downstream variant-effect prediction and functional sequence generation. Our results only partially support this premise. After supervised fine-tuning, the 202M-parameter GenDA model reaches a pooled ClinVar SNV AUROC of 0.774, exceeding a similarly scaled autoregressive model by 0.103. However, a matched random-span variant reaches 0.777, providing no evidence that entropy guidance causes the ClinVar improvement. More unexpectedly, GenDA fails a zero-shot functional inpainting stress test: across promoters, enhancers, exon boundaries, and intron boundaries, it does not consistently outperform a control that shuffles the native gap while exactly preserving 3-mer composition. Failure is already present for 50--500-bp gaps, although enhancer degradation worsens at longer gaps. Diagnostics identify several boundary conditions: entropy measures local sequence complexity rather than functional importance; 1-mer tokenization limits physical context; training spans are capped at 300 bp; and high absolute AlphaGenome fidelity can coexist with negative control-normalized restoration. These results show that strong fine-tuned variant prediction, a plausible corruption prior, and functional generation are distinct claims that require separate validation.

</details>


### [138] [From Deep to Shallow: Unconstrained and Efficient Layer Merging Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04881)
*Petro Shulzhenko,Gabriele Spadaro,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的深度压缩策略，使得在无解析解的情况下（如带padding的卷积层）也能合并层，且不增加合并后层的卷积核大小，从而在资源受限环境中获得更好的推理加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络计算量大，在资源受限环境中难以应用。深度压缩方法通过线性化冗余激活函数并合并层来减少计算，但现有方法有两个关键限制：一是无法直接应用于带padding的卷积（因缺乏合并层的解析解），二是合并后卷积核增大导致加速收益受限。

Method: 提出一种高效策略，使不存在现有解析解的层也能被合并，同时在不增加内核大小的情况下完成层融合。方法在多种架构和数据集上验证，并在真实嵌入式平台上测量推理速度提升。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并在真实嵌入式平台上取得了推理速度提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法克服了深度压缩中合并带padding卷积层和内核膨胀的两大挑战，在嵌入式平台上实现了有效的推理加速，代码已公开。

Abstract: Although Deep Neural Networks have become foundational in many areas of Machine Learning, high computational demands limit their application in resource-constrained environments. To address this issue, depth compression methods have been proposed to identify and linearize redundant activation functions, thereby allowing for the merging of layers without intermediate non-linearities. However, these methods face two key challenges: they cannot be directly applied to convolutions with padding due to the absence of an analytical solution for merging these layers, and they typically increase the kernel size of merged layers, thus limiting speed-up gains. To overcome these limitations, we propose an efficient strategy that enables merging of layers without an existing analytical solution, and also without increasing kernel size. We validate our approach across multiple architectures and datasets, and measure inference speed-up gains on real embedded platforms. We publicly released the code at https://github.com/ShulzhenkoPetr/deep-to-shallow.

</details>


### [139] [Adaptation Interfaces for In-Context Tabular Foundation Models in Time-to-Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04901)
*Minh-Khoi Pham,Luca Cotugno,Dan Cernei,Alina Sirbu,Stefano Masi,Giuseppe Prencipe,Alessandro Pingitore,Patrizia Landi,Working Group on Uric Acid,Cardiovascular Risk of the Italian Society of Hypertension,Tai Tan Mai,Martin Crane,Marija Bezbradica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何将表格基础模型（TabFMs）扩展到删失时间-事件预测（生存分析）任务。作者基于先前工作，将TabFM与CoxPH、DeepHit等生存模型连接，改进上下文重采样训练过程，并在74个单风险数据集和4个竞争风险数据集上评估多种适应策略。结果显示：零样本推理在小数据集上有效，监督适应在大数据集上更优；Cox界面在大数据集上对IBS指标最稳健，DeepHit在时间依赖Concordance Index上更强，分类微调随数据规模增长更具竞争力但概率预测仍较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabFMs在标准分类和回归任务上表现优异，但将其扩展到生存分析（删失时间-事件预测）面临挑战，因为需要妥善处理删失机制和事件时间动态。作者希望系统性地评估TabFM在生存任务上的迁移能力及不同适配接口的优劣。

Method: 在构建先前工作（将TabFM与CoxPH、DeepHit连接）的基础上，修订了上下文重采样的训练过程。采用冻结的TabFM骨干网络，评估三类适应策略：（1）时间零样本重构（temporal zero-shot reformulation）、（2）基于分类的微调、（3）生存头适配（survival-head adaptation）。分别在74个单风险数据集和4个竞争风险数据集上进行基准测试。

Result: 零样本推断在较小单风险数据集上有效；监督适应在数据集规模增大时优势明显。Cox接口在较大数据集上的Integrated Brier Score（IBS）表现最可靠；DeepHit在时间依赖Concordance Index上相对优于IBS；在竞争风险分析中，cause-specific MTLR在四个数据集中排名最高。分类微调随数据规模增长更接近零样本推断水平，但在概率预测方面仍较弱。

Conclusion: TabFM向生存任务的有效迁移取决于数据规模和数据所代表的统计结构，不同的适应界面（Cox、DeepHit、MTLR等）在不同场景下有各自的优势，不存在普适最优方案。实现脚本已开源。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TabFMs) achieve strong performance on structured data, particularly for standard classification and regression problems. Yet, extending them to censored time-to-event prediction is challenging because it requires properly handling censoring and event-time dynamics. Building on our prior work, we further link TabFMs with CoxPH and DeepHit and revise the context-resampled training procedure. We evaluate temporal zero-shot reformulation, classification-based fine-tuning, and survival-head adaptation using frozen TabFM backbones on 74 single-risk data sets, and we additionally study 4 competing-risk data sets. Zero-shot inference is effective on smaller single-risk data sets, whereas supervised adaptation becomes increasingly advantageous as data sets scale. Cox provides the most reliably strong interface, especially for Integrated Brier Score (IBS) on larger data sets. DeepHit is relatively stronger for the time-dependent Concordance Index than for IBS, while cause-specific MTLR ranks highest among the TabFM survival heads in the four-data-set competing-risk analysis. Classification fine-tuning becomes more competitive with zero-shot inference as data sets grow but remains weaker for probabilistic prediction. Overall, our results indicate that effective TabFM transfer depends on the data regime and on the statistical structure represented by the chosen adaptation interface. The implementation scripts used for this work are available at https://github.com/kaylode/survival-fm.

</details>


### [140] [Physics-Aware Random Walk Fingerprints for Scalable Power Grid Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04943)
*Adnan Anwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-PA-RWF，一种面向电力系统的多通道物理感知随机游走指纹（Random Walk Fingerprints）框架，通过将物理边状态引入随机游走动力学中，构建边加权通道的图级表示，在不进行端到端训练的情况下，在PowerGraph基准上达到与强GNN基线相当的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力级联故障分类中，GNN虽预测性能强但需要端到端训练和模型特定调参，且潜在表示难以与物理传播模式建立联系。RWF虽是可扩展且可解释的替代方案，但现有变体主要强调拓扑和节点级信息，未将电网相关的运行边状态纳入游走动力学中。

Method: 该方法从领域相关属性构造多个边加权通道，对每个加权图提取通道特定的随机游走指纹，并将所得向量拼接成紧凑的图级表示。扩展版本MC-PA-RWF+进一步引入节点-边联合信息。

Result: 在三个PowerGraph基准系统上，MC-PA-RWF显著优于仅基于拓扑的RWF，并与强GNN基线（GCN、GAT、GINE、TransformerConv）相比具有竞争力的平衡准确率。在最大规模设置下，MC-PA-RWF+达到约98.04%-99.32%的平衡准确率，较最强GNN基线在故障类F1上提升1.60-5.84个百分点，且在三个系统上均有统计显著提升。

Conclusion: 物理感知的多通道随机游走指纹是一种轻量级、可解释且高性能的电力系统级联故障分类框架，能在无需端到端深度训练的情况下超越强GNN基线，为可解释的电力图表示学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent benchmarks such as PowerGraph provide large collections of power-grid graphs for cascading-failure classification. Graph neural networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on this task, but typically require end-to-end training and model-specific tuning, while their latent representations can be difficult to relate to physically meaningful propagation patterns. Random Walk Fingerprints (RWF) offer a scalable and interpretable alternative, but existing variants primarily emphasise topology and node-level information, leaving grid-relevant operational edge states in the walk dynamics. We propose Multi-Channel Physics-Aware Random Walk Fingerprints (MC-PA-RWF) for power systems, a lightweight graph-level representation framework that introduces physical edge states into random-walk propagation. The method constructs multiple edge-weighted channels from domain-relevant attributes, extracts a channel-specific fingerprint from each weighted graph, and concatenates the resulting vectors into a compact representation. Experiments on three \textit{PowerGraph} benchmark systems show substantial improvements over topology-only RWF and competitive balanced accuracy against strong GNN baselines, including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCN), Graph Attention Networks (GAT), Graph Isomorphism Networks with edge features (GINE), and Transformer-based Graph Convolutional Networks (TransformerConv). At the largest evaluated settings, the node-edge extension MC-PA-RWF+ achieves around 98.04% - 99.32% balanced accuracy and improves failure-class F1 over the strongest GNN baseline by 1.60 -- 5.84 percentage points, with statistically significant gains across all three systems.

</details>


### [141] [Fractal basins trap latent reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04963)
*Jeffrey Lai,Anthony Bao,John Quinn,William Gilpin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示推理模型在处理困难任务时表现出瞬态混沌现象，这是任务计算复杂性的物理结果。多种主流推理模型构成具有分形盆地的动力系统，其分形程度随任务难度增加而上升，该现象源于推理过程在接近正确答案的解（鞍点）附近长时间滞留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在更困难任务上推理时间更长的现象已被广泛观察，但其背后的普适机制尚不清楚。作者旨在从动力系统和混沌理论的角度揭示推理变慢的根本原因，理解这种现象是否是问题难度的必然结果。

Method: 作者将推理模型视为动力系统，通过分析推理痕迹来检测瞬态混沌和分形盆地的存在。他们在数独与迷宫求解、视觉谜题和数学逻辑等多样化任务中测量系统的分形维度，并识别限制在鞍点（对应近乎正确的候选解）附近的动力学行为，从而建立推理延时与混沌动力学之间的关联。

Result: 发现多样的前沿推理模型均为具有分形盆地的动力系统，且分形程度随任务难度增加而单调上升。瞬态混沌源于推理在接近正确的候选解（鞍点）附近的长时间滞留。研究证明推理减速是问题难度的不可避免结果。

Conclusion: 推理过程的减速是任务内在复杂性的物理必然结果，而非可归因于具体模型实现的偶然现象。该工作将推理轨迹确立为一类新型动力系统，为理解现代AI推理模型的行为提供了物理和数学框架。

Abstract: Reasoning allows artificial intelligence models to revisit and correct their mistakes, enabling recent frontier advances in mathematical theorem solving, software engineering, and autonomous task planning. Reasoning models are widely observed to reason for longer on harder tasks, but the general mechanism responsible for these slowdowns is unknown. Here, we show that reasoning models exhibit transient chaos, a physical consequence of the computational complexity of difficult tasks. As a consequence, we show that diverse leading reasoning models are dynamical systems with fractal basins, with fractality increasing with task difficulty across diverse tasks like Sudoku and maze solving, visual puzzles, and mathematical logic. We show that transient chaos emerges due to reasoning becoming trapped for extended durations near saddle points, which we show correspond to nearly-correct attempted solutions of the underlying problem. Our results show that reasoning slowdowns are an inevitable consequence of problem hardness in modern artificial intelligence models, and establish reasoning traces as a rich new class of dynamical system.

</details>


### [142] [BeaconKV: Key-Value Cache Compression Guided by Beacon Queries for Efficient Large Reasoning Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04971)
*Janghyeon Kim,Minsoo Kim,Kyuhong Shim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BeaconKV，一种无需训练的KV缓存压缩方法，通过维护beacon queries来预测长程推理中哪些键值对会被"思想回溯令牌"重新关注，从而在几乎不损失精度的前提下实现高达5.8倍的内存压缩和4.3倍以上的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）通过扩展思维链（CoT）获得优异的问题求解能力，但KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成严重的内存瓶颈。现有KV缓存压缩方法假设近期查询可作为未来注意力模式的有效代理，作者发现这一假设在长程推理中失效——某些解码步骤生成"思想回溯令牌"（TRT）会重新关注较早的上文（如任务计划），导致现有方法性能退化。

Method: BeaconKV的核心洞察是：TRT对应的查询在嵌入空间中聚类为少数相似组。基于此，方法维护beacon queries（每个全局查询簇的紧凑代表），据此预测哪些KV对将被重新访问，而无需存储完整查询历史。该方法是训练免费的，作为KV缓存压缩策略直接应用于推理过程。

Result: 在四个开源大型推理模型和多种推理基准上，BeaconKV总体上优于现有压缩方法，实现了最高5.8倍的内存压缩，同时几乎保持完整缓存的精度，吞吐量提升超过4.3倍。

Conclusion: 作者通过揭示长程推理中注意力模式的新特性（TRT及其聚类结构），提出了一种利用该结构的有效压缩方法，为解决LRMs在长推理轨迹下的KV缓存内存瓶颈提供了新的、无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve superior problem-solving through extended Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation, but the resulting key-value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length and creates severe memory bottlenecks, often exceeding GPU capacity for long reasoning traces. Existing KV cache compression methods rely on recent queries to estimate future token importance, implicitly assuming these serve as reliable proxies for future attention patterns. We demonstrate that this assumption fails in long-horizon reasoning: certain decoding steps generate Thought Revisiting Tokens (TRT) that re-attend to distant previous context, such as task-solving plans formulated early in the trace. Through systematic analysis, we discover that queries corresponding to the TRT cluster into a small number of similarity groups in the embedding space. Based on this insight, we propose BeaconKV, a training-free KV cache compression method that maintains beacon queries, compact representatives for each global query cluster, to anticipate which KV pairs will be revisited without storing the entire query history. Across four open-source LRMs and diverse reasoning benchmarks, BeaconKV generally outperforms existing compression methods, achieving up to $5.8\times$ memory reduction while nearly preserving full cache accuracy and improving throughput by over $4.3\times$.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Homoscedasticity: Decoupled Uncertainty Optimization for Deep Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04995)
*Juncheng Zhou,Jiaxi Lu,Weijing Zeng,Zhong Li,Hao Qi,Jingsong Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DUO是一个面向深度不平衡回归任务的不确定性感知框架，通过条件高斯分布建模实例级预测不确定性，采用解耦均值-方差优化将不确定性转化为尾部样本的动态增强信号，并设计基于分布重叠的分布引导对比学习机制以缓解特征松散和跨标签语义纠缠问题，在多个视觉和生物DIR基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DIR方法大多在MSE或其变体下学习确定性点映射，隐含假设所有样本具有统一的不确定性水平，忽略了长尾数据中普遍存在的实例级异方差性。此外，异方差负对数似然在DIR场景下存在梯度耦合问题，会削弱困难尾部样本的学习信号，导致优化惯性和尾部欠拟合。

Method: DUO包含三个核心组件：(1) 将回归目标建模为条件高斯分布以显式刻画实例级预测不确定性；(2) 通过解耦均值-方差优化将不确定性转化为尾部样本的动态增强信号；(3) 设计分布引导的对比学习机制，基于样本分布之间的重叠度自适应构建正负样本对，以缓解特征松散和跨标签语义纠缠。

Result: 在IMDB-WIKI-DIR、AgeDB-DIR和AAV2-DIR等视觉与生物DIR基准上，DUO在few-shot bMAE和GM指标上达到最优，并在few-shot MAE上保持有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: DUO通过不确定性感知建模和动态增强机制，有效解决了DIR中异方差性被忽视和梯度耦合导致的尾部欠拟合问题，实验结果验证了其在多模态长尾回归任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) is pervasive in continuous prediction tasks across diverse modalities, such as age estimation, depth prediction, and protein mutation activity prediction, where label-scarce tail samples often carry higher practical value. However, most existing methods still learn deterministic point mappings under mean squared error or its simple variants, implicitly assuming a uniform uncertainty level across all samples and thereby overlooking the instance-wise heteroscedasticity that is widespread in long-tailed data. We further point out that even heteroscedastic negative log-likelihood suffers from a gradient coupling issue, which, under DIR scenarios, weakens the learning signal of hard tail samples and leads to optimization inertia as well as tail underfitting. To address this, we propose DUO, an uncertainty-aware long-tailed regression framework. Specifically, the proposed method models the regression target as a conditional Gaussian distribution to explicitly characterize instance-level predictive uncertainty, and transforms uncertainty into a dynamic enhancement signal for tail samples through decoupled mean-variance optimization. Furthermore, we design a distribution-guided contrastive learning mechanism that adaptively constructs positive and negative pairs based on the overlap between sample distributions, thereby alleviating feature looseness and cross-label semantic entanglement. Across visual and biological DIR benchmarks, DUO achieves the best few-shot bMAE and GM on IMDB-WIKI-DIR, AgeDB-DIR, and AAV2-DIR while remaining competitive on few-shot MAE.

</details>


### [144] [Solution-space heterogeneity shapes federated learning dynamics across partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05012)
*Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Ziqing Wen,Tao Sun,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议，将连续监督响应转化为可复用的解分区（solution bins），并通过最优传输量化联邦PDE学习中客户端间的非独立同分布（non-IID）分离程度。研究发现Dirichlet浓度的降低会系统性地增加客户端间的解空间距离、优化异质性和参数发散，但其对最终误差的影响具有任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦科学机器学习允许各机构在不集中本地物理数据的情况下训练神经代理模型，但现有的PDE研究缺乏可迁移（transferable）的非IID数据定义。已有协议根据方程特定规则划分坐标、系数、边界条件或几何，缺乏通用且可量化的基准，阻碍了不同任务间联邦PDE学习的公平比较与系统评估。

Method: 提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议：首先将连续监督响应（PDE解）转化为离散解分区（solution bins）作为可复用标签；然后利用最优传输（optimal transport）在这些分区的几何上量化客户端间的实际数据分离程度。推导了总体分配异质性与Dirichlet浓度参数之间的精确反比关系，并建立了响应异质性如何引发梯度不一致、局部更新分散和参数分歧的条件。

Result: 在7个受控与公开PDE任务、3种神经算子家族和5个随机种子上的实验表明：更低的Dirichlet浓度一致地增大客户端间的实现解空间距离与优化异质性。最终误差的退化具有任务依赖性——在低黏性Burgers方程上效应最大，最异质设定下最终误差退化达4.157个百分点；而增加通信轮次或采用更光滑的动力学可缩小最终差距，尽管参数分离仍然存在。

Conclusion: 结果区分了可复现的几何机制（数据异质性导致的优化与参数分歧）与任务依赖的泛化结果，为评估non-IID联邦PDE学习提供了统一的、可迁移的公共基准和方法论基础。

Abstract: Federated scientific machine learning enables institutions to train neural surrogates without centralizing local physical data, yet studies of partial differential equations (PDEs) lack a transferable definition of non-independent and identically distributed data. Existing protocols partition coordinates, coefficients, boundary conditions, or geometries according to equation-specific rules. Here, we introduce solution-space PDE-Dirichlet, a protocol that converts continuous supervised responses into reusable solution bins and quantifies the realized separation between clients through optimal transport over the geometry of these bins. We derive an exact inverse relation between population allocation heterogeneity and the Dirichlet concentration, and we establish conditions under which response heterogeneity induces gradient disagreement, local-update dispersion, and parameter divergence. Across seven controlled and public PDE tasks, three neural-operator families, and five random seeds, a lower concentration consistently increases the realized solution distance and optimization heterogeneity. The degradation in final error is task dependent: the largest effect occurs for low-viscosity Burgers, reaching 4.157 percentage points under the most heterogeneous setting, whereas additional communication or smoother dynamics can reduce the final gap despite persistent parameter separation. These results distinguish a reproducible geometric mechanism from task-dependent generalization outcomes and provide a common basis for evaluating non-IID federated PDE learning.

</details>


### [145] [Amortizing Scaling Law Construction Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05016)
*Abhash Kumar Jha,Diana Alexandra Onuţu,Neeratyoy Mallik,Swagatam Haldar,Sam Laing,Niccolò Ajroldi,Shiwei Liu,Joaquin Vanschoren,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过贝叶斯优化高效收集数据来构建缩放定律（scaling law）的框架。由于拟合缩放定律仅需各计算量下的最优损失前沿，大多数训练的配置都被浪费，作者将数据收集形式化为贝叶斯优化问题，提出在计算量受限下比较拟合方法的指标，并通过渐进扩展计算预算和用代理幻想（surrogate-fantasized）评估补齐网格，在节省10–100倍计算的前提下接近全密集网格拟合的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建缩放定律通常需要穷举训练超参数、token预算和参数量的大规模组合网格，计算开销极高；然而拟合缩放定律实际上只需要每个计算规模下的最佳损失前沿，抛弃了大量已训练配置的信息。因此亟需一种高效的数据收集方法，用更少的计算量获取拟合所需的关键信息。

Method: (1) 将缩放定律构建中的数据收集形式化为一个贝叶斯优化问题；(2) 提出在受限计算预算下比较不同缩放定律拟合方法的度量指标；(3) 在数据采集过程中逐步扩大计算预算（compute-ordered evaluation），以对应实际中按计算量排序评估配置的做法；(4) 用代理模型幻想（surrogate-fantasized）的评估结果扩充观测到的配置，从而恢复完整实验网格，无需训练所有配置即可完成拟合。

Result: 渐进扩展计算预算的采集策略能显著提升恢复效率；结合代理幻想评估可准确拟合缩放定律，在全密集网格效果接近的情况下实现最高10–100倍的计算节省。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯优化驱动的智能采集与代理幻想补充，可在大幅降低计算成本的前提下重构出与全密集网格训练相当精确的缩放定律，为大规模基础模型的效率化设计提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Scaling laws guide the design choices for training large foundation models, but deriving them involves training an exhaustive grid over hyperparameters, token budgets, and parameter counts, which is computationally expensive. Fitting a scaling law, however, only requires the best-loss frontier across compute scales, discarding most of the trained configurations. We propose a framework for efficient scaling law construction that formulates data collection as a Bayesian optimization problem, and introduce metrics for comparing scaling law fitting methods under constrained compute budgets. We find that progressively expanding the compute budget during acquisition, mirroring the compute-ordered evaluation of configurations in practice, substantially improves recovery efficiency. Augmenting the observed configurations with surrogate-fantasized evaluations then recovers the broader experimental grid, allowing accurate scaling law fitting without training every configuration. Together, these can closely match scaling law fits over a full dense grid at computational savings of up to $10\text{--}100\times$.

</details>


### [146] [Deep Microcompression: Structured Pruning and Bit-packed Quantization for Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05081)
*Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Tolulope Matthew Busoye,Eghonghon-aye Eigbe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DMC（Deep Microcompression），一个面向裸机微控制器的硬件感知深度学习推理流水线，集成结构化剪枝、量化感知训练和定长比特打包，在LeNet-5上实现55.8×权重压缩比（准确率98.77%），生成无依赖的C库并具有确定性延迟。在RP2040（Cortex-M0+）上相比TensorFlow Lite将二进制体积减少3×同时保持同等准确率，并首次实现了标准CNN在仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裸机微控制器（如ATmega328P）的SRAM和Flash资源极为有限（仅2KB SRAM），此前普遍认为在此类设备上进行CNN推理不可行。现有深度学习推理框架（如TensorFlow Lite）在资源极度受限的微控制器上仍过于庞大，亟需一种硬件感知的压缩流水线，使标准CNN能够在这些极低资源设备上实现部署。

Method: DMC采用三步流水线：(1) 结构化剪枝（structured pruning）去除冗余结构；(2) 量化感知训练（quantization-aware training）进行低比特量化；(3) 定长比特打包（fixed-length bit-packing）进一步压缩存储。整个流程为硬件感知设计，最终生成无依赖的C代码库，保证推理延迟的确定性。

Result: 在LeNet-5上实现55.8×权重压缩比且准确率达98.77%。在RP2040（基于Cortex-M0+）上，相比TensorFlow Lite二进制体积减少3×，准确率保持一致。关键贡献是在受限于2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上首次成功部署了标准CNN推理。

Conclusion: DMC证明了通过深度压缩技术，标准CNN推理能够在此前被认为不可行的极低资源裸机微控制器（如2KB SRAM的ATmega328P）上实现，为嵌入式边缘AI的部署开辟了新可能性。

Abstract: This paper introduces Deep Microcompression (DMC), a hardware-aware pipeline for deep learning inference on bare-metal microcontrollers. DMC integrates structured pruning, quantization-aware training, and fixed-length bit-packing to achieve a 55.8$\times$ weight compression ratio on LeNet-5 (98.77\% accuracy), generating a dependency-free C library with deterministic latency. On the RP2040 (Cortex-M0+), DMC reduces binary size by 3$\times$ versus TensorFlow Lite while matching its accuracy. Critically, DMC enables the first documented deployment of a standard CNN on the ATmega328P, a device constrained to 2KB SRAM, previously considered infeasible for CNN inference.

</details>


### [147] [NEAT-POCKET: Pocket-Conditioned Autoregressive 3D Molecular Generation with a Neighborhood-Guided Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05097)
*Roxane Axel Jacob,Daniel Rose,Thierry Langer,Johannes Kirchmair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NEAT-POCKET，一个基于回归自回归NEAT模型的口袋条件扩展，用于3D分子生成，能够逐原子地在蛋白质口袋环境中生成分子，同时保持原子排列不变性并显式建模氢原子。在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上取得了具有竞争力的结构基生成性能，且采样速度明显快于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速早期药物发现，通过在靶蛋白结合口袋内直接生成新型配体，提供更快速、灵活的基于结构的药物设计工具。

Method: 将自回归NEAT模型扩展为口袋条件版本NEAT-POCKET，在蛋白质口袋环境中逐原子生成分子，保持原子排列不变性并显式建模氢原子。此外支持口袋条件下的片段补全。

Result: 在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上达到有竞争力的结构基分子生成性能，同时采样速度大幅快于现有基线。

Conclusion: NEAT-POCKET是一种快速、灵活且实用的基于结构的药物设计框架，不仅支持全分子生成，还支持与先导化合物优化和骨架延伸直接相关的片段补全任务。

Abstract: AI-driven de novo molecular design offers a promising route to accelerate early-stage drug discovery by generating novel ligands directly within target protein binding pockets. We present NEAT-POCKET, a pocket-conditioned extension of the autoregressive NEAT model for 3D molecular generation. NEAT-POCKET generates molecules atom by atom in protein pocket environments while preserving atom permutation invariance and explicitly modeling hydrogen atoms. Benchmarks on the CrossDocked and SPINDR datasets show that NEAT-POCKET achieves competitive structure-based generation performance while sampling substantially faster than existing baselines. Beyond full-molecule generation, NEAT-POCKET naturally enables pocket-conditioned fragment completion, a task directly relevant to lead optimization and scaffold elaboration. These results position NEAT-POCKET as a fast, flexible, and practical framework for structure-based drug design.

</details>


### [148] [A Comparative Study of Counterfactual Explainers for Graph Neural Networks Enabling Multiple Types of Graph Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05113)
*Maria Myrto Villia,Filippos Gouidis,Theodore Patkos,Panos Trahanias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种最新的图结构数据反事实解释方法进行了系统性比较研究，在多样化的真实和合成数据集上，覆盖二分类和多分类的图分类及节点分类任务，并使用多种定量与定性指标评估各方法的性能，旨在识别各方法的优缺点以指导未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据的反事实解释旨在找到对输入图进行最小且现实的修改以改变模型预测结果。虽然支持同时添加和删除边的反事实解释器已经出现，但仍缺乏通用且高效的方法，尤其在生成解释的质量方面。现有方法各有优缺点，常在解释大小、覆盖率和质量之间权衡，因此需要系统性地比较各方法的适用场景，以指导该领域的未来研究。

Method: 该研究对六种最新的（SOTA）模型进行了大规模实证比较。实验设置包括：在多样化的真实世界和合成数据集上进行评测，覆盖二元分类和多分类的图分类与节点分类任务，并使用多种定量和定性指标评估各方法的性能表现。

Result: 研究通过在不同任务类型（图分类和节点分类）、不同分类设定（二元和多分类）以及多样化的数据集上的系统比较，揭示了各SOTA反事实解释方法在解释大小、覆盖率和质量之间的权衡关系，明确了每个方法的优势场景和不足之处。

Conclusion: 图反事实解释问题尚未得到完全解决，不同方法存在强项与局限，需要在解释大小、覆盖率和质量之间做出权衡。该比较研究为从业者选择合适的方法提供了指导，并为未来研究指明了需要改进的方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations for graph-structured data seek to determine minimal and realistic modifications required in an input graph to alter a model's prediction to a predefined output. Although counterfactual explainers that support modifying the graph by both adding and removing edges have recently emerged, there is still a lack of general and efficient methods, especially when considering the quality of the generated explanations. Moreover, the problem remains far from solved, as existing methods exhibit different strengths and weaknesses, often trading off between explanation size, coverage and quality. For this reason, it is important to identify where each method performs well and where it falls short, so as to guide future research in the field. Thus, our study compares six state-of-the-art (SOTA) models on a diverse set of real-world and synthetic datasets, covering both binary and multi-class graph and node classification tasks, and evaluates their performance using diverse quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [149] [Single-Query Black-Box Calibration Auditing via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05125)
*Roman Plaud,Antoine Saillenfest,Matthieu Labeau,Thomas Bonald,Willem Waegeman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在商业LLM API不暴露输出概率的情况下，利用logit_bias参数通过单次查询即可精确评估概率阈值的方法，进而为二分类任务构建了一致且高效的真实校准误差（True Calibration Error）估计器，为黑盒基础模型的审计提供了可行框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为零样本分类器部署时，校准评估至关重要。然而商业API提供商日益隐藏标准校准指标所需的连续输出概率，阻碍了模型可靠性的审计与安全评估。

Method: 首先证明任何暴露logit_bias参数的LLM API可通过数学手段用每样本单次查询准确评估概率阈值；基于此机制，提出一种新颖且具有一致性保证的二分类任务真实校准误差估计器。

Result: 所提出的估计器在数学上被证明是一致（provably consistent）的，能够在不获取连续概率的情况下有效评估黑盒模型的校准性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为审计不透明API背后的基础模型提供了一种高效且理论上严谨的校准审计框架，填补了因输出概率不可见而导致的校准评估空白。

Abstract: Evaluating the calibration of Large Language Models (LLMs) is critical for their safe deployment as zero-shot classifiers. Yet, commercial API providers increasingly hide the continuous output probabilities required by standard calibration metrics. To bypass this opacity, we demonstrate that any LLM API exposing a logit\_bias parameter can be mathematically manipulated to evaluate exact probability thresholds using strictly one query per sample. Leveraging this mechanism, we introduce a novel and provably consistent estimator of the True Calibration Error for binary tasks. Our approach therefore provides an efficient framework for auditing black-box foundation models.

</details>


### [150] [MomentQuant: an even more minimalist interval method with linear time complexity for time series classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05136)
*Johann Faouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种加速时间序列分类算法Quant的方法：一是优化Quant的代码实现，二是利用Cornish-Fisher展开推导近似分位数替代精确分位数，从而省去排序步骤、降低计算复杂度，新算法命名为MomentQuant。实验证明优化版Quant比原版更快，而MomentQuant比优化版Quant更快，仅以极小的预测性能损失为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类在多领域有广泛应用。Quant算法虽然在准确率和速度上表现优秀，但仍有进一步加速的空间。实际应用中推理频率远高于训练频率，因此提升推理速度具有重要的现实意义。

Method: 提出两个改进：(1) 对原始Quant算法（基于递归固定dyadic区间的分位数提取）进行更优化的代码实现；(2) 提出MomentQuant算法，利用Cornish-Fisher展开从矩估计推导近似分位数，代替精确分位数，从而消除对时间序列排序的需求，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验证据表明：(1) 优化后的Quant实现比原始版本更快；(2) MomentQuant比优化版Quant更快；(3) MomentQuant的加速以极小的预测性能下降为代价。

Conclusion: 两种加速策略均有效，其中MomentQuant通过近似分位数换取计算效率的提升，特别适合推理频繁的真实应用场景。

Abstract: Time series data is very common in many real-world applications and in numerous domains, with increasing interest for automated information extraction using machine learning. One of these subfields is time series classification, which consists in assigning a label to each new, unseen time series. Many algorithms have been developed over the past decades, with the trade-off between predictive performance and computational cost being consistently discussed. Quant, an interval-based algorithm extracting quantiles from recursive, fixed, dyadic intervals, was shown to achieve high accuracy, while being very fast. We propose two changes to make this algorithm even faster. The first one is a better optimized implementation of the exact same algorithm. The second one is to derive approximate quantiles, using the Cornish-Fisher expansion, instead of exact quantiles. This change removes the necessity to sort the time series, leading to a smaller computational complexity. We call this novel algorithm MomentQuant. We provide evidence that our implementation of Quant is faster than the original one, and that MomentQuant is even faster than our implementation of Quant, at the cost of a tiny decrease in predictive performance. These improvements are especially relevant for real-life applications, where inference is performed much more often than training.

</details>


### [151] [From 80x to 385x: A Best-Matching-Unit Search at the L2 Roof, Measured Against a Symmetrically Tuned Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05138)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个对GPU实现进行对称调优的研究：作者不仅调优了自己提出的新型SOM算法（SparseBin），还调优了基线算法cuSPARSE。通过四个调优杠杆（tile大小、tile成员聚类、神经元轴分块、向量化加载），SparseBin速度提升5.6-10.1倍/epoch（32x32至512x512映射），并因对cuSPARSE也进行了调优，使两者的性能差距从~80x提升至~385x（差距反而扩大）。最终核函数达到L2带宽屋顶的77%，其他单元40-65%，理论上限约1.3x，表明这是调优的终点而非中途站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出GPU实现比较中的不公平性：通常研究者只调优自己的算法，而基线算法直接用现成版本运行，导致比较结论不可靠。作者提出了一种对称调优的方法论，同时调优自研算法和基线算法，以获得更公正的性能比较结论。

Method: 针对SOM训练中占主导地位的best-matching-unit（BMU）搜索，通过四个调优杠杆进行优化：tile size（块大小）、tile-membership clustering（块成员聚类）、neuron-axis chunking（神经元轴分块）和vectorised loads（向量化加载）。同时，对基线cuSPARSE也施加了所有有对应物的调优杠杆，确保比较的对称性。

Result: SparseBin在映射大小32x32到512x512范围内实现了每epoch 5.6-10.1倍的速度提升；与之前的CUDA实现相比，性能差距从约80倍扩大到385倍；cuSPARSE经过调优也加速了2-3倍。调优后的核函数达到L2带宽屋顶的77%（其他单元40-65%），剩余调优空间被限制在约1.3倍以内，是终端结果而非中途站。

Conclusion: 对称调优是一种有价值的比较方法论。SparseBin经过系统调优后已触及L2带宽屋顶，所有未测试的杠杆要么被该性能上限所限制，要么经测量无效。这项工作的意义在于：既证明了SparseBin相对于cuSPARSE的优势在公平调优下依然成立（甚至差距更大），也展示了一套完整的系统调优方法论。

Abstract: Comparisons between GPU implementations are usually asymmetric: one side is tuned by its author, the other is run as found. I report a programme that tuned both a novel SOM algorithm (SparseBin) and the baseline algorithm it was being compared to (cuSPARSE). The best-matching-unit search that dominates self-organizing map training was tuned through four levers - tile size, tile-membership clustering, neuron-axis chunking and vectorised loads - reaching 5.6-10.1x per epoch over the previously published configuration at map sizes from 32x32 to 512x512, and lifting the margin over the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases from ~80x to ~385x. cuSPARSE, the implementation SparseBin is compared against, received every lever with an analogue on its side, and became 2-3x faster in the process. The tuned kernel pressed the L2 bandwidth roof at 77% of peak with every other unit at 40-65%, bounding any further lever at ~1.3x - a terminal result rather than a waypoint, and every untested lever was either capped by that bound by construction or measured null.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Stationarity in Time Series: Discovering Causal Structures and Latent Regimes via Markov Blankets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05150)
*Lei Zan,Charles K. Assaad,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RCBNB-MB算法，一种用于时间序列的因果发现方法，它放宽了单一、时间一致因果结构的假设，通过识别潜在因果制度（时间点子集内的稳定因果结构）来处理非平稳时间序列中的制度变化。该算法迭代地分割时间序列为制度并在每个制度内发现因果图，利用马尔可夫毯而非直接父节点来增强对因果发现错误的鲁棒性，并提供理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列通常在离散时间点观测并表现出制度变化，而传统因果发现算法假设静态的因果结构，这在许多动态系统中具有局限性。作者旨在解决非平稳时间序列中因果结构随时间变化的挑战。

Method: RCBNB-MB采用迭代策略：首先将时间序列分割成多个制度（regimes），然后在每个制度内发现因果图。与依赖直接父节点不同，该方法利用马尔可夫毯（Markov blanket）来增强鲁棒性并保留预测信息，从而对因果发现中的错误更具抵抗力。

Result: 在已知真实因果关系的模拟数据集和真实世界IT监控数据上的大量实验表明，RCBNB-MB在准确检测制度变化及其关联因果图方面系统性优于基线方法。同时也提供了在合理假设下恢复制度转换和因果图的理论保障。

Conclusion: RCBNB-MB被定位为一种稳健且通用的非平稳时间序列分析框架，能够有效处理制度变化带来的因果结构动态性，适用于需要识别动态因果关系的真实场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces Regime-aware Constraint-Based and Noise-Based causal discovery with Markov Blankets (RCBNB-MB), a novel causal discovery algorithm for time series that relaxes the common assumption of a single, time-consistent causal structure. Time series are typically observed at discrete time points and often exhibit regime changes that challenge the assumption of a static causal structure, a limitation in many real-world dynamic systems. To address this challenge, RCBNB-MB identifies latent causal regimes, defined as subsets of time points within which a stable causal structure holds. The algorithm follows an iterative strategy that segments the time series into regimes and discovers the causal graph within each regime. By leveraging the Markov blanket rather than direct parents, RCBNB-MB gains robustness to errors in causal discovery and preserves predictive information. We provide theoretical guarantees for RCBNB-MB's ability to recover both regime transitions and causal graphs under reasonable assumptions. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on simulated datasets with known ground truth and real-world IT monitoring data, where taking into account regime shifts is critical. Empirical results show that RCBNB-MB systematically outperforms baseline approaches in accurately detecting regime changes and their associated causal graphs, positioning it as a robust and versatile framework for non-stationary time series analysis.

</details>


### [153] [Phase Transition Frequency as a Training Time Predictor of Test Accuracy in ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05194)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过75项实验（涵盖4个基准、3种ResNet架构），实证考察了ResNet微调过程中"类可分性跃迁次数"作为最终测试准确率预测因子的有效性。在标准i.i.d.分类基准上观察到强负相关（CIFAR-10: r=-0.84, CIFAR-100: r=-0.87），但在分布偏移下相关性减弱（TinyImageNet: r=-0.45, CIFAR-10-C: r=-0.19）。偏相关分析、六种替代训练曲线信号的比较以及提供的轻量检测程序共同限定了该观测的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种简洁、廉价的训练过程信号，能够在训练早期或过程中预测模型最终测试精度，从而无需等待完整训练即可评估训练质量或进行早停。现有文献中的强竞争信号（有效秩、Hessian锐度、Fisher信息、margin、神经坍缩等）计算成本高昂，作者希望验证一种基于离散类可分性跃迁次数的低成本探针。

Method: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和CIFAR-10-C四个基准上，使用ResNet-18/50/101三种架构、每配置5-10个种子共75次ResNet微调实验。计算训练过程中离散类可分性（类间可分性）发生"跃迁"的次数，并将其作为自变量与最终测试准确率进行相关分析。还进行了三种补充分析：以架构深度为线性协变量的偏相关分析；将跃迁次数与六种替代训练曲线信号进行比较；提供适用于标准训练循环的廉价检测程序。

Result: 标准i.i.d.基准上获得强负相关：CIFAR-10上r=-0.84（p<10^-8, n=30），CIFAR-100上r=-0.87（p<10^-5, n=15）。分布偏移下衰减：TinyImageNet r=-0.45，CIFAR-10-C r=-0.19。CIFAR-100上的偏相关（控制架构深度为线性协变量）为r_partial=-0.69（p=0.007），保留统计显著性；但n=15时更严格的分类别条件化结果不成立。与六种替代信号比较中，跃迁次数在CIFAR-100上最强、CIFAR-10上接近最强，但在两个压力基准上被其他信号超越。与有效秩、Hessian锐度等强竞争者的对比不在研究范围内。

Conclusion: 类可分性跃迁次数可作为分布内（in-distribution）训练质量的探针信号，尤其在标准i.i.d.分类场景下具有预测力。然而该关系在分布偏移下显著衰减，表明其适用边界主要限于分布内场景。该观测被定位于"候选探针族"中的一员，而非普适最优信号。作者呼吁将其与更强大的文献竞争信号（有效秩、Hessian锐度等）的比较留作未来工作，并提供了一种适合在标准训练循环中附带记录的低成本检测方法。

Abstract: The number of discrete class-separability jumps observed during ResNet finetuning is examined empirically as a predictor of final test accuracy. Across 75 experiments spanning four benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and CIFAR-10-C) and three architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ResNet-101), with five to ten seeds per configuration, a strong within-dataset negative correlation is obtained on standard i.i.d. classification benchmarks: \(r = -0.84\) on CIFAR-10 (\(p < 10^{-8}\), \(n = 30\)) and \(r = -0.87\) on CIFAR-100 (\(p < 10^{-5}\), \(n = 15\)). Under distributional stress, the relationship attenuates: TinyImageNet yields \(r = -0.45\), and the CIFAR-10-C corruption benchmark yields \(r = -0.19\). Two additional analyses discipline the empirical claim. A partial correlation controlling for architecture depth, treated as a linear covariate, shows that on CIFAR-100 the transition count retains statistically significant predictive power (\(r_{\mathrm{partial}} = -0.69\), \(p = 0.007\)); the corresponding result under the stricter categorical conditioning is not established at \(n = 15\). A comparison against six alternative training-curve signals shows that transition count achieved the strongest correlation among the evaluated signals on CIFAR-100 and one of the strongest on CIFAR-10, but is dominated by other signals on the two stressed benchmarks. The comparison is restricted to training-curve-level signals; comparisons against effective rank, Hessian sharpness, Fisher information, margin, and neural-collapse measures, which are the strongest competitors in the current literature, are not part of the present study and remain open. The observation is presented as an in-distribution training-quality probe among a family of candidate probes, and an inexpensive detection procedure suitable for logging alongside a standard training loop is provided.

</details>


### [154] [Dimension-Adaptive Batched Lipschitz Narrowing Without Knowing the Zooming Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05214)
*Yasong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该笔记改进了A-BLiN算法中的ACE序列，使其不再依赖于缩放维度d_z，提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，在不使用d_z和缩放常数C_z的情况下仍达到Õ_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})的遗憾界和O_d(log log T)的批次数，结合原有自适应网格下界，确认了d_z未知时最优批复杂度仍为Θ_d(log log T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始A-BLiN算法中的ACE（适当组合边长）序列依赖于缩放维度d_z，这限制了算法在d_z未知场景下的适用性，需要移除这一依赖以增强算法的实用性。

Method: 提出Count-Adaptive策略：通过前一轮消除后幸存的立方体数量来动态选择下一轮边长的尺度和边长，从而替代原本依赖d_z和C_z的ACE序列选择机制。

Result: Count-Adaptive BLiN算法实现了Õ_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})的遗憾界和O_d(log log T)的批次数，与使用d_z信息的原算法性能相当；结合原论文Theorem 10的自适应网格下界，证明了d_z未知时Θ_d(log log T)仍是最优批复杂度。

Conclusion: Count-Adaptive BLiN消除了对缩放维度d_z和缩放常数C_z的依赖，同时保持了最优的遗憾界和批复杂度，且log log T的批复杂度下界在d_z未知时依然成立。

Abstract: The Appropriately Combined Edge-length (ACE) sequence in A-BLiN depends on the zooming dimension $d_z$. This note removes that dependence. The next edge length is selected from the number of cubes that survive the preceding elimination. The resulting Count-Adaptive BLiN algorithm does not use $d_z$ or the zooming constant $C_z$, yet it attains $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$ regret with $\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$ batches. Together with the adaptive-grid lower bound in Theorem 10 of the original paper, the optimal batch complexity remains $Θ_d(\log\log T)$ when $d_z$ is unknown.

</details>


### [155] [FedDRAW: Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting for Heterogeneous Multi-Institutional Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05223)
*Maryam Moradpour,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDRAW（联邦双声誉退火加权），一种服务器端联邦学习聚合方法，通过结合数据规模先验与客户端-全局参数余弦相似度，在双重耦合退火调度下动态调整各医院的聚合权重，解决FedAvg中按样本量加权导致小但信息量高的医院影响力被长期低估的问题。在12个胸片数据模拟场景下，FedDRAW在AUC和几何均值两项指标上均取得所有方法中最高平均排名，并经Friedman检验与Nemenyi事后分析确认统计显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中FedAvg按本地样本数量加权聚合，导致样本少但信息价值高的医院机构被永久赋予较小影响力，而样本量大的客户端即使信息量较低也可能主导全局模型，造成诊断模型偏差。作者致力于在保护数据隐私的前提下，设计一种不依赖样本数的公平聚合权重方法。

Method: FedDRAW在服务器端聚合时结合两个信号：数据规模先验和客户端参数与全局参数之间的余弦相似度。方法采用内外双退火调度：内层调度将客户端声誉从规模先验逐步转向相似度信号；外层延后的退火调度作用于softmax逆温度参数，在训练早期和中轮保持权重选择性强，收敛阶段放松至均匀分布。整体流程在每个通信轮中由服务器计算并分配各客户端的聚合权重。

Result: 在CheXpert与ChestMNIST两个数据集上的12种模拟客户端划分场景中，FedDRAW与七种联邦基线方法在相同本地训练设置下对比。FedDRAW在AUC和灵敏度与特异度几何均值两项指标上平均排名均为所有八种方法中最高，Friedman检验结合Nemenyi事后分析证实方法间差异具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 通过调度数据规模与参数相似度两种信号而非仅依据样本数固定权重，FedDRAW能够在联邦学习中赋予小样本但信息量高的医院机构适当影响力，从而可能产生偏差更小的诊断模型。该方案为跨医院协作训练中规避数据主导偏差提供了一种有效的服务器端可行策略。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are promising for medical diagnosis, but they require large numbers of unbiased data, which in medicine are distributed across hospitals and cannot be centralized to protect patient privacy. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this, since hospitals train one shared diagnostic model while patient data remain local. Training proceeds in communication rounds, in which each hospital trains the shared model locally and returns it to the server for merging by weighted average. This aggregation weight determines whose institutional knowledge shapes the result.
